### [1] [Some New Sharp Boundedness Result Concerning Linear functionals on Banach spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06872)
*L. -E. Persson,V. Tsagareishvili,G. Tutberidze,G. Cagareishvili*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究Lipschitz类\(Lip_1\)上函数与一般正交规范系所构成的线性泛函序列的有界性问题，给出新结果并证明其最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于线性泛函序列有界性的结果多针对特定函数类或特定正交系，本文旨在将讨论推广到Lipschitz类\(Lip_1\)与一般正交规范系的组合情形，并明确所获界限的精确程度。

Method: 论文以陈述并证明的方式，建立若干关于\(Lip_1\)类函数和一般正交规范系下线性泛函序列的有界性命题，并通过构造或逼近手段论证这些结果在某种意义下是最优的。

Result: 得到一批新的有界性结论，同时证明这些结论在相应意义下是最佳可能的，即不能进一步改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的有界性结果完善了Lipschitz类与一般正交规范系在线性泛函序列框架下的理论，且其最优性保证了界限的不可改进性。

Abstract: In this paper we state, prove, and discuss some new boundedness results concerning sequences of linear functionals of functions from the Lipschitz class \(Lip_1\) and general orthonormal systems. It is also proved that these results are, in a sense, the best possible.

</details>


### [2] [Equilibria of Inverse-Square Repulsion on the Line Are Arithmetic Progressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06944)
*Alper Ferudun*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 证明了Benjamini猜想：在平方反比斥力下处于平衡状态的实直线上的点配置必为等差数列，且无需任何间隙假设，该结论推广到任意指数1<s≤2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Benjamini提出猜想：实直线上在平方反比斥力下处于平衡的点配置是否必为等差数列。Georgakopoulos与Kolountzakis在存在最大或最小间隙的假设下证明了该结论，但将一般（非周期）情形列为公开问题。本文旨在完全解决这一开放问题。

Method: 将相邻两点的平衡方程相减，得出间隙序列g_n构成Z上某个显式可逆长跳随机游走的正调和函数；利用平衡条件给出相邻间隙比值上界（s=2时为1.5386…）；借助该界与能量估计证明该游走经g的Doob变换后是常返的；由于1/g是变换后游走的正调和函数，故其为常数。

Result: 证明了Benjamini猜想成立：对任意1<s≤2，若局部有限集合X⊂R至少含两点，且对每个x处总力有限、合力为零，则X必为等差数列，无需对间隙作任何先验假设。s=2时相邻间隙比值上界为1.5386…。

Conclusion: 该平衡点配置必为等差数列的结论在1<s≤2范围内完全成立，彻底解决了Georgakopoulos与Kolountzakis所提的公开问题，方法上通过随机游走的调和函数与Doob变换建立了力平衡与间隙结构之间的联系。

Abstract: Benjamini asked whether every configuration of points on the real line that is in equilibrium under the inverse-square repulsive force must be an arithmetic progression. Georgakopoulos and Kolountzakis proved this when some gap between consecutive points has maximal or minimal length, and described the general (aperiodic) case as open. We show that the answer is yes. More generally, let $1<s\le2$, and let $X\subset\mathbb{R}$ be a locally finite set with at least two points such that, for every $x\in X$, the total force $\sum_{y\in X\setminus\{x\}}|y-x|^{-s}$ is finite and the net force $\sum_{y\in X\setminus\{x\}}\operatorname{sgn}(y-x)\,|y-x|^{-s}$ is zero. Then $X$ is an arithmetic progression. No a priori assumption on the gaps is needed. Subtracting the equilibrium equations of two consecutive points shows that the gaps $g_n$ form a positive harmonic function for an explicit reversible random walk on $\mathbb{Z}$ with long-range jumps. Equilibrium also bounds the ratio of consecutive gaps, by $1.5386\ldots$ when $s=2$. With this bound, an energy estimate shows that the Doob transform of the walk by $g$ is recurrent. Since $1/g$ is a positive harmonic function of the transformed walk, it is constant.

</details>


### [3] [Full-Range $Γ$-Convergence of Weak-Type Nonlocal Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07001)
*Xiaosheng Lin,Dachun Yang,Sibei Yang,Wen Yuan,Yangyang Zhang*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文研究弱型非局部泛函 F_{λ,p,γ} 在 λ→∞（γ>0）或 λ→0⁺（γ≤−1）时的 Γ-收敛，证明其极限为经典的 p-能量（p>1）或全变差（p=1），并给出 Brezis 等人所提问题的完整解答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brezis 等人在 Anal. PDE 2024 第 7C 节提出一个公开问题：弱型非局部泛函在参数极限下是否收敛到经典局部能量泛函。本文旨在彻底解决这一问题，刻画非局部到局部的变分极限。

Method: 在 L^p(Ω)（及 L^1_loc(R^N)）中建立 Γ-收敛框架，证明对任意 γ∈R\{0} 与 λ>0 定义的弱型非局部泛函 F_{λ,p,γ}，在 γ∈(0,∞) 且 λ→∞ 或 γ∈(−∞,−1] 且 λ→0⁺ 时收敛到极限泛函 Ψ_{p,γ}^Ω：p∈(1,∞) 时为 C^{cell}_{N,p,γ}∫_Ω|∇u|^p，u∈W^{1,p}；p=1 时为 C^{cell}_{N,1,γ}|Du|(Ω)，u∈BV；否则为 ∞。常数 C^{cell}_{N,p,γ} 与 Ω 无关。

Result: 证明了上述 Γ-收敛结论，并推广到全空间 Ω=R^N 情形，即 F_{λ,p,γ}(·;R^N) 在 L^1_loc(R^N) 中 Γ-收敛到类似泛函，从而完整回答了 Brezis 等人的问题。

Conclusion: 弱型非局部泛函在相应参数极限下非局部性消失，Γ-极限恰为经典的 Sobolev p-能量或 BV 全变差；所得极限常数具有普适性，为非局部-局部变分极限提供了统一刻画并解决了已有公开问题。

Abstract: Let $N\ge1$, $p\in[1,\infty)$, and $Ω\subset\mathbb R^N$ be a bounded open interval when $N=1$ or a bounded Lipschitz domain when $N\ge2$. For any given $γ\in\mathbb R\setminus\{0\}$ and $λ\in(0,\infty)$, consider the weak-type nonlocal functional \begin{align*} F_{λ,p,γ}(u;Ω):=λ^p \underset{|u(x)-u(y)|>λ|x-y|^{1+\fracγ{p}}} {\int_Ω\int_Ω}|x-y|^{γ-N}\,dy\,dx. \end{align*} In this article, we prove that, for $γ\in(0,\infty)$ as $λ\to\infty$ or for $γ\in(-\infty,-1]$ as $λ\to0^+$, the family $F_{λ,p,γ}(\cdot;Ω)$ converges, in the sense of $Γ$-convergence in $L^p(Ω)$, to the functional \begin{align*} Ψ_{p,γ}^Ω(u) :=\begin{cases} C_{N,p,γ}^{\rm cell}\displaystyle\int_Ω|\nabla u|^p\,dx, &p\in(1,\infty)\ \hbox{and}\ u\in W^{1,p}(Ω),\\[2mm] C_{N,1,γ}^{\rm cell}|Du|(Ω), &p=1\ \hbox{and}\ u\in BV(Ω),\\[1mm] \infty,&\hbox{otherwise}. \end{cases} \end{align*} where the positive constants $C_{N,p,γ}^{\mathrm{cell}}$ are independent of $Ω$. As applications, we further show that, for $γ\in(0,\infty)$ as $λ\to\infty$ or for $γ\in(-\infty,-1]$ as $λ\to0^+$, the weak-type nonlocal functional $F_{λ,p,γ}(\cdot;\mathbb R^N)$ converges, in the sense of $Γ$-convergence in $L^1_{\rm loc}(\mathbb R^N)$, to a similar functional; this gives an complete answer to the problem posed by Brezis et al. in [Section~7C, Anal. PDE 2024].

</details>


### [4] [$\mathcal C^m$ solutions of semialgebraic equations on curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07210)
*Edward Bierstone,Jean-Baptiste Campesato*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 在半代数（更一般地，o-minimal 可定义）情形下，若方程 $A(x)\cdot F(x)=B(x)$ 存在一个由 $\mathcal C^m$ 函数 $f$ 的 $m$ 阶 Taylor 多项式场构成的解，则当 $\dim X=1$ 时存在 $\mathcal C^m$ 半代数解 $f$。方法基于 Fefferman–Luli 关于 $\mathbb R^2$ 的工作，并说明其次级目标：在 $\dim X=1$ 及 $\mathbb R^2$ 半代数 Whitney 扩张问题中，其方法可大幅简化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性方程 $A(x)\cdot F(x)=B(x)$ 在集合 $X$ 上以 $m$-jet 场为未知量的可解性，并追问：若已知解可由某个 $\mathcal C^m$ 函数 $f$ 的 Taylor 多项式场实现，能否进一步要求 $f$ 具有半代数（或 o-minimal 可定义）结构。这是 Whitney 扩张型问题在半代数/可定义范畴下的推广，具有几何与分析交叉意义。

Method: 将问题置于 o-minimal 扩张的 $\mathbb R$ 上的可定义函数框架；在 $X\subset\mathbb R^n$ 上，$A,B$ 为半代数矩阵/向量值函数，$F$ 为向量值 $m$-jet 场。借鉴 Fefferman–Luli 处理 $X\subset\mathbb R^2$ 的方法，将其适配并简化到 $\dim X=1$ 的情形，同时用于 $\mathbb R^2$ 上的半代数 Whitney 扩张问题。

Result: 在 $\dim X=1$ 时给出肯定答案：存在 $\mathcal C^m$ 半代数解 $f$，使 $F$ 为其 $m$ 阶 Taylor 多项式场并满足原方程。结论对 o-minimal 扩张中可定义的函数同样成立，且方法在 $\dim X=1$ 及 $\mathbb R^2$ 半代数 Whitney 扩张问题中更为简洁。

Conclusion: 该工作推广并简化了 Fefferman–Luli 的方法，证明了低维（$\dim X=1$）情形下 Taylor 多项式场解可由 $\mathcal C^m$ 半代数/可定义函数实现，并为 $\mathbb R^2$ 半代数 Whitney 扩张问题提供了更简单的处理路径。

Abstract: Consider a system of equations $A(x)\cdot F(x) = B(x)$ on a subset $X$ of $\mathbb R^n$, where $A(x)$ and $B(x)$ are matrix- and vector-valued semialgebraic functions on $X$, and the unknown $F(x)$ is a field of vector-valued $m$-jets. We assume there is a solution which is the field of Taylor polynomials of order $m$ on $X$ of a $\mathcal C^m$ vector-valued function $f$ on $\mathbb R^n$, and ask whether we can find a $\mathcal C^m$ semialgebraic solution $f$. Our main result is a positive answer in the case $\dim X = 1$. The methods are based on an article of Fefferman and Luli for $X \subset \mathbb R^2$, and a secondary goal is to show that their approach applies in a much simpler way in the case $\dim X =1$, or in the case of the semialgebraic Whitney extension problem in $\mathbb R^2$. Our results hold more generally for functions definable in an o-minimal expansion of $\mathbb R$.

</details>


### [5] [A Proof of McLeod's 1959 Conjecture on Complete Homogeneous Symmetric Ratios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07302)
*Suvrit Sra*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了McLeod (1959) 关于完全齐次对称多项式比值的猜想：对任意 $\ell\ge0, r\ge1$，$(h_{\ell+r}/h_\ell)^{1/r}$ 关于相关参数是次可加的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: McLeod 在1959年提出一个关于对称函数的不等式猜想，长期未被解决。该问题涉及完全齐次对称多项式比值的性质，具有独立的理论意义。

Method: 证明分两步：首先证明比值 $h_k/h_{k-1}$ 是凸的，其证明出人意料地借助了排队论（queuing theory）；其次，由于 $r$ 个相邻比值的几何平均即为McLeod猜想函数，而凸函数的几何平均未必凸，作者采用张量化技巧（tensorization trick）将相邻比值紧密耦合，从而使几何平均成为凸函数。

Result: 成功证明了McLeod猜想，即 $(h_{\ell+r}/h_\ell)^{1/r}$ 对所有 $\ell\ge0$ 和 $r\ge1$ 是次可加的。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅解决了悬而未决的McLeod猜想，还展示了排队论与对称函数理论之间意外而深刻的联系，以及张量化技巧在证明凸性中的有效性。

Abstract: We prove McLeod's (1959) conjecture that $(h_{\ell+r}/h_\ell)^{1/r}$ is subadditive for all $\ell\ge0$ and $r\ge1$, where $h_k$ is the complete homogeneous symmetric polynomial of degree $k$ (J. B. McLeod, "On four inequalities in symmetric functions", Proc. Edinburgh Math. Soc., 1959). We first prove that the ratio $h_k/h_{k-1}$ is convex, and our proof takes an unexpected detour through queuing theory. The geometric mean of $r$ consecutive such ratios equals McLeod's conjectured function, but geometric means of convex functions need not be convex. Our second and decisive step is a tensorization trick that couples neighboring ratios tightly enough to make their geometric mean convex.

</details>


### [6] [Positive Bidiagonal Factorizations and Mixed-Type Chebyshev Multiple Orthogonal Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08568)
*Manuel Mañas*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文刻画带状全正矩阵何时存在正双对角分解：有限维总存在；对任意有限带宽的半无限矩阵，存在性等价于Metelmann因子确定的有限族正级数收敛，其倒数为Jacobi连分数极限值。并证明有界带状全正矩阵可用限于固定初始块的扰动在算子范数下逼近具正双对角分解的矩阵；对带状Toeplitz矩阵，用符号多项式负根给出具界因子的整体分解，根的序分拆导出混合型多重正交多项式，并计算其正测度矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维带状全正矩阵的正双对角分解总存在，但半无限情形尚无完整判据；作者希望刻画存在性条件，并建立带状Toeplitz结构与多重正交多项式、随机游走之间的联系。

Method: 利用Metelmann因子构造正级数判据，借助标量Jacobi连分数的收敛性；对有界带状全正矩阵采用限于固定初始块的扰动逼近；对带状Toeplitz矩阵，通过符号多项式负根的序分拆构造混合型多重正交多项式，并应用已有的谱Favard定理计算其正测度矩阵。

Result: 半无限有限带宽矩阵存在正双对角分解当且仅当有限族正级数收敛，其倒数为Jacobi连分数极限；有界带状全正矩阵可被具正双对角分解的矩阵在算子范数下逼近；带状Toeplitz矩阵的负根给出具界因子的整体分解，并得到显式混合型多重正交多项式，其正测度矩阵支撑为单一区间，测度绝对连续且具秩一代数密度，同时给出相关killed随机游走的转移律与首返律。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了带状全正矩阵的正双对角分解判据、Toeplitz结构与多重正交多项式理论，并将结果应用于killed随机游走的概率律，揭示了分解存在性与级数收敛性、连分数极限之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We characterize when a banded totally positive matrix admits a positive bidiagonal factorization. In finite dimension such a factorization always exists. For semi-infinite matrices of arbitrary finite bandwidth, existence is equivalent to convergence of a finite family of positive series determined by the Metelmann factors. Their reciprocals are the limiting values of scalar Jacobi continued fractions. We also prove that every bounded banded totally positive matrix can be approximated in operator norm by matrices admitting positive bidiagonal factorizations, through perturbations confined to a fixed initial block. For banded Toeplitz matrices, the negative roots of the polynomial symbol give a global factorization with bounded factors. Ordered partitions of these roots determine explicit mixed-type multiple orthogonal polynomials. Applying the existing spectral Favard theorem, we compute their matrix of positive measures: its support is one interval, and the measure is purely absolutely continuous with an algebraic density of rank one. These formulas also give transition and first-return laws for the associated killed random walks.

</details>

*Jongchon Kim,Liang Wang*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了与截断双曲抛物面相关的Fourier延拓算子的Mizohata–Takeuchi型估计，并给出了加权L²估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双曲抛物面上Fourier延拓算子的Mizohata–Takeuchi型估计，推广和精细化相关调和分析结果。

Method: 利用双线性精细decoupling估计，建立Fourier变换支集在双曲抛物面小邻域内函数的加权L²估计，从而推导出所需结果。

Result: 主要结果用权重在由双曲抛物面ℓ² decoupling产生的多尺度平行六面体族上的L²质量，控制了延拓算子的加权L²范数。

Conclusion: 该工作为双曲抛物面上的延拓算子提供了Mizohata–Takeuchi型加权估计，并展示了双线性精细decoupling方法的应用。

Abstract: We prove Mizohata--Takeuchi-type estimates for the Fourier extension operator associated with the truncated hyperbolic paraboloid in $\R^3$. Our main result controls the weighted $L^2$ norm of the extension operator by the $L^2$ mass of the weight over a multiscale family of parallelepipeds arising from $\ell^2$ decoupling for the hyperbolic paraboloid. We deduce this result from a weighted $L^2$ estimate for functions whose Fourier transforms are supported in a small neighborhood of the hyperbolic paraboloid, which we establish using a bilinear refined decoupling estimate.

</details>


### [8] [Sharp two-sided bounds for the product of modified Bessel functions and for its logarithmic derivative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07933)
*Javier Segura,Soichiro Suzuki*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了修正贝塞尔函数乘积 $I_\nu(x)K_\nu(x)$ 及其对数导数和的尖锐双边不等式，四个常数均为最优，并给出了相关单调性与比值界的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修正贝塞尔函数 $I_\nu$ 与 $K_\nu$ 的乘积 $I_\nu(x)K_\nu(x)$ 及其对数导数和在特殊函数论与渐近分析中具有重要地位，已有不等式往往常数非最优或适用范围有限，因此需要建立精确且常数最优的双边估计。

Method: 作者构造相应的辅助函数，证明其满足二阶微分不等式，并借助强极值原理（strong maximum principle）导出所要求的双边估计。四个证明采用统一的初等结构，并论证四个常数的不可改进性。

Result: 证明了：对 $x>0$，有 $\frac{1}{2\sqrt{x^2+\nu^2+1/5}}<I_\nu(x)K_\nu(x)<\frac{1}{2\sqrt{x^2+\nu^2-1/4}}$，其中下界对 $\nu>-1$ 成立、上界对 $\nu\ge1/2$ 成立；以及对数导数和满足 $-\frac{x}{x^2+\nu^2-1}<\frac{I_\nu'(x)}{I_\nu(x)}+\frac{K_\nu'(x)}{K_\nu(x)}<-\frac{x}{x^2+\nu^2+7/20}$，其中上界对 $\nu>-1$ 成立、下界对 $\nu\ge1$ 成立。四个常数均为最优。

Conclusion: 本文给出修正贝塞尔函数乘积及其对数导数和的常数最优双边不等式，并获得其乘积的尖锐单调性质以及 $I_{\nu-1}(x)/I_\nu(x)$ 与 $K_{\nu+1}(x)/K_\nu(x)$ 比值的改进界。

Abstract: We prove that, for all $x > 0$, the product of modified Bessel functions satisfies \[ \frac{1}{2\sqrt{x^2+ν^2+1/5}}<I_ν(x)K_ν(x)<\frac{1}{2\sqrt{x^2+ν^2-1/4}} , \] where the lower bound holds for $ν>-1$ and the upper bound for $ν\ge 1/2$, and that the sum of the logarithmic derivatives satisfies \[ -\frac{x}{x^2+ν^2-1}<\frac{I_ν'(x)}{I_ν(x)}+\frac{K_ν'(x)}{K_ν(x)}<-\frac{x}{x^2+ν^2+7/20}, \] where the upper bound holds for $ν>-1$ and the lower bound for $ν\ge 1$. All four constants are best possible. The four proofs share the same elementary structure: in each case the relevant auxiliary function satisfies a second-order differential inequality, and the inequality follows from the strong maximum principle. Among other applications, we obtain sharp monotonicity properties of the product and improved bounds for the ratios $I_{ν-1}(x)/I_ν(x)$ and $K_{ν+1}(x)/K_ν(x)$.

</details>


### [9] [Counterexamples to Hardy--Littlewood maximal inequalities on solvable groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08121)
*Koji Fujiwara*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 在三种可解群（灯夫群、Baumslag--Solitar群BS(1,n)、三维Sol群）上，中心Hardy--Littlewood极大算子对任何1≤p<∞都不是弱型(p,p)；并首次给出有限生成群配字度量与计数测度下中心极大算子非弱型(1,1)的例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察经典Hardy--Littlewood极大算子在非交换、可解群几何（字度量/计数测度、Riemannian体积）背景下是否仍满足弱型(p,p)有界性，填补该方向此前缺乏反例的空白。

Abstract: We develop a sparse domination framework adapted to nested families in general measure spaces, possibly including point masses. We show that the Carleson condition is equivalent to a slight generalization of the usual sparse condition, and use this characterization to obtain one- and two-weight estimates for the associated averaging operators, together with converse testing conditions. We apply our general results to integral operators on locally finite trees. Trees come naturally equipped with two nested geometries: sectors and pruned sectors. We prove pointwise sparse domination of natural versions of the harmonic Bergman projector by the corresponding averaging operators, depending on whether the measure on the tree is doubling or not, and show that the distinction is necessary in general. For the positive Bergman projector, we obtain pointwise comparability. As a consequence, we obtain new weighted inequalities and boundedness results for the Bergman projector and a class of Toeplitz-like multipliers.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lagarias与Maddock曾对经典Takagi函数 $T_2$ 引入局部水平集概念，并证明期望个数为 $3/2$。本文旨在将这一结果推广到任意整数 $r\geq 2$ 的Takagi-van der Waerden函数。

Method: 对每个整数 $r\geq2$ 定义相应的局部水平集，利用函数 $T_r(x)=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\varphi(r^n x)}{r^n}$ 的自相似/位展开结构，分析水平集在值域上的分布，进而计算局部水平集个数的期望。

Result: 证明了当 $y$ 在 $T_r$ 值域上均匀分布时，高度 $y$ 处水平集内局部水平集数的期望为 $(r+1)/r$（$r$ 为偶数）与 $(r+1)/(r-1)$（$r$ 为奇数）。当 $r=2$ 时退化为已知的 $3/2$。

Conclusion: 该工作将局部水平集的期望计数结果从经典情形 $r=2$ 推广至所有整数 $r\geq2$，并揭示了期望值随 $r$ 奇偶性呈现不同表达式的规律。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the Takagi-van der Waerden function, $$ T_r(x) = \sum_{n=0}^{\infty} \frac{φ(r^n x)}{r^n} ,\quad x\in [0,1], \quad r \geq 2, $$ where $φ(x)={\rm dist}(x,\mathbb{Z})$ represents the distance from $x$ to the nearest integer.
  Lagarias and Maddock[13] introduced the notion of local level sets for the classical Takagi function $T_2$. They proved that if $y$ is uniformly distributed over the range of $T_2$, then the expected number of local level sets contained in the level set at height $y$ equals $3/2$. We define the corresponding local level sets for every integer $r\geq2$ and prove that, if $y$ is uniformly distributed over the range of $T_r$, then the expected number of local level sets contained in the level set at height $y$ equals $(r+1)/r$ if $r$ is even, and $(r+1)/(r-1)$ if $r$ is odd.

</details>

Abstract: We prove a sharp $L^\infty$ bound for the density of an infinite convolution in terms of $L^2$ norms of convolution powers of the summand densities. The bound may be finite even when every summand density is unbounded and the products arising from Young's inequality diverge.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce and study a new class of mappings termed generalized quadrilateral Ciric-Reich-Rus type mappings, which serve as a four-point analogue of Ciric- Reich-Rus type mappings. We establish the existence and non-uniqueness of fixed points, with at most three fixed points possible for these mappings in complete metric spaces under the condition that the mapping has no periodic points of prime period 2 and 3. We determine that while these mappings are often discontinuous within their domain of definition, they must be continuous at their fixed points. By applying additional constraints, notably asymptotic regularity and continuity, we broaden the applicability of fixed-point theorems to include a broader class of mappings. At the end, we attain fixed point theorems for generalized quadrilateral Ciric- Reich-Rus type mappings in metric spaces that are not necessarily complete. Several illustrative examples are provided to validate the theoretical results and demonstrate the independence of our new class from existing notions.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Let $\mathbb{P}_n$ be the cone of $n\times n$ complex positive definite matrices. For $0<θ<1$ and $r\ge0$, let $M_r^{(θ)}$ denote the weighted matrix power mean, with the logarithmic endpoint at $r=0$, defined by $$
  M_r^{(θ)}(A,B)
  =
  \begin{cases}
  \big((1-θ)A^r+θB^r\big)^{1/r}, & r>0,\\[2mm]
  \exp\big((1-θ)\log A+θ\log B\big), & r=0.
  \end{cases}
  $$
  For $p>q\ge0$, we introduce
  $$
  Φ_{p,q}^{(θ)}(A,B)
  =
  \operatorname{Tr}\big[M_p^{(θ)}(A,B)-M_q^{(θ)}(A,B)\big].
  $$

  sense of Bhatia-Gaubert-Jain. Moreover, for

  0\le q\le1\le p\le2,\qquad p>q,

Abstract: We construct a Banach lattice $d$-algebra with a strong order unit whose product has no positive associative bilinear extension to its Dedekind completion. This gives a negative answer to a question of Wickstead and to the $d$-algebra case of a question of Huijsmans. It also disproves an extension theorem of Chil. The example shows that norm completeness and a strong order unit do not ensure that multiplication extends to the Dedekind completion.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究非局部凸的 p-Banach 空间中等距嵌入的线性左逆问题，证明 Figiel 定理在 0<p<1 时完全失效，并给出 Lipschitz-free p-空间中的定量估计作为关键工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Figiel 定理表明实 Banach 空间之间的保基点等距嵌入在其像的闭线性张成上存在线性收缩左逆。作者欲探究当失去局部凸性（即转向 p-Banach 空间，0<p<1）时，这一现象是否仍然成立。

Abstract: A classical theorem of Figiel asserts that every isometric embedding $\varphi\colon X\to Y$ between real Banach spaces, with $\varphi(0)=0$, admits a linear contractive left inverse on the closed linear span of $\varphi(X)$. We show that this phenomenon breaks down completely once local convexity is lost. More precisely, for every $0<p<1$ and every nonzero real Banach space $X$, we construct a $p$-Banach space $Y$ and an isometric embedding $\varphi\colon X\to Y$ such that $\varphi(0)=0$ and the closed linear span of $\varphi(X)$ is the entire space $Y$, but there is no linear map, bounded or not, on the linear span of $\varphi(X)$ which is a left inverse of $\varphi$. The construction is carried out using the $p$-Banach counterpart of Lipschitz-free spaces. In fact, $Y$ may be chosen isomorphic to the Lipschitz-free $p$-space over $X$. The main ingredient is a quantitative estimate controlling the distance to elementary molecules in Lipschitz-free $p$-spaces, a phenomenon specific to the range $0<p<1$. As a further consequence, we show that a real Banach space has the isometric $p$-lifting property if and only if its dimension is at most one.

</details>

Abstract: A Banach space $X$ is $L$-embedded in its bidual if there is a projection $P\colon X^{**}\to X$ such that $\|x\|=\|Px\|+\|x-Px\|$ for all $x\in X^{**}$. Result: 对径向初值 $f$、$2n/(n-1)\le p<\infty$、$\gamma<1/p$ 及 $T\ge0$，存在与 $f$ 和 $T$ 无关的常数 $C_{p,\gamma}$ 使得 $\left(\int_T^{T+1}\|u(t,\cdot)\|_p^p\,dt\right)^{1/p}\le C_{p,\gamma} e^{(n-1)(\frac12-\frac1p)T}\|(-\Delta_{\mathbb H^n})^{\frac{n-1}2(\frac12-\frac1p)-\frac\gamma2}f\|_p$；同时证明指数因子 $e^{(n-1)(1/2-1/p)T}$ 不可改进（最优）。

Abstract: Let $\mathbb H^n$ be the $n$-dimensional hyperbolic space, and $Δ_{\mathbb H^n}$ be the Laplace--Beltrami operator on $\mathbb H^n$. Let $u=u(t,\cdot)$ be the solution to the wave equation $\partial^2_{t}u-\big(Δ_{\mathbb H^n}+\frac{1}{4}(n-1)^2\operatorname{Id}\big)u=0$ with initial conditions $u(0,x)=f(x)$ and $\partial_tu(0,x)=0$. In this article we show that for a radial function $f$, $2n/(n-1)\leq p<\infty$, $γ<1/p$ and $T\geq 0$, there exists a constant $C_p$ independent of $f$ and $T$ such that $$ \left(\int_T^{T+1}\|u(t,\cdot)\|^p_p\mathrm d t\right)^{\frac1p}\leq C_{p,γ}\,\mathrm e^{(n-1)\left(\frac12-\frac1p\right)T}\left\|(-Δ_{\mathbb H^n})^{\frac{n-1}2\left(\frac12-\frac1p\right)-\frac\gamma2}f\right\|_p. $$ We also show that the exponential part $\mathrm e^{(n-1)(1/2-1/p)T}$ of the bound is optimal.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: This paper constructs explicit closed-form Green's functions for the Helmholtz equation on annular and spherical-shell domains with two independent Robin impedances on the inner and outer boundaries. Graf's and the hyperspherical addition theorems reduce each angular mode to an explicit $2\times2$ linear system, and the resonance spectrum is governed by a characteristic determinant bilinear in the two impedances. This bilinearity has a direct inverse-problem consequence: two resonant frequencies of one non-radial angular mode generate at most two candidate impedance pairs via an explicit quadratic equation, a third resonance selects the physical pair, and an exact reflection-symmetry obstruction identifies where the radial spherical mode cannot recover the impedances. Spectrally, we prove the branches positive, simple and strictly increasing in both impedances; derive first-order asymptotics at the four corners of the impedance plane, with coefficients given by boundary masses and normal derivatives of the limiting eigenfunctions, and an explicit mixed second-order coefficient at the Dirichlet--Dirichlet corner, which for the radial mode evaluates in closed form to $2π/(R_2-R_1)^3$; establish a low-frequency resonance-free band, with a second-order threshold expansion explicit in dimension three and a rational approximation accurate over the whole impedance range; and prove the universal high-frequency spacing law with a shell-curvature correction. Jacobian-based sensitivity and conditioning criteria are included. Abstract: It is known that on complete Riemannian manifolds with nonnegative Ricci curvature, the space of harmonic 1-forms of polynomial growth is finite-dimensional. In comparison, our result treats the critical quadratic decay regime, at the expense of requiring the corresponding coefficient to be sufficiently small. As a byproduct, we obtain an analogous finite-dimensionality result for polynomial-growth solutions to the Schrödinger heat equation, which generalizes the finite-dimensionality results in \cite{lin2019ancient},\cite{colding2021optimal} and \cite{lin2026space} to some extent.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 核心论点是：要提供语义忠实的翻译，必须先消解数学NL文本中的歧义，而该歧义消解问题在Solvability Complexity Index（SCI）层级／算术层级中处于任意高位，即SCI = ∞。由此，语义忠实的AI自动形式化在计算上比包括停机问题（SCI = 1）在内的任何计算问题都更难。为展示该结论的实际影响，作者给出若干AI把NL陈述与证明误译为Lean的实例，考察NL证明与其Lean"验证"之间的不匹配，并以OpenAI的Navier-Stokes证明作为具体案例进行分析。

Conclusion: 由于消解数学NL歧义以实现语义忠实翻译的问题在SCI／算术层级中不可计算地高（SCI = ∞），AI自动形式化在一般情况下无法保证语义忠实。因此，形式语言中的机械验证成功并不能为原始NL论证提供可信度；实际出现的误译案例（含OpenAI的Navier-Stokes证明）表明NL证明与其Lean验证可能并不对应。

Abstract: Autoformalisation is increasingly used to verify mathematical texts, including those generated by AI, as in OpenAI's announced proof of blow-up of solutions to the Navier-Stokes equations. In this process, an AI system translates the text from a natural language (NL) into a formal language such as Lean. Once this translation is done, the argument expressed in the formal language can easily be mechanically verified. The purpose of this article is to demonstrate why this process may offer no confidence in the original NL argument, owing to the various difficulties in performing the translation semantically faithfully. In particular, we highlight that the problem of resolving ambiguities in mathematical NL text, which is necessary in order to provide semantically faithful translation, is arbitrarily high up in the Solvability Complexity Index (SCI) hierarchy/arithmetical hierarchy (the SCI $= \infty$). Hence, informally, providing semantically faithful AI autoformalisation is harder than any computational problem including the Halting problem (which has SCI $= 1$). To demonstrate the effect of this result we provide several examples of AI mistranslations of NL statements and proofs into Lean in practice, resulting in mismatches between NL proofs and their Lean `verifications'. These include OpenAI's announced Navier-Stokes proof. In particular, we show that the formalised Lean proof does not correspond to the NL proof of blow-up of solutions to the Navier-Stokes equations.

</details>

Abstract: We study linear Maxwell's equations on a cuboid that is split into two parts by a planar interface, across which the tangential magnetic field jumps proportionally to the tangential electric field. Conditions of this type arise in the modeling of atomically thin metamaterials, where the sheet is replaced by an effective surface current. Working in a state space that admits a surface charge on the interface, we show that the problem is governed by a contraction semigroup. To this end, we provide density results, Weber-type inequalities, and trace estimates for the underlying function spaces. We further prove a hidden regularity result, stating that the fields in the domain of the Maxwell operator are piecewise Sobolev regular of first order. Since the damping acts on the interface only, the energy does not decay for all initial fields. The main results describe the degeneration produced by the Robin condition: the height, the instantaneous velocity, and the spatial gradient diverge, while the normalized slope $u_x/u$ converges to one away from the symmetry axis.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We consider the following class of singular elliptic problems \[ \begin{cases} -Δu =
-\dfrac{u}{|u|^{β+1}}χ_{\{|u|>0\}} +λ|u|^{p-1}u, & \text{in }Ω,\\[1mm] u=0, & \text{on }\partialΩ, \end{cases} \] where $Ω\subset\mathbb R^N$ is a bounded smooth domain, $N\geq3$, $0<β,p<1$, and $λ>0$. The energy functional is not of class $C^1$ in $H_0^1(Ω)$, and the standard critical point theory does not apply. We prove that a nontrivial nonnegative weak solution exists if and only if $λ$ exceeds a critical value $\barλ$, which we identify and locate strictly between two Rayleigh-type parameters $0<λ_*<\barλ<λ^*<+\infty$. This gives a sharp existence threshold. We also prove that for every $λ$ in a neighborhood on the left of $λ^*$, there exist at least two distinct nontrivial nonnegative weak solutions. The first is a minimizer on the Nehari manifold, with an energy changing sign at $λ^*$. The second has a strictly positive energy. For $λ<λ^*$ sufficiently close to $λ^*$, both solutions have positive energy. This contrasts with previous multiplicity results for the same problem, in which the two solutions obtained have energies of opposite signs.

</details>

Conclusion: 直柱在分式周长下具有带显式条件的唯一极小性，阈值由 $M$ 的谱隙与 $k,s$ 决定；谱约化、截面重排与单变量比较的组合方法可推广至平坦环面等周期性设定，并蕴含定量稳定性。

Abstract: Let $M$ be a closed connected Riemannian manifold and let $\mathcal P_s$ be the fractional perimeter on $M\times\mathbb R^k$ defined through its heat semigroup, where $k\ge1$ and $0<s<1$. We prove that the straight cylinder $M\times B_R$, with $B_R\subset\mathbb R^k$ a ball of radius $R$, uniquely minimizes $\mathcal P_s$ among all measurable sets of the same volume, up to translations in $\mathbb R^k$ and null sets, whenever $R\sqrt{λ_1(M)}$ is at least an explicit threshold depending only on $k$ and $s$. Here $λ_1(M)$ is the first positive eigenvalue of the nonnegative Laplace--Beltrami operator $-Δ_M$ on $M$. Abstract: We establish a global large-data existence theory for the Bhatnagar--Gross--Krook equation in a slab with the physical collision frequency given by the local density. We consider both inflow and Maxwell boundary conditions and assume only finite mass, kinetic energy, and entropy of the initial data, together with the corresponding inflow flux bounds. The data need not be small or pointwise bounded, and we impose neither positive lower bounds on the density or temperature nor prescribed velocity moments above order two. To our knowledge, this is the first nonperturbative global existence result for this initial and boundary value problem under these assumptions. The central obstacle is the simultaneous loss of energy compactness and integrability of the unrenormalized collision operator. Finite energy alone does not give uniform integrability of the second velocity moment, while the density dependent collision frequency prevents separate local $L^1$ control of the gain and loss terms. We overcome these difficulties through a compensated estimate uniform in the truncation of the collision frequency, a de la Vallée Poussin weight determined by the data, and two successive renormalizations using entropy production. This yields the compactness of the macroscopic energy, local Maxwellians, and nonlinear collision term, and hence global renormalized solutions.

Abstract: We first prove a rigidity result for uniformly rotating vortex patch solutions to Euler equations in bounded simply-connected planar domains. Namely, if a bounded simply-connected domain with real-analytic boundary contains a nontrivial vortex patch that remains strictly inside the domain while undergoing a uniform rigid rotation, then the ambient domain must be a disc centered at the rotation center. The proof combines the rotating-frame formulation with harmonic unique continuation and the generalized Schwarz's reflection principle across analytic boundary arcs. These tools imply that, unless the outer domain is rotationally invariant, the velocity must vanish in an open region surrounding the patch, contradicting the nonzero circulation generated by the patch. In the same spirit, we show that only circular domains possess a circular point vortex orbit.
  As a second result, we construct quasi-periodic vortex patch solutions to the Euler equations in domains that are small perturbations of the unit disc $\mathbb{D}$. The amplitudes of the solutions are linked to the size of the domain deformation, allowing the classical Rankine vortices $b\cdot\mathbb{D}$, $b\in(0,1)$, to be regarded as equilibrium states. Our analysis is perturbative with respect to the construction developed in the undeformed case, and the resulting quasi-periodic solutions exist for most values of the inner radius $b$. This provides the first construction of quasi-periodic vortex patch solutions to the Euler equations in non-radial domains. Moreover, combined with the rigidity result established above, this shows that, in the periodic case, these solutions cannot arise from a purely rigid rotation.

Abstract: We study a discrete Lagrangian model for a one-dimensional chain of rigid rods coupled by elastic springs. We characterize the geometry of its zero-energy constraint and distinguish the spinner and flipper regimes. Under graph-type hypotheses, selected zero-energy chains converge, after rescaling, to trajectories of an autonomous ordinary differential equation. For the physical linkage, we identify a simple alternating selection criterion under which chains form a unique monotone heteroclinic family in the spinner regime. In the flipper regime, we determine the topology of the constraint set and the number of successors, but leave global branch selection open. We also derive one- and two-component continuum residual expansions, distinguishing consistency from convergence of time-dependent solutions. Reproducible numerical experiments test the selected spinner recurrence and its asymptotic multiplier; for a simplified one-component model with smooth periodic data, they recover first- and second-order discrepancies for the leading and corrected continuum equations, respectively.

Abstract: Let $m_T[0,1)$ denote the number of Laplacian eigenvalues of a tree $T$ that are strictly less than $1$. In this paper, for any diameter $d$, $m_T[0,1)=\lceil(d+1)/3\rceil$ if and only if $γ(T)=\lceil(d+1)/3\rceil$; we characterize all trees satisfying this necessary and sufficient condition, and also prove that almost all trees satisfy $m_T[0,1)\ge\lceil(d+1)/3\rceil+1$.

Abstract: Braces play a fundamental role in matching theory, as they, together with bricks, constitute the basic building blocks of matching covered graphs in the tight cut decomposition. A brace is minimal if deleting any edge from it results in a graph that is not a brace. We prove that every perfect matching of a minimal brace of order at least six contains an edge whose ends both have degree three. Consequently, every such brace has at least three edges with this property. Combining this result with the forest structure induced by the noncubic vertices, we show that every minimal brace $G$ of order $n\ge6$ and size $m$, other than $K_{3,3}$, satisfies $n_3(G)\ge\max\left\{8,\left\lceil\frac{2n+8}{5}\right\rceil, \left\lceil\frac{m-n+4}{2}\right\rceil\right\}$, where $n_3(G)$ is the number of cubic vertices of $G$. Finally, we characterize the graphs attaining the constant lower bound: a minimal brace has exactly eight cubic vertices if and only if it is isomorphic to $B_8$, $B_{10}$, $Q_{10}^{+}$, or $Q_{12}$.

The construction combines a local similarity transformation with a transport potential and joint routing across consecutive row gaps. Exact verification of finite certificates, followed by induction, proves the required routing inequalities for every admissible width. We also give an entirely analytic construction with fewer than $7N/2$ dependencies and prove a lower bound of $5N/2-O(\sqrt N)$ for the retained path and full rectangular grid minor. Abstract: For a fixed integer $k\ge 2$, let $kK_3$ denote the vertex-disjoint union of $k$ triangles. A recent fixed-size spectral theorem of Das and Yamini asserts that, for all sufficiently large $m$, every $kK_3$-free graph $G$ of size $m$ satisfies $λ(G)\le (k-1)+\sqrt{m-k(k-1)},$ and equality holds if and only if $(2k-1)\mid m$ and $ G\cong \bigl(K_{2k-1}\vee qK_1\bigr)\cup tK_1,\, q=\frac{m}{2k-1}-(k-1) $ for some $t\ge 0$. They explicitly posed the open problem: Let $k\ge 2$ be fixed and $\ell$ be a residue in $\{1,\dots,2k-2\}$. For all sufficiently large integers $m\equiv \ell\pmod{2k-1}$, determine the exact value of $\max\bigl\{λ(G): e(G)=m,\ G\text{ is }kK_3\text{-free}\bigr\}, $ and characterize all graphs attaining this maximum. In this paper, using the positive-defect version of the bounded-core method for divisible sizes together with several new ideas developed in this paper, we give a complete solution to the aforementioned open problem.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tutte流猜想关注图在何种条件下存在取值较小的无处为零整数流，是图论中的核心难题。作者希望寻找一个统一而强有力的工具，把这些分散的流定理（如Seymour六流定理、六边连通三流定理、双生成树四流定理）纳入同一框架，并借此推进模q定向、加法基猜想和五流猜想等未决问题。

Abstract: Tutte's flow conjectures ask when a graph has a nowhere-zero flow with small integer values. We approach these questions through spanning-tree packing. A common analytic argument recovers Seymour's six-flow theorem, the three-flow theorem for six-edge-connected graphs, and the four-flow theorem for graphs with two edge-disjoint spanning trees. Its main new consequence is a prescribed-orientation theorem: for every integer $q\ge2$, $q$ edge-disjoint spanning trees suffice to realize every compatible outdegree prescription modulo $q$. For odd prime moduli this proves the graphic Additive Base Conjecture. A version with prescribed ranges of edge values gives sharp circular-flow bounds from tree packing and a bound in terms of cycle rank that is strictly below six for connected bridgeless graphs. We also examine a reformulation of the five-flow conjecture on graphs obtained by tripling every edge of a three-edge-connected cubic graph. A $46$-vertex graph with fractional spanning-tree packing number $41/9$ and no modulo-five orientation shows that packing greater than $9/2$ alone does not guarantee such an orientation for arbitrary graphs. The proof combines the minimization method of Alon, Bucić, and Davies with integral rounding.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受普通Turán问题与ℓ-度Turán问题并行发展的启发，作者希望理解（可能为无穷的）r-一致超图族的(r-2)-一致Turán密度的结构，特别是其取值集合是否具有相变、是否可数以及拓扑性质（闭性）。

Method: 研究(r-2)-一致Turán密度集合；对充分大的r证明相变现象；刻画阈值以下的密度为有限调色板Lagrangian，并证明每个值可由有限禁族实现；改编Pikhurko的方法分析密度集合的极限点与闭性。

Result: 对充分大的r，密度在4r^{-r}处发生相变：阈值以下为可数代数值集合，阈值以上为完整区间[4r^{-r},1]。对每个r≥3，阈值以下密度恰为该范围内的有限调色板Lagrangian，且每个值由有限禁族实现。对每个r≥3，一致密度集合缺失阈值以下连续多个极限点，因而非闭。

Conclusion: (r-2)-一致Turán密度在大r时呈现清晰的相变结构：低密度区为可数的代数（有限调色板Lagrangian）值，高密度区连续填满区间；且密度集合对每个r≥3都不是闭集，说明其极限点结构复杂。

Abstract: Motivated by parallel developments in ordinary and $\ell$-degree Turán problems, we study the set of $(r-2)$-uniform Turán densities of possibly infinite families of $r$-uniform hypergraphs. We prove that, for all sufficiently large $r$, these densities exhibit a phase transition at $4r^{-r}$, from a countable set of algebraic values below this threshold to the full interval $[4r^{-r},1]$. For every $r\ge3$, the densities below the threshold are exactly the finite-palette Lagrangians in that range, and each is realized by a finite forbidden family. Adapting a method of Pikhurko, we show that, for every $r\ge3$, the set of uniform densities omits continuum many of its limit points below the threshold and is therefore not closed.

</details>


### [68] [Extremal subspace covers in finite vector spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07051)
*Mohsen Aliabadi*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结果指出覆盖 n 维有限向量空间 V 至少且恰好需要 q+1 个真子空间。作者希望在覆盖定理基础上做极值加细：不仅关心覆盖所需子空间数目，还关心 m 个真子空间并集能有多大，以及等号结构、最小覆盖结构、并集缺陷等问题。同时将“覆盖所有向量”推广为“与每个基相交”的基阻塞族。

Method: 设 V 为 F_q 上 n 维向量空间，1≤m≤q+1。通过组合计数与线性子空间结构分析，求 maximize |W_1∪…∪W_m|；对等号情形进行分类。进一步定义 basis-blocking family，即真子空间族其并集与 V 的每个基相交，求最小规模并分类极值族。最后给出仿射类比与极值族的初等识别准则。

Result: 证明 max |W_1∪…∪W_m| = q^{n-2}(1+m(q-1))。对于 m≥2，等号成立当且仅当这些子空间是包含同一余维二子空间的不同超平面。作为推论，得到真子空间最小覆盖的结构分类，以及 q 个真子空间并集的尖锐缺陷估计。基阻塞族的最小规模为 q，并完成该规模极值族的分类；同时建立仿射类比和初等识别准则。

Conclusion: 该工作对有限向量空间被真子空间覆盖的问题给出统一的极值组合刻画，将经典覆盖定理细化为并集大小精确公式、等号分类、最小覆盖结构、缺陷估计及基阻塞族理论。

Abstract: Let $V$ be an $n$-dimensional vector space over the finite field $\mathbb F_q$, where $n\ge 2$. It is classical that $q+1$ proper subspaces are necessary and sufficient to cover $V$ [@Jamison1977; @Khare2009; @Clark2012]. We study extremal refinements of this covering theorem.
  For $1\le m\le q+1$, we determine the maximum possible size of the union of $m$ proper subspaces of $V$, proving that $$ \max_{W_1,\ldots,W_m<V} \left|W_1\cup\cdots\cup W_m\right| =
q^{n-2}\bigl(1+m(q-1)\bigr). $$ We also classify all equality cases: for $m\ge 2$, equality holds precisely when the subspaces are distinct hyperplanes containing a common codimension-two subspace. As consequences, we obtain a structural classification of minimum covers of $V$ by proper subspaces and a sharp defect estimate for unions of $q$ proper subspaces.
  We then introduce basis-blocking families, namely families of proper subspaces whose union meets every basis of $V$. We prove that the minimum size of such a family is $q$ and classify all extremal families of this size. Finally, we establish the affine analogue and give an elementary recognition criterion for extremal families. Together, these results provide a unified extremal-combinatorial description of coverings of finite vector spaces by proper subspaces.

</details>

Conclusion: 该结果推动了KL多项式实根性与负根性研究，表明稀疏铺路拟阵具有良好（单重负实根）的根结构，并揭示了特定均匀拟阵作为"极值"与全体高余秩稀疏铺路拟阵之间的全局根交错关系，为后续扩展至更一般拟阵类的根分布与交错性研究提供了思路。

Abstract: We prove that every nonconstant Kazhdan--Lusztig polynomial of a sparse paving matroid has only simple negative zeros. For each rank $d\ge5$, the Kazhdan--Lusztig polynomial of the uniform matroid of rank $d-2$ on $d$ elements strictly interlaces those of all sparse paving matroids of rank $d$ and corank at least two.

### [70] [Cyclic Hamilton Cycle Decompositions of Carousel Tournaments of Order $pq$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07102)
*Hongci Liao,Yongju Peng,Guang Li,Yingbin Ma*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kelly 猜想问是否每个正则锦标赛都容许 Hamilton 圈分解。本文受其"对称保持"扩展的驱动，研究轮盘锦标赛 T_n = Cay(Z_n, {1,2,...,(n-1)/2}) 在平移 1 下不变的循环 Hamilton 分解。n 为素数时答案显然，但合数阶会出现真实障碍：非单位差生成短圈而非 Hamilton 圈，故需针对合数阶建立新方法。

Method: 对于 n=pq（7≤p<q 为素数），结合两类技术：(1) 素数域上的 Hamilton 差序列；(2) 匹配论证，用于在 Z_{pq} 中构造两条差集不相交的基路径。由此拼装出在平移 1 下不变的 Hamilton 圈分解。

Result: 证明了当 n=pq（p,q 为素数且 7≤p<q）时，轮盘锦标赛 T_n 容许循环 Hamilton 圈分解，给出一个无限族例子；同时指出素数阶情形平凡，而合数阶存在由非单位差引起的实质障碍。

Conclusion: 该结果支持正则锦标赛 Hamilton 分解问题的循环、对称保持扩展，表明在合数阶轮盘锦标赛上，通过差序列与匹配的组合方法可以克服非单位差带来的障碍，构造出平移不变的 Hamilton 分解。

Abstract: Kelly's conjecture asks whether every regular tournament admits a Hamilton cycle decomposition. Motivated by its symmetry-preserving extension, we study cyclic Hamilton decompositions of carousel tournaments. For an odd integer $n$, let \[ T_n=\Cay\left( \mathbb Z_n,\left\{1,2,\ldots,\frac{n-1}{2}\right\} \right) \] be the carousel tournament. We ask whether $T_n$ has a Hamilton decomposition invariant under translation by $1$. Although the answer is immediate when $n$ is prime, composite orders introduce a genuine obstruction: nonunit differences generate short cycles rather than Hamilton cycles. We resolve a general composite-order family by proving that, whenever $n=pq$ for primes $7\le p<q$, the tournament $T_n$ admits a cyclic Hamilton cycle decomposition. The proof combines Hamiltonian difference sequences over prime fields with a matching argument that constructs two base paths with disjoint difference sets in $\mathbb Z_{pq}$. Thus our result gives an infinite family supporting the cyclic, symmetry-preserving extension of the Hamilton decomposition problem for regular tournaments.

</details>

*Jason Stack,Nathan Williams*

Motivation: 研究 W-稳定、忠实且各参数同次的齐次参数系所携带的表示，推广 Ito–Okada 的已有结果，并厘清商环 S/(Θ) 的 W-模结构。

Method: 引入 posh hsop 及“携带表示 U”的概念；对所有使得度为 p 的 posh hsop 携带 U 存在的 posh 对 (p, U) 进行分类；证明这类 U 均为 V* 的 Galois 扭。

Result: 完成 posh 对 (p, U) 的分类；每个这样的 U 是 V* 的 Galois 扭；由推广 Ito–Okada 工作得到 S/(Θ) 是 W 的置换模当且仅当 U ≅ V*。

Conclusion: posh hsop 携带的表示受限为 V* 的 Galois 扭；商环 S/(Θ) 的置换模性质恰对应于 U ≅ V*，为相关不变量理论提供了结构性判据。

Abstract: Let $W$ be an irreducible complex reflection group with reflection representation $V$. A $W$-stable, faithful homogeneous system of parameters $Θ\subseteq \mathrm{Sym}(V^*)$ of common positive degree $p$ is called a \emph{posh hsop}; $Θ$ \emph{carries} a $W$-representation $U$ if $Θ\simeq U$ as ungraded $W$-modules. We classify the \emph{posh} pairs $(p,U)$ for which a posh hsop of degree $p$ carrying $U$ exists. Every such $U$ is a Galois twist of $V^*$. Extending work of Ito and Okada, we deduce that the quotient $S/(Θ)$ is a permutation module for $W$ if and only if $U \simeq V^*$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Let $m,n\geq2$ be integers. We study the least exponent $t_{m,n}$ such that the $m$-fold additive energy of any subset $A$ of the discrete cube $\{0,1,\cdots,n-1\}^d$ in any dimension $d$ satisfies $E_m(A)\leq |A|^{t_{m,n}}$. For every fixed $m$, we obtain the asymptotic formula $$
  t_{m,n}=2m-1-(1+o(1))\log_n
  \frac{(2m-1)^{m-1/2}}{(2m)^{m-1}}
  \qquad(n\to\infty). $$ For $m=2$, this gives $$ t_{2,n} = 3 - (1+o(1)) \log_n\frac{3\sqrt{3}}{4}, $$ proving a conjecture of the first author.

</details>

In this range we also give exact criteria for connected, simple, and equimultiple target factors. The proofs use a system of integer counts recording the number of added edges of each type. Every system satisfying these equations gives an embedding with the minimum multiplicity spread allowed by

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单射词复形在群上同调、同调稳定性及随机-随机马尔可夫链中有应用，已有结果（Farmer的同调楔形、Björner-Wachs的壳性、Reiner-Webb的S_n-模结构）表明其结构丰富。作者希望引入自然子复形——固定排列模式σ并只取避免σ的单射词——来拓展这一理论，并探索壳性、同调与计数之间的联系。

Method: 定义模式避免单射词复形；利用壳性理论（当σ以其最大或最小字母开始或结束）证明子复形可壳；构造避免此类模式复形的同调基；提出一种用枚举组合学证明壳性的技术；计算全复形及各可壳子复形的h-数的组合公式。

Result: 证明了若σ以最大或最小字母开始或结束，则模式避免复形可壳，并给出同调基；长度3的全部模式均具此性质，其顶同调秩为Riordan数；长度4的模式除四个外均有此性质，其中两个剩余模式用另一方法证得壳性；给出全复形及可壳子复形所有h-数的组合公式；用可壳性推出模式避免排列的新精细计数公式。

Conclusion: 模式避免单射词复形构成单射词复形理论的自然推广，其壳性与同调可由σ的端点字母条件保证，h-数公式与Riordan数等经典计数对象相联系，且壳性可作为推导模式避免排列精细计数公式的工具，建立了几何组合学与枚举组合学之间的双向联系。

Abstract: The complex of injective words is a cell complex that arises in a number of different areas. It has applications to proving homological stability and to the study of group cohomology, and it is closely related to the random-to-random Markov chain. This complex was first studied by Farmer, who proved it has the homology of a wedge of top-dimensional spheres. Later, Björner and Wachs established its shellability, and Reiner and Webb uncovered its $S_n$-module structure, observing in the process that the rank of its top homology group is the $n$th derangement number.
  We introduce natural subcomplexes of the complex of injective words by fixing a permutation pattern $σ$ and considering only those injective words in the alphabet $\{1,2,\dots,n\} $ that avoid $σ$. We prove that such pattern-avoiding complexes are shellable if $σ$ begins or ends with its largest or smallest letter, and we construct homology bases for the complexes avoiding such patterns. For patterns of length 3, all of which have this property, we show that the rank of the top homology of the resulting complex is a Riordan number. All but four patterns of length 4 also have this property, and for two of the remaining four patterns, we establish shellability using a different method.
  We also introduce a technique to use enumerative combinatorics to prove shellability, and we apply it to the complex of separable injective words, thereby deducing shellability in this case. Along the way, we give a combinatorial formula for all of the $h$-numbers in the full complex of injective words as well as for each of the subcomplexes which we prove are shellable. Going in the other direction, we use shellability of complexes of pattern-avoiding injective words to deduce new refined counting formulas for pattern-avoiding permutations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASM的模式避免已有丰富理论,但其自然矩形推广(ASR)的研究尚不充分。作者希望建立ASR上的可扩展模式避免框架,以统一刻画ASM角部的计数结构并连接经典组合对象。

Method: 定义ASR可扩展避免模式π(即可作为避免π的ASM的左上角)。针对等价类{312,132,213,231}中的每个长度3模式,建立按尺寸r×k与非空行数d计数的完整递推系统;对π=312猜测闭式表达式,并通过涉及Schröder选票数、精化Schröder数和不垂直穿过主对角线的Delannoy路径的双射进行证明;对(r-1)×(r+1)的213可扩展避免ASR构造到长度r小Schröder路径的双射。

Result: 对{312,132,213,231}中每个模式得到完整递推关系;对π=312给出闭式猜测并在若干对角切片上借助Schröder选票数、精化Schröder数与Delannoy路径的双射完成证明;对(r-1)×(r+1)的213可扩展避免ASR给出到小Schröder路径的双射。长度3的123与321模式则更难处理,与ASM情形相呼应。

Conclusion: 可扩展模式避免为ASR的计数提供了统一框架,并揭示其与Schröder数、Delannoy路径等经典对象的深刻联系;123与321的困难性表明ASR情形忠实反映了ASM模式避免的复杂性。

Abstract: We introduce extendable pattern avoidance for alternating sign rectangles (ASRs), the natural rectangular generalization of alternating sign matrices (ASMs). An ASR extendably avoids a pattern $π$ if it is the upper left corner of an ASM avoiding $π$. For each of the four length-three patterns in the equivalence class $\{312, 132, 213, 231\}$ we establish a complete system of recurrence relations enumerating extendably $π$-avoiding ASRs of size $r \times k$ with a prescribed number $d$ of nonempty rows. For $π= 312$ we further conjecture a closed-form expression and prove it on several diagonal slices via bijections involving Schröder ballot numbers, refined Schröder numbers and Delannoy paths that do not cross the main diagonal vertically. For ASRs of size $(r-1) \times (r+1)$ extendably avoiding $213$ we give a bijection to little Schröder paths of length $r$. The remaining patterns of length three, $123$ and $321$, are more elusive, mirroring the situation of ASMs.

</details>

Abstract: The hypersimplex $Δ_{k,n}$ is the convex hull of all 0/1-vectors of length $ n $ with coordinate sum $ k $. Early conjectured, and Kim proved, a combinatorial formula for the $ h^* $-polynomial of $Δ_{k,n}$ in terms of hypersimplicial decorated ordered set partitions. In this paper, we refine the Early--Kim formula to the flag $h$-numbers of the alcove triangulation of $\hyp$. We also give a representation-theoretic interpretation of its flag $f$-numbers in terms of Young permutation modules.

Abstract: A family of paths in a graph $G$ strongly separates the edges of $G$ if for every ordered pair of distinct edges $(e,f)$ some path in the family contains $e$ and avoids $f$; the minimum size of such a family is denoted by $\operatorname{ssp}(G)$. Bonamy, Botler, Dross, Naia, and Skokan proved, for every $n$-vertex graph $G$, that $\operatorname{ssp}(G)\le 19n$; Liu, Xu, and Yang recently improved this to $\operatorname{ssp}(G)\le 10n-o(n)$. We prove, for every $n$-vertex graph $G$, that $\operatorname{ssp}(G)\le 7.75n$.

</details>

Abstract: Consider a finite planar graph with positive real edge weights and designated boundary vertices, called nodes. Such a graph is called a circular planar electrical network. A grove is a spanning forest in which every component contains at least one node. The connected components of a grove determine a partition of the nodes. We relate weighted grove counts to invariant theory for the symplectic group.
  To a planar electrical network $G$, we associate an $\mathrm{Sp}(2n)$-invariant tensor $Z_G$. For $\mathrm{Sp}(2)=\mathrm{SL}(2)$, we expand $Z_G$ in the Temperley--Lieb basis indexed by noncrossing matchings and relate its coefficients to the Kenyon--Wilson grove formulas. For $\mathrm{Sp}(4)$, we give reduction rules for superpositions of two groves and prove that the tensors indexed by $3$-noncrossing matchings form a basis of the space of $\mathrm{Sp}(4)$-invariant tensors. The coefficients of $Z_G$ in this basis are weighted counts of reduced double groves, up to normalization.

Abstract: An $(s,p)$-edge-coloring of a graph $G$ is an edge coloring using $s$ colors such that exactly $p$ colors appear at each vertex. To generalize the notion of proper edge-coloring, these colorings are defined to be equitable: the numbers of edges of each color incident to a vertex are fairly distributed. We find the necessary and sufficient conditions for embedding an equitable $(s_1,p_1)$-edge-coloring of $K_{n_1}$ into an equitable $(s_2,p_2)$-edge-coloring of $K_{n_2}$. We focus on the values of $n_1$, $p_1$, $n_2$, and $p_2$ where $s_1$ is necessarily larger than $p_1$ and $s_2$ is necessarily larger than $p_2$.

Motivation: 此类规范化多项式与排列统计生成函数存在深刻联系，作者希望推广已知的 MacMahon 恒等式并统一多个统计量的生成函数表示。

Result: 获得了该规范化多项式的三个公式：（1）推广 MacMahon 恒等式；（2）inv 与广义 mstc 统计的生成函数；（3）含 $2n$ 个 $q$-二项式系数的有限求和式。

Conclusion: 基本超几何级数在特定参数约束下可规范化为正多项式，且该多项式具有多种组合统计生成函数表示，拓展了 MacMahon 恒等式及多重集排列统计理论。

Abstract: The basic hypergeometric series ${}_{n+1}φ_{n}$ whose upper and lower parameters are non-negative integer powers of $q$ satisfying a certain constraint can be, after a normalisation, computed as a positive polynomial. We derive three formulas for this polynomial. The first formula is a generalisation of the MacMahon identity in which an infinite binomial series is identified with a normalised generating function of des and maj statistics of multiset permutations. The second formula is a generating function of multiset permutations for the inversion statistic and a generalisation of the statistic mstc. The third formula is a finite sum containing $2n$ $q$-binomial coefficients.

</details>


### [81] [Random Cayley sum hypergraphs and $k$-fold sumsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08629)
*Jihyo Chae,Hyunwoo Lee*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限阿贝尔群中子集成为k重和集（k-fold sumset）的阈值函数$f_k(\Gamma)$，证明其上界为$\widetilde{O}(n^{(2k-1)/(4k-3)})$，并在特定条件下给出下界$\widetilde{\Omega}(n^{1/k})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Alon和Pham的近期结果，改进Balogh、Liu和Sharifzadeh之前的上界，并统一k=2的情形。研究动机在于确定有限阿贝尔群中“几乎所有子集都是k重和集”这一性质所需补集的最小规模。

Method: 利用随机Cayley和超图（random Cayley sum hypergraph）的独立数（independence number）的新上界作为主要证明工具，该上界本身也可能具有独立价值。

Result: 对所有有限阿贝尔群$\Gamma$和整数$k\geq 2$，证明了$f_k(\Gamma) \leq \widetilde{O}(n^{(2k-1)/(4k-3)})$；当$\Gamma$没有阶整除$k$的非平凡元素时，证明了$f_k(\Gamma) \geq \widetilde{\Omega}(n^{1/k})$。该上界改进了先前结果，并在$k=2$时恢复了Alon-Pham的界。

Conclusion: 给出了有限阿贝尔群中k重和集阈值函数的新上下界，改进了已有结果，并通过随机Cayley和超图的独立数估计提供了新的证明技术。

Abstract: We denote by $f_k(Γ)$ the largest integer with the property that every subset of a finite abelian group $Γ$ of size at least $|Γ| - f_k(Γ)$ is a $k$-fold sumset. Extending a recent result of Alon and Pham, we prove that
  $$
  f_k(Γ) \leq \widetilde{O} \left(n^{(2k-1)/(4k-3)}\right)
  $$ holds for all finite abelian groups $Γ$ and integers $k \geq 2$, where $n = |Γ|$. Additionally, we also show that the lower bound $f_k(Γ) \geq \widetildeΩ \left(n^{1/k}\right)$ holds if $Γ$ has no nontrivial element of order dividing $k$. Our upper bound improves a previous result of Balogh, Liu, and Sharifzadeh, and recovers the bound of Alon and Pham in the case $k = 2$. The proof relies on a new upper bound for the independence number of random Cayley sum hypergraphs, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [82] [The Area Asymptotics of $(sn,n)$-Dyck Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07671)
*Evan Conway,James Harbour*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究 $(sn,n)$-Dyck 路径的总面积与平均面积，给出总面积精确公式、上下界及渐近结果，并验证了 Kotesovec 关于某二项式和的渐近猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Merlini、Sprugnoli 和 Verri 已研究过 $s=1$ 情形下总面积问题，本文旨在将该结果推广到一般的 Fuss-Catalan 路径 $(sn,n)$-Dyck 路径，并刻画其面积随 $s$、$n$ 变化的渐近行为；同时验证 Kotesovec 的猜想。

Method: 对停留在直线 $x=sy$ 下方、从 $(0,0)$ 到 $(sn,n)$ 的格路径（由 Fuss-Catalan 数计数）推导总面积精确公式，并据此导出总面积与平均面积的显式上下界及渐近展开。

Result: 得到总面积精确公式及总面积、平均面积的显式上下界；固定 $s$ 时平均面积渐近为 $\sqrt{\pi s(s+1)/8}\cdot n^{3/2}$；固定 $n$ 且 $s$ 大时平均面积渐近为 $sn\cdot Q(n)/2$，其中 $Q(n)$ 为 Ramanujan $Q$-函数；并证实 Kotesovec 关于某二项式和的渐近猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作将面积统计结果从经典 Dyck 路径推广至 Fuss-Catalan 路径，刻画了双参数渐近，并解决了相关二项式和的渐近猜想。

Abstract: We study the total and average area of $(sn,n)$-Dyck paths: lattice paths from $(0,0)$ to $(sn,n)$ that stay weakly below the line $x=sy$, counted by the Fuss-Catalan numbers. Generalizing a result of Merlini, Sprugnoli, and Verri for the case $s=1$, we derive an exact formula for the total area over all such paths. From this we obtain explicit upper and lower bounds for both the total and the average area, together with the corresponding asymptotics: for fixed $s$, the average area is asymptotic to $\sqrt{πs(s+1)/8} \cdot n^{3/2}$, while for fixed $n$ it is asymptotic to $sn \cdot Q(n)/2$ as $s$ grows large, where $Q(n)$ denotes Ramanujan's $Q$-function. Along the way, we confirm a conjecture of Kotesovec on the asymptotics of a binomial sum that also arises in several other enumeration problems.

</details>


### [83] [Quantum walks and graph operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07673)
*Joy Cooper,Homer De Vera,Hermie Monterde,S. A. Talebpour,Xuzhu,Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子游走在图运算（笛卡尔积、联图、补图）下的行为，刻画了峰值态传输与pretty good态传输在这些运算下的保持条件，并给出了原图与补图量子游走差异的界及Nordhaus-Gaddum型关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子游走是图上态传输的重要模型，但其在常见图运算（笛卡尔积、联、补图）下的传输性质保持条件，以及原图与补图之间量子游走行为的定量差异尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 以邻接矩阵A或拉普拉斯矩阵L定义转移矩阵U_X(t)。通过分析图运算下转移矩阵的结构，刻画峰值态传输和pretty good态传输的保持条件；定义f_{u,v}(t)与g_{u,v}(t)度量原图与补图的态传输差异，利用正则性、主特征值和bounding matrix导出上界。

Result: 给出峰值态传输与pretty good态传输在笛卡尔积、联、补图下的保持条件，并构造新图族。对f_{u,v}(t)：当X正则或M=L时上界为2/|V(X)|；当X非正则且M=A时用主特征值给出仅依赖A的上界。利用bounding matrix给出|U_X(t)_{u,v}|+|U_{X^c}(t)_{u,v}|与乘积的Nordhaus-Gaddum型界，并证明多数界对特定图族是紧的。

Conclusion: 图运算下态传输性质的保持条件可系统刻画，原图与补图量子游走行为差异可被有效界定，且所得界在若干图族中达到紧性。

Abstract: Let $U_X(t)$ be the transition matrix of a quantum walk on a graph $X$ relative to its adjacency matrix $A$ or the Laplacian matrix $L$. This paper investigates the behavior of quantum walks under Cartesian products, joins, and graph complements. We have two main goals. First, we characterize the conditions such that peak state transfer and pretty good state transfer are preserved under these operations, allowing us to construct new families of graphs admitting these properties. Our second goal is to analyze the relationship between the quantum walks on a graph and its complement. We provide bounds for $f_{u,v}(t)=\big|U_{X^c}(t)_{u,v}-e^{itδ}U_X(-t)_{u,v}\big|$ and $g_{u,v}(t)=\big||U_X(t)_{u,v}|-|U_{X^c}(t)_{u,v}|\big|$, where $δ=-1$ when dealing with $A$ and $δ=n$ otherwise. Note that $f_{u,v}(t)$ and $g_{u,v}(t)$ both measure the difference between the behavior of quantum state transfer between vertices $u$ and $v$ in a graph and its complement. If $X$ is regular or $M=L$, then $f_{u,v}(t)$ is bounded above by $\frac{2}{|V(X)|}$. If $X$ is non-regular and $M=A$, then we utilize the main eigenvalues of a graph to obtain an upper bound for $f_{u,v}(t)$ which depends only on $A$. We also use the bounding matrix of the graph to give bounds for the Nordhaus-Gaddum type relations $|U_X(t)_{u,v}|+|U_{X^c}(t)_{u,v}|$ and $|U_X(t)_{u,v}|\cdot |U_{X^c}(t)_{u,v}|$. Finally, we demonstrate that most of our bounds are sharp for certain families of graphs.

</details>


### [84] [The maximum size of simple solid matching covered graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07733)
*Tong Zhang,Wei Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文否证了Lucchesi-Murty关于solid匹配覆盖图最大边数的猜想，证明$2n$个顶点的简单solid匹配覆盖图最大边数为$n^2+2$，并刻画了达到该界的图；同时证明了$n\ge4$时简单solid brick最多有$n^2$条边。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lucchesi和Murty猜想存在正整数$N$，使得对任意$n\ge N$，$2n$个顶点的简单solid匹配覆盖图的最大边数为$n^2$。本文旨在检验并否定这一猜想，确定真实的最大边数上界并刻画极值图。

Method: 通过构造反例推翻原猜想，证明简单solid匹配覆盖图在$2n$（$n\ge2$）个顶点上的最大边数为$n^2+2$，并对达到该界的图进行完全刻画；进一步对简单solid brick进行边数分析，证明$n\ge4$时其边数不超过$n^2$。

Result: 否证了Lucchesi-Murty猜想；确定简单solid匹配覆盖图在$2n$（$n\ge2$）个顶点上的最大边数为$n^2+2$，并刻画了所有达到此界的图；证明$n\ge4$时简单solid brick的边数至多为$n^2$。

Conclusion: Lucchesi-Murty猜想不成立，solid匹配覆盖图的最大边数比猜想值多2；极值图已被完全刻画，且solid brick的边数上界为$n^2$。

Abstract: A connected graph with at least two vertices is matching covered if each of its edges is contained in a perfect matching. A matching covered graph is solid if every separating cut in it is a tight cut. A matching covered graph which is free of nontrivial tight cuts is a brick if it is nonbipartite. Every bipartite matching covered graph is solid. Lucchesi and Murty conjectured that there exists a positive integer $N$ such that, for every integer $n\ge N$, the maximum number of edges in a simple solid matching covered graph on $2n$ vertices is $n^2$. In this paper, we disprove this Conjecture, and show that the maximum number of edges of a simple solid matching covered graph on $2n$ ($n\ge2$) vertices is $n^2+2$. Moreover, we characterize the graphs attaining this bound. In addition, we prove that every simple solid brick of order $2n$ has at most $n^2$ edges for $n\ge4$.

</details>


### [85] [Spectral extremal problems on 1-planar graphs without Friendship graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07805)
*Jiamin Li,Dan Li,Yuanyuan Chen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究 $F_t$-free 1-平面图的谱极值问题，对所有 $t\geq1$ 与充分大的 $n$ 建立了极值图的结构定理，并确定 $\textit{spex}_{\mathcal{P}_1}(n,F_t)$ 及其唯一极值图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tait 与 Tobin (2017) 确定了充分大阶下最大谱半径的唯一平面图，Zhang、Wang 与 Wang (2024) 刻画了 1-平面图中对应的极值图。本文将研究对象推广到 $F_t$-free 1-平面图，寻求其谱极值图的结构刻画与谱半径精确值。

Method: 定义友谊图 $F_t$（$t$ 个三角形恰共享一个公共顶点），研究 $\textit{spex}_{\mathcal{P}_1}(n,F_t)$。通过对 $F_t$-free 1-平面图谱极值图建立结构定理，结合 $K_{3,6}$ 的作图性质，确定谱半径并刻画唯一极值图。

Result: 对所有 $t\geq1$ 和充分大的 $n$，证明每个极值图连通且包含 $K_{2,n-2}$ 的拷贝；当 $t\geq2$ 时，两个特殊顶点相邻，且其余顶点诱导子图为二部图。据此确定 $\textit{spex}_{\mathcal{P}_1}(n,F_t)$ 并刻画其唯一极值图。

Conclusion: 本文推广并深化了 1-平面图谱极值理论，给出了 $F_t$-free 1-平面图谱极值图的完整结构刻画与精确谱半径，证明了极值图的唯一性。

Abstract: Let $\textit{spex}_{\mathcal{P}_1}(n,F)$ be the maximum spectral radius among all $n$-vertex $F$-free $1$-planar graphs. Define $F_t$ as the friendship graph formed by $t$ triangles sharing exactly one common vertex. Tait and Tobin (2017)~\cite{Tait2017} used the fundamental structure of spectral extremal graphs to determine the unique planar graph with maximum spectral radius for sufficiently large order. Subsequently, Zhang, Wang and Wang (2024)~\cite{Zhang2024} characterized the corresponding extremal graph in the class of $1$-planar graphs. In this paper, we focus on $F_t$-free $1$-planar graphs and establish a structural theorem for their spectral extremal graphs for all $t\geq1$ and sufficiently large $n$. More precisely, every extremal graph is connected and contains a copy of $K_{2,n-2}$, and for $t\geq2$ the two distinguished vertices are adjacent and the subgraph induced by the remaining vertices is a bipartite graph. Based on this structure result together with the drawing properties of $K_{3,6}$, we determine $\textit{spex}_{\mathcal{P}_1}(n,F_t)$ and characterize its unique extremal graph.

</details>


### [86] [Shuffle Squares in Differentiable Words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07828)
*Michał Zwierzyński*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过二元游程可微性实验，给出了shuffle square（洗牌平方）的尖锐判定准则，涵盖C³、C²、C¹三个层级，并应用于Kolakoski前缀与二元词的修复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: shuffle square的可判定性与阈值问题缺乏精确刻画，尤其是不同可微性层级下的全局/局部判据、例外结构及可复现计算尚未系统给出，作者旨在用计算机辅助实验建立精确准则。

Method: 通过二元游程可微性（C³、C²、C¹层级）实验，结合精确递推、残差状态检查与独立的Python程序进行有限穷举与分类计算，并对morphism反射shuffle square的性质及二元词的删除距离、局部修复进行刻画。

Result: C³下长度>16的字典中每个C³-word当且仅当两字母重数均为偶数时是shuffle square，34个非空even-Parikh例外均光滑且在各更高可微性类中持续存在；C²的尖锐阈值为48，有212个非空例外；C¹无全局奇偶阈值，但长度≥36的even-Parikh非shuffle-square都有真非空shuffle-square前后缀，恰有230个非空even-Parikh C¹-word无此非空前缀，对应规避树最终含422条周期射线；非空Kolakoski前缀除长度4与8外，当且仅当两重数均为偶数时为shuffle square；对doubly binary words确定了精确删除距离与最大twins，并证明单点局部修复的尖锐界。

Conclusion: 不同可微性层级对应不同的尖锐奇偶阈值与例外结构，可微性越高阈值越低（C³为16、C²为48），C¹则退化为前后缀条件与周期射线结构；Kolakoski前缀给出一个几乎完全由奇偶性刻画的shuffle square判别，全部有限计算可由递推与Python程序复现。

Abstract: Experiments on binary run-length differentiability lead to sharp computer-assisted criteria for shuffle squares. Every $C^3$-word of length greater than $16$ is a shuffle square exactly when both letter multiplicities are even. All $34$ nonempty even-Parikh exceptions are smooth and persist in every higher differentiability class. For $C^2$ the sharp threshold is $48$, with $212$ nonempty exceptions. At level $C^1$ no global parity threshold exists, but every even-Parikh non-shuffle-square of length at least $36$ has proper nonempty shuffle-square prefixes and suffixes. Exactly $230$ nonempty even-Parikh $C^1$-words have no nonempty shuffle-square prefix. The full tree avoiding such prefixes eventually consists of $422$ periodic rays. Consequently, a nonempty Kolakoski prefix is a shuffle square exactly when both multiplicities are even, apart from lengths $4$ and $8$. We also characterize classes of morphisms reflecting shuffle squares. For doubly binary words, we determine the exact deletion distance and largest twins, and prove sharp bounds for single local repairs. Exact recurrences, residual-state checks, and separate Python programs make the finite computations reproducible.

</details>


### [87] [A proof of the Erdős--Gallai cycle decomposition conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07840)
*Jaehoon Kim*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了 Erdős–Gallai 猜想：任意 n 顶点图的边可分解为 O(n) 个圈和边；等价地，任意 n 顶点欧拉图的边可分解为 O(n) 个圈，从而在常数因子意义下证实了 Hajós 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1960 年代 Erdős 与 Gallai 提出猜想：每个 n 顶点图的边集能否用 O(n) 个圈与边覆盖（分解）。该问题长期未决，并与欧拉图中圈分解的 Hajós 猜想密切相关，是图分解领域的核心开放问题。

Method: 论文属于纯理论证明，核心手段是建立“边分解为圈与边”与原问题之间的等价性：将一般图问题化归为欧拉图的圈分解问题，再构造性地给出 O(n) 规模的圈分解方案。

Result: 证明了 Erdős–Gallai 猜想成立：任何 n 顶点图的边集都可分解为 O(n) 个圈和边；等价地，任何 n 顶点欧拉图可分解为 O(n) 个圈，即 Hajós 猜想在常数因子范围内成立。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了悬置约六十年的经典猜想，给出圈分解数量的线性上界，同时把 Hajós 猜想从渐近（常数因子）层面确定下来，对图分解理论具有基础性意义。

Abstract: In the 1960s, Erdős and Gallai conjectured that the edges of every $n$-vertex graph can be decomposed into $O(n)$ cycles and edges. We prove this conjecture. Equivalently, every Eulerian graph on $n$ vertices can be decomposed into $O(n)$ cycles, which confirms Hajós' conjecture up to a constant factor.

</details>


### [88] [A forbidden-induced-subgraph characterization of beautiful graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07924)
*Henning Wunderlich*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明了有限图是"beautiful graph"当且仅当它不含诱导 C4、gem、net、watch 或奇洞，并给出多项式时间识别与极小障碍族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Beautiful graphs 作为 Berge 图的一个遗传类被引入，但此前未获得完整的诱导子图（禁用子图）刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 从极大闭邻域构造偏序：其代表元诱导出二部图，其支配区域可赋予相容定向；借助经典的 double-bound graphs 以及极大双团与区间交封闭偏序集之间的对应关系，用单个 order matrix 表示原图及其所有主子矩阵对应的诱导子图。

Result: 证明有限图 beautiful 等价于无诱导 C4、gem、net、watch 及奇洞；等价地，它们恰为允许"每个区间均为链"的偏序的 C4-free 可比图。推论包括多项式时间识别、精确的极小障碍族，以及 K4-free 情形的修正刻画。

Conclusion: 本文给出了 beautiful graphs 的完整诱导禁用子图刻画与结构性（可序化）表征，统一了表示与识别问题，并修正了 K4-free 情形下的既有结论。

Abstract: A graph is beautiful if each of its induced subgraphs is the intersection graph of all maximal nonempty 1-rectangles of a binary matrix, with adjacency defined by a common cell. Beautiful graphs were introduced as a hereditary class of Berge graphs, but a complete forbidden-induced-subgraph characterization was not obtained. We prove that a finite graph is beautiful if and only if it has no induced $C_4$, gem, net, watch, or odd hole. More precisely, these graphs are exactly the $C_4$-free comparability graphs admitting a partial order in which every interval is a chain. The proof constructs such an order from inclusion-maximal closed neighbourhoods: their representatives induce a bipartite graph whose domination regions admit compatible orientations. One order matrix then represents the graph and, through its principal submatrices, every induced subgraph. The representation step is formulated using classical double-bound graphs and the established correspondence between maximal bicliques and interval-intersection-closed posets. Consequences include polynomial-time recognition, the exact minimal obstruction families, and a corrected characterization in the $K_4$-free case.

</details>


### [89] [A duality-preserving extension of the Worley-Sagan insertion and Haiman's mixed insertion for the hyperoctahedral group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08171)
*Masato Nakagiri*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将 Worley-Sagan 插入与 Haiman 混合插入从对称群推广到带色置换，保持二者对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Worley-Sagan 插入与 Haiman 混合插入均为移位 Young 表上的插入算法，各自给出对称群与同形移位表对之间的 RS 型对应，且已知二者互为对偶。作者希望在保持对偶关系的前提下将这两种插入推广到更一般的带色置换。

Method: 构造两种从带色置换产生移位表对的扩展插入。对 Worley-Sagan 的扩展不同于 Sagan "Knuth 版本"限制到带色置换的结果。证明对偶性时，通过"加倍"移位表将扩展插入嵌入到 Shimozono-White 的非移位表双重混合插入中，并利用该插入的自对偶性。

Result: 得到两个保持对偶关系的扩展插入：均由带色置换产生移位表对，且通过嵌入双重混合插入成功证明其互为对偶。

Conclusion: 移位表插入可在带色置换层面自然推广，且对偶性可通过与双重混合插入的嵌入关系得以保持和证明。

Abstract: The Worley-Sagan insertion and Haiman's mixed insertion are insertion algorithms for shifted Young tableaux, and each of them gives a Robinson-Schensted-type correspondence between the symmetric group of degree $n$ and a set consisting of certain pairs of same-shape shifted Young tableaux with $n$ cells. It is a known fact that these two insertions are dual to each other. Our purpose is to give an extension of these two insertions without losing the duality relationship. The extended ones will be insertions producing pairs of shifted tableaux from colored permutations. Our extension of the Worley-Sagan insertion is different from the restriction of Sagan's own "Knuth version" to colored permutations. In proving the duality between our extended insertions, we "embed" them into Shimozono and White's doubly mixed insertion for unshifted tableaux by "doubling" shifted tableaux and use the self-duality of the doubly mixed insertion shown by Shimozono and White.

</details>


### [90] [Maximum $2$-scattered subspaces of $V(r,q^6)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08237)
*Francesco Ghiandoni,Alessandro Giannoni*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文对每个素幂 $q$ 及与 6 互素的整数 $r\\geq5$，构造了 $V(r,q^6)$ 中的最大 $2$-scattered $\\mathbb{F}_q$-子空间，并给出等价的 MRD 码结果及维数推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大 $2$-scattered 子空间与 rank-metric 码（MRD 码）密切相关，是有限几何与编码理论的重要研究对象。已有构造多针对特定维数或参数，作者希望在广泛参数范围内系统构造最大 $2$-scattered 子空间，尤其覆盖 $V(r,q^6)$ 的一般维数 $r$。

Method: 将 $\\mathbb{F}_{q^{6r}}$ 上的二维 $\\mathbb{F}_{q^r}$-子空间视为 $\\mathbb{F}_{q^6}$ 上的 $r$ 维向量空间，从而构造 $V(r,q^6)$ 中的 $2$-scattered $\\mathbb{F}_q$-子空间。通过迹论证（trace argument）将证明归结为两个 $\\mathbb{F}_{q^r}$-子空间的互补性；再分情形讨论该互补性，分别化为一个三次多项式障碍和一个二次范数障碍。

Result: 对每个素幂 $q$ 及与 6 互素的 $r\\geq5$，得到最大 $2$-scattered 子空间；其对应的 rank-metric 码为 $[2r,r,4]_{q^6/q}$ MRD 码，且等价于其对偶码。取 $r=5,7$ 并与标准三维构造作直和，可得 $V(r,q^6)$ 中每个 $q$ 及每个 $r\\geq3$（除 $r=4$）的最大 $2$-scattered 子空间；当 $q$ 为 2 的奇次幂时，已知的四维构造也覆盖 $r=4$ 这一遗留情形。

Conclusion: 该工作几乎完整解决了 $V(r,q^6)$ 中最大 $2$-scattered 子空间的存在性问题：除 $r=4$ 需依赖 $q$ 为 2 的奇次幂的已知构造外，其余 $r\\geq3$ 均已覆盖，并给出了相应 MRD 码的自对偶等价性质。

Abstract: For every prime power $q$ and every integer $r\geq5$ coprime to $6$, we construct a maximum $2$-scattered $\mathbb{F}_q$-subspace of $V(r,q^6)$. The construction is a two-dimensional $\mathbb{F}_{q^r}$-subspace of $\mathbb{F}_{q^{6r}}$, viewed as an $r$-dimensional vector space over $\mathbb{F}_{q^6}$. A trace argument reduces the proof to the complementarity of two $\mathbb{F}_{q^r}$-subspaces. We establish this complementarity by separating two cases, which lead to a cubic polynomial obstruction and a quadratic norm obstruction. The associated rank-metric code is a $[2r,r,4]_{q^6/q}$ MRD code equivalent to its dual. Taking the cases $r=5,7$ and direct sums with the standard dimension-three construction gives maximum $2$-scattered subspaces of $V(r,q^6)$ for every $q$ and every $r\geq3$ except $r=4$. When $q$ is an odd power of $2$, the known dimension-four construction also covers this remaining case.

</details>


### [91] [Perfect state transfer on mixed graphs: complete classes and transfer times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08333)
*Xingkun Song*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文对无权重混合图上完美态传输（PST）的归一化传输时间进行分类，刻画了完全PST类的传输时间集合，并研究了有向实现、返回相位及传输分区结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完美态传输的传输时间结构此前缺乏对完全PST类归一化传输时间的系统分类，尤其是非平稳周期顶点上可行时间集合的代数刻画，以及这些集合能否由连通有向图实现等问题尚未解决。

Method: 证明结合虚二次域限制与一种无权重构造方法，用于选取完全目标集；通过余弦值的有理性和返回相位分析对传输时间集合进行分类，并构造满足条件的连通有向实现。

Result: 含零的有限集合Λ出现在非平稳周期顶点上当且仅当对所有x,y∈Λ有cos(2π(x−y))∈Q；每个可行集合均有连通有向实现。刻画了最小顶点周期处有向实现的返回相位；有理倍数传输将完全类划分为大小至多6（有向图返回相位−1时至多3）的集合，且界是紧的。构造了任意有限大小的完全类，包括所有异顶点传输发生在周期无理倍数且无切换自同构的类，并刻画了连通有向图中带指定相对最小顶点周期和返回相位的同步实现。

Conclusion: 完全PST类的传输时间集合可由余弦有理性条件完整刻画，并可通过连通有向图实现；有理传输倍数导致大小受限的划分且界最优，同时可实现任意有限大小及无理传输时间的完全类。

Abstract: For perfect state transfer (PST) on unweighted mixed graphs, we classify the normalized transfer times of complete PST classes. A finite set $Λ\subset\mathbb R/\mathbb Z$ containing zero occurs at a nonstationary periodic vertex if and only if $\cos(2π(x-y))\in\mathbb Q$ for all $x,y\inΛ$. Every admissible set has a connected oriented realization. We also classify the possible return phases of oriented realizations at the minimum vertex period. Transfers at rational multiples of the common minimum vertex period partition a complete class into sets of size at most six, or at most three in an oriented graph with return phase $-1$; both bounds are sharp. We construct complete classes of every finite size, including classes in which all transfers between distinct vertices occur at irrational multiples of the period and no switching automorphism maps a class vertex to a distinct class vertex. We also characterize simultaneous realization in connected oriented graphs with prescribed relative minimum vertex periods and return phases. The proof combines an imaginary quadratic field restriction with an unweighted construction that selects the complete target set.

</details>


### [92] [Optimal Bounds on Spanning Tree Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08362)
*Csongor Beke,Vladimir Bošković,Nina Kamčev,Yiting Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了将任意n顶点树T映入n顶点、最大度为d的图G的标号嵌入数的上界为(d/e)^n·exp(o_d(1)n)，该界对路径也是紧的，并由此得到了d-正则图中均匀随机生成树同构类的最优反集中不等式，证实了H. Lee的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究树在受限最大度图中的嵌入数量，并解决H. Lee关于随机生成树同构类反集中性质的猜想。

Method: 结合Brégman不等式与熵方法进行证明。

Result: 证明了inj(T,G) ≤ (d/e)^n·exp(o_d(1)n)；该界对路径情形也是紧的，且误差项对d的依赖是必要的；作为推论得到了d-正则图均匀随机生成树同构类的最优反集中界。

Conclusion: 给出了树嵌入数的sharp上界并证实了H. Lee的猜想，同时确立了误差项对d依赖的必要性。

Abstract: We prove that the number of labelled embeddings of any $n$-vertex tree $T$ into an $n$-vertex graph $G$ of maximum degree $d$ satisfies $$ \mathrm{inj}(T,G) \leq (d/e)^n \exp(o_d(1) n). $$ The bound is sharp up to determining $o_d(1)$, even for paths, and the dependence of the error $\exp(o_d(1)n)$ on $d$ is necessary. As an immediate corollary, we obtain an optimal anticoncentration bound for the isomorphism class of a uniformly random spanning tree in a connected $d$-regular graph, answering a conjecture of H. Lee. The proof combines Brégman's inequality with entropy methods.

</details>


### [93] [Regular $K_3$-Irregular Graphs of Every Regularity at Least Nine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08478)
*Zhanhe Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了：对任意正则度 r≥9，都存在正则的三角形唯一（K3-irregular）图，从而完全解决了 Chartrand–Erdős–Oellermann 提出的正则 K3-irregular 图存在性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chartrand、Erdős 和 Oellermann 曾提出是否存在正则的 K3-irregular（三角形唯一）图这一公开问题，此前仅有零散的小正则度例证，缺乏对一般正则度的系统性构造。

Method: 构造基于两个反正则阈值块，通过具有指定行和（margins）的 0-1 矩阵相连，并用保持该行和的矩阵 2-交换进行调整；经统一的参考扰动后仅剩三处三角形度碰撞，再按奇偶选择交换消除其中两处，最后一处可能碰撞由短二次判别式论证控制。奇 k≥17 与偶 k≥62 给出符号化处理，其余 24 个值用精确有限验证解决。

Result: 对每个整数 k≥15，构造出 4k+2 个顶点上的 2k-正则三角形唯一图；取补图得到同阶的 (2k+1)-正则例子。结合已知的 9≤r≤29 的例证，对所有 r≥9 均存在正则 K3-irregular 图。

Conclusion: 该工作关闭了正则 K3-irregular 图存在性问题的正方向，证明 r≥9 时正则三角形唯一图存在，结合已有结果给出了完整的 r≥9 存在性结论。

Abstract: For a vertex $v$ of a graph $G$, the triangle-degree $\operatorname{td}_G(v)$ is the number of triangles containing $v$. A graph is triangle-distinct, or $K_3$-irregular, if its vertex triangle-degrees are pairwise distinct. Chartrand, Erdős, and Oellermann asked whether a regular $K_3$-irregular graph exists. We prove that such graphs exist for every regularity $r\ge 9$. More precisely, for every integer $k\ge 15$ we construct a $2k$-regular triangle-distinct graph on $4k+2$ vertices; complementation gives a $(2k+1)$-regular example of the same order. The construction uses two antiregular threshold blocks joined by a zero-one matrix with prescribed margins, followed by matrix $2$-switches that preserve those margins. After a uniform reference perturbation, exactly three triangle-degree collisions remain. Switches chosen according to parity remove two of them, and the last possible collision is controlled by a short quadratic discriminant argument. Odd $k\ge 17$ and even $k\ge 62$ are handled symbolically, while the remaining 24 values are settled by exact finite verification. Together with the known examples for $9\le r\le 29$, this closes the positive existence problem for every $r\ge 9$.

</details>


### [94] [A proof of the quartic Berkovich-Dhar sign-change conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08496)
*Shutao Jiang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了 $P_n(q)^4$ 中 $[q^{3m+2}]$ 系数（去零项后）恰有一次由正变号的变号，结合第二 Borwein 定理验证了 Berkovich–Dhar 猜想四次情形，并给出了变号位置的渐近区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Berkovich–Dhar 猜想中四次情形（quartic case）的符号断言，即 $P_n(q)^4$ 相关系数的符号变化规律。

Method: 对 $P_n(q)=\prod_{j=1}^n(1-q^{3j-2})(1-q^{3j-1})$，分析两个共轭鞍点贡献之间的抵消；用鞍点关系的一阶修正控制远离转变区的系数，用高阶展开处理转变区附近的抵消；并在重标度变量下给出对低次范围一致成立的估计。

Result: 证明 $[q^{3m+2}]P_n(q)^4$ 去零项后恰有一次由正到负的变号；对 $n\ge301$，转变区间长度为 $3000/n^2$，中心为 $\alpha n^2+\beta n+\gamma+\delta/n$，其中 $\alpha=0.7490800947885107\ldots$，四个常数均有显式解析定义。

Conclusion: 结合第二 Borwein 定理，确立了 Berkovich–Dhar 猜想四次情形的符号断言，并精确定位了变号区间。

Abstract: Let $P_n(q)=\prod_{j=1}^n(1-q^{3j-2})(1-q^{3j-1})$. We prove that, after zero terms are omitted, the coefficients $[q^{3m+2}]P_n(q)^4$ change sign exactly once, from positive to negative. Together with the Second Borwein Theorem, this establishes the sign assertions in the quartic case of the Berkovich-Dhar conjecture. For $n\ge 301$, the transition lies in an interval of length $3000/n^2$ centred at $αn^2+βn+γ+δ/n$, where $α=0.7490800947885107\ldots$ and the four constants have explicit analytic definitions. The proof analyses the cancellation between two conjugate saddle contributions. A correction to the saddle relation controls the coefficients away from the transition, while a higher-order expansion resolves the cancellation near it. Uniform estimates in a rescaled variable cover the small-degree range.

</details>


### [95] [Character expansions and affine Jacobi-Trudi identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08500)
*Ilse Fischer,Moritz Gangl,Álvaro Gutiérrez,Nishu Kumari*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出了 $\mathrm{B}_n$、$\mathrm{C}_n$、$\mathrm{BC}_n$ 型多参数 Hall–Littlewood 多项式在矩形形状下某些特殊化的特征标展开，并用这些展开证明了 Ole Warnaar 于 2025 年提出的六个 Jacobi–Trudi 型恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ole Warnaar 在 2025 年提出了六个关于 $\mathrm{B}_n$、$\mathrm{C}_n$、$\mathrm{BC}_n$ 型多参数 Hall–Littlewood 多项式的 Jacobi–Trudi 型恒等式猜想，这些恒等式尚缺乏证明，本文旨在解决这一公开猜想。

Method: 针对矩形形状，对 $\mathrm{B}_n$、$\mathrm{C}_n$、$\mathrm{BC}_n$ 型多参数 Hall–Littlewood 多项式的某些特殊化进行特征标展开（character expansion），并以此为基础推导相应的 Jacobi–Trudi 型恒等式。

Result: 通过矩形形状下的特征标展开，成功建立了 Warnaar 所猜想的全部六个 Jacobi–Trudi 型恒等式，从而验证了这些猜想的正确性。

Conclusion: 矩形形状下多参数 Hall–Littlewood 多项式的特征标展开为证明 $B/C/BC$ 型 Jacobi–Trudi 恒等式提供了有效工具，解决了 Warnaar 2025 年提出的公开猜想，并暗示该展开方法可能推广至更一般的形状与参数。

Abstract: We provide character expansions of certain specialisations of multiparameter Hall--Littlewood polynomials of types $\mathrm{B}_n$, $\mathrm{C}_n$, and $\mathrm{BC}_n$ for rectangular shapes. We use these expansions to establish the six Jacobi-Trudi-type identities conjectured by Ole Warnaar in 2025.

</details>

### [96] [Densities of Picard Rank Jumps for K3 Surfaces Over Global Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06888)
*Robin Huang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了定义在特征0或p≥3的整体域K上的代数K3曲面X（在K为整体函数域时假设为ordinary）的精确公式：用X所附的Galois表示来刻画使约化X_p的几何Neron-Severi群具有给定秩的素数密度。并进一步证明存在无穷多个约化，其几何Neron-Severi群含有表示固定平方类（相对于交配对）的元素，并给出此类位置的密度结果；特别地，对足够一般的模点，满足该条件的位置集密度为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究K3曲面约化后几何Neron-Severi群秩的变化及其与Galois表示的关系，是算术几何中理解曲面族算术性质的核心问题。固定平方类的存在性则涉及交配对下的局部/整体不变量与模空间一般性之间的联系。作者希望把秩密度问题从公式化推广到更精细的平方类条件，并给出相应的密度刻画。

Method: 利用K3曲面的Galois表示（在ordinary情形下处理整体函数域，以保证相关群作用与约化理论适用）来计算约化X_p的几何Neron-Severi群秩的分布；通过将秩条件翻译为Galois表示中特定不变子空间或单值群作用的组合条件，导出素数密度公式；在平方类问题上，结合交配对给出的二次形式结构，分析几何Neron-Severi群中表示固定平方类的元素存在性与模空间的一般性条件。
