Result: 得到使几何Neron-Severi群具有给定秩的素数密度的精确公式，该公式以X附带的Galois表示为表达；证明存在无穷多个约化，其几何Neron-Severi群含有表示固定平方类的元素，并给出该现象发生位置的密度结果；特别地，当X对应足够一般的模点时，满足该条件的位置密度为零。

Conclusion: 该工作将K3曲面约化的几何Neron-Severi群秩统计与Galois表示精确联系起来，并推广到交配对下固定平方类的存在性统计。模空间一般点处密度为零的结果表明该现象在一般情形下是退化的/稀有的，从而为理解K3曲面族的算术与几何不变量分布提供了定量框架。

Abstract: Let $X$ be an algebraic K3 surface defined over a global field $K$ of characteristic $0$ or $p \geq 3$, assumed ordinary in the case where $K$ is a global function field. We provide an exact formula for the density of primes $p$ of $K$ at which the geometric Neron-Severi group of the reduction $X_p$ has a given rank in terms of the Galois representation attached to $X$. As an extension, we prove that there are infinitely many reductions of $X$ with an element of the geometric Neron-Severi group representing a fixed square class under the intersection pairing and prove results about the density of such places. In particular, we show that if $X$ corresponds to a sufficiently generic moduli point, a density zero set of places of $K$ satisfy this condition.

</details>


### [97] [CUDA-MPQS: A GPU-Resident Self-Initializing Quadratic Sieve, and the Factorization of RSA-155](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07126)
*Fabian Januszewski,Christoph Heinrichs*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出全GPU流水线化的自初始化二次筛（SIQS）实现，首次将多项式初始化、筛法、关系后处理、大素数匹配、GF(2)矩阵构造、Block Wiedemann线性代数与平方根全部置于GPU执行，端到端分解512位RSA-155模数，创下二次筛最大分解记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次筛（QS）是不规则、缓存不友好且分支密集的整数分解算法，此前GPU工作仅加速其中个别阶段。作者旨在实现全流程GPU化，突破QS的规模上限并验证GPU相对CPU的能效与性能优势。

Method: 自初始化二次筛（SIQS）全GPU实现：所有阶段均在GPU执行，CPU仅负责编排、初始化与I/O；筛窗99.9%时间GPU忙碌，无设备级同步、事件同步或同步拷贝。采用64×H100筛 + 单H100做GF(2)求解完成RSA-155端到端分解。

Result: 端到端分解512位RSA-155，耗时700.6 GPU-h、242 kWh（98.4%用于筛法），超越此前QS记录RSA-150。单卡H100上RSA-100用时29.2 s，消费级RTX 5070 Ti用时51.0 s；同模数对照中H100快于96核EPYC 9655上的YAFU（106–122 s）、CADO-NFS的SIQS（292 s）与GNFS（263 s）。跨四款GPU的RSA-100吞吐近似与CUDA核心数成正比，内存带宽预测力较弱；七次基准分解成本随规模增长为LN(1/2,1)^1.04（R²=0.993）。RSA-150上用302.9 GPU-h，对比原记录的11,664核心小时。

Conclusion: 全GPU自初始化二次筛可端到端高效分解512位模数，刷新QS最大分解记录，并在能效与墙钟时间上显著优于主流CPU实现（YAFU、CADO-NFS），表明GPU化QS在大整数分解上具有可行性与竞争力。

Abstract: The quadratic sieve (QS) is an irregular, cache-hostile, branch-heavy integer factorization algorithm; prior GPU work accelerates individual stages of it. We present \swname{}, a self-initializing quadratic sieve in which \emph{every} stage --- polynomial initialization, sieving, relation post-processing, large-prime matching, \GFtwo{} matrix construction, Block Wiedemann linear algebra and the square root --- executes on the GPU, the CPU restricted to orchestration, setup and I/O, the sieve window 99.9\% GPU-busy with no device-wide or event synchronization and no synchronous copy. With it we factored the 512-bit RSA-155 challenge modulus end to end on GPUs: a 64$\times$H100 sieve feeding a single-H100 \GFtwo{} solve, 700.6~GPU-h and 242~kWh in total, 98.4\% of it sieving. To our knowledge this is the largest integer factored by the quadratic sieve, exceeding the prior QS record RSA-150 (Buhrow, YAFU, 2025); all comparable general factorizations since 1996, RSA-155's own in 1999 included, used the number field sieve. On a single device we factor RSA-100 in 29.2~s on an H100 and 51.0~s on a consumer RTX~5070~Ti; in a controlled same-modulus measurement, with energy measured on both sides, the H100 is faster than the fastest CPU quadratic sieve, YAFU, on 96 cores of an EPYC~9655 (106--122~s), and than CADO-NFS's SIQS (292~s) and GNFS (263~s). Across four discrete GPUs (a 3.7$\times$ range in CUDA cores) RSA-100 throughput is close to proportional to core count, memory bandwidth being a weaker predictor, and measured cost over seven benchmark factorizations from RSA-100 to RSA-155 grows as $\LN{1/2}{1}^{1.04}$ ($R^2=0.993$). On RSA-150, the identical modulus on which the prior QS record was set, we measure 302.9~GPU-h against that run's 11{,}664 core-hours.

</details>


### [98] [On the class number of real multiquadratic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07180)
*Mohamed Mahmoud Chems-Eddin,Hamza El Mamry*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了2-类数为2的实双二次域，并给出了所有奇类数实多二次域的完整列表；附录还提供了实二次域基本单位平方根的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conner与Hurrelbrink（1988）已分类奇类数的实二次域与双二次域，并研究了2-类数为2的实二次域；结合Kučera的结果，实二次域2-类数为2的情形已获完整分类。本文旨在将此类分类推广至实双二次域，并进一步刻画实多二次域的奇类数情形。

Method: 在Conner–Hurrelbrink与Kučera既有分类结果的基础上，对实双二次域进行2-类数分析，给出2-类数为2的完整分类；进而对实多二次域进行奇类数的完整枚举；附录中推导实二次域基本单位ε_d的平方根√ε_d的显式公式。

Result: 得到2-类数为2的全部实双二次域的完整分类；给出所有奇类数实多二次域的完整列表；并在附录中给出√ε_d的显式公式。

Conclusion: 本文完善了实双二次域与实多二次域在2-类数与奇类数方面的分类，补充了基本单位平方根的显式表达，推广了Conner–Hurrelbrink与Kučera的经典结果。

Abstract: In their 1988 monograph, Conner and Hurrelbrink classified all real quadratic and biquadratic fields with odd class number. They also investigated real quadratic fields whose $2$-class numbers are equal to $2$. Their results, together with some well-known results of Ku{č}era, provide a complete classification of real quadratic number fields with $2$-class number $2$.
  The main aim of this paper is to provide a complete classification of all real biquadratic fields whose $2$-class numbers equal $2$; and moreover, we draw the complete list all real multiquadratic fields with odd class numbers. Finally, as an appendix we give explicit formulas for $\sqrt{\varepsilon_d}$, where $\varepsilon_d$ denotes the fundamental unit of the real quadratic field $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{d})$.

</details>


### [99] [The One-Power Erdős Conjecture for Primes $p \equiv 7 \pmod{36}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07214)
*Shen Harman,Dana Paquin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究Erdős关于奇数可表示为2^a加无平方因子数的猜想，针对素数 p ≡ 7 (mod 36)。通过分离原理将四次型 x(6x-1)(2x-1)(6x-7) 的无平方因子问题化为独立线性问题，无条件证明 a ≤ 3 可表示素数类的相对密度为 1 - ρ_4（ρ_4 ≈ 1.8637×10^{-5}），从而该类中有无穷多素数不可表示；并计算验证 10^8 以下所有该类素数，a ≤ 5，列出11个需 a = 5 的素数，附录修正Hercher一引理的误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős猜想断言每个大于1的奇数n都可写成 n = 2^a + m，其中a ≥ 0为整数，m为正的无平方因子数。论文研究该猜想在素数 p ≡ 7 (mod 36) 类上的情形，希望通过控制素因子2和3并利用分离原理，定量刻画该类素数可表示的比例，从而获得无条件结论。

Method: 令 x = (p-1)/6，候选余数形如 p-1 = 6x、p-2 = 6x-1、p-4 = 3(2x-1)、p-8 = 6x-7，其中2和3被完全控制，无平方因子的障碍均来自素数 q ≥ 5。对这类q建立分离原理：因两余数之差至多7 < 25，故 q^2 至多整除其中一个余数，且在宽度小于20的指数窗口上保持。由此将四次型 x(6x-1)(2x-1)(6x-7) 的平方因子问题分解为独立线性问题，并推导相对密度常数 ρ_4。

Result: 无条件推出 p ≡ 7 (mod 36) 中满足 a ≤ 3 可表示的素数具有相对密度 1 - ρ_4，其中 ρ_4 = 1.8637 × 10^{-5} 为显式Euler型常数；特别地，该类中有无穷多素数不能用 a ≤ 3 表示。计算方面，验证了 10^8 以下所有该类素数满足猜想，且总有 a ≤ 5，并给出了11个需要 a = 5 的素数。附录修正了Hercher某引理中的误差界，其结论不变。

Conclusion: 对 p ≡ 7 (mod 36) 的素数，a ≤ 3 的可表示性由显式密度 1 - ρ_4 刻画，存在无穷多不可如此表示的素数，逼近了Erdős猜想的一个子类；数值验证支持猜想且所需指数不超过5。附录的修正不影响Hercher的结论。

Abstract: A conjecture of Erdős asserts that every odd integer $n > 1$ can be written as $n = 2^a + m$, where $a \ge 0$ is an integer and $m$ is a squarefree positive integer. We study this conjecture for primes $p \equiv 7 \pmod{36}$. Writing $x = (p-1)/6$, the candidate remainders take the shapes $p - 1 = 6x$, $p-2 = 6x-1$, $p-4 = 3(2x-1)$, and $p-8 = 6x-7$, in which the primes $2$ and $3$ are completely controlled: every obstruction to squarefreeness comes from a prime $q \ge 5$. For such $q$ a separation principle applies --- $q^2$ divides at most one of the remainders, since two of them differ by at most $7 < 25$ --- and it persists across any window of exponents of width less than $20$. Separation collapses the square-divisor problem for the quartic $x(6x-1)(2x-1)(6x-7)$ into independent linear problems, and we deduce unconditionally that the primes of the class representable with $a \le 3$ have relative density $1 - ρ_4$, where $ρ_4 = 1.8637 \times 10^{-5}$ is an explicit Euler-type constant; in particular, infinitely many primes of the class are not so representable. Computationally, we verify the conjecture for all primes of the class below $10^8$, always with $a \le 5$, and we exhibit the eleven primes below $10^8$ requiring $a = 5$. An appendix corrects the error bound in a lemma of Hercher, whose conclusions are unaffected.

</details>


### [100] [Systems of equations with elements of finitely generated groups and with linear recurrence sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07227)
*Subham Bhakta,Alina Ostafe,Igor E. Shparlinski*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对定义在有限生成群有限子集上的对角多项式方程组解数给出新的本质最优上界，推广并改进了已有结果，并应用于线性递推序列情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于对角方程解数上界的结果主要针对单方程（k=1，Bourgain 等 2014）和两方程（k=2，Manning 等 2026），缺乏对任意 k 的一般性本质最优界；同时这些界可用于算术统计问题。

Method: 研究特征零域上有限生成群有限子集中 s 个变量的 k 个对角多项式方程组，建立解数的新上界；并将方法推广至 s 元线性递推序列的线性方程与二次方程情形。

Result: 得到本质最优的解数上界，推广并部分改进了 k=1 和 k=2 的已有结果；对线性递推序列在线性与二次方程情形获得类似结果；并据此改进了 Manning、Ostafe 与 Shparlinski (2026) 关于某些矩阵算术统计的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并强化了对角方程解数估计，改进的界进一步提升了相关矩阵算术统计结论的精度。

Abstract: We obtain new and essentially optimal bounds on the number of solutions of diagonal systems of $k$ polynomial equations in $s$ variables from an arbitrary finite subset of a finitely generated group in a field of characteristic zero. Our bounds generalise and sometimes improve previous results of J. Bourgain, M. Z. Garaev, S. V. Konyagin, and I. E. Shparlinski (2014), for $k=1$, and of A. Manning, A. Ostafe and I. E. Shparlinski (2026), for $k=2$. In turn, this improvement leads to sharpening a result of A. Manning, A. Ostafe and I. E. Shparlinski (2026) concerning arithmetic statistics of certain matrices.
  We also obtain similar results for $s$-tuples of linear recurrence sequences, in the case of linear equations and quadratic equations.

</details>


### [101] [On eutactic forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07375)
*Nikolay Moshchevitin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了关于 n 元正定共晶型（eutactic form）类数的两个新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该摘要信息极为简略，未明确交代研究动机，可推测其动机在于深化对正定共晶型类数这一数论/二次型理论中经典计数问题的理解。

Method: 摘要未给出方法细节，仅说明以证明的形式得到两个新结果，可能涉及二次型理论、格论或相关代数工具。

Result: 得到关于 n 元正定共晶型类数的两个新结论（摘要未列出具体结论）。

Conclusion: 在 n 元正定共晶型类数问题上取得两项新进展，具体内容需参见正文。

Abstract: We prove two new results about the number of classes of positive definite eutactic forms in n variables.

</details>


### [102] [Lifting ethereal Bianchi modular forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07445)
*Lewis Combes,Håvard Damm-Johnsen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究模 p Bianchi 模型式的提升性问题，证明在给定域上、某些小素数情形下总能通过提升水平实现特征零提升，并提供高效算法与数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模 p Hecke 特征形式若不能提升到特征零，被称为 ethereal 模型式，已获广泛关注；作者希望将这一研究从经典同余子群推广到 Bianchi 模型式情形。

Method: 发展上同调中退化映射的高效算法，用于严格验证特征值同余；并结合 Caraiani-Newton 的模性定理，对某些小素数及特定数域证明提升的存在性。

Result: 对给定的小素数与特定域，证明提升总是存在；Magma 实现结合 LMFDB 数据表明，在有利参数范围内，绝大多数模 p Bianchi 模型式可以提升。

Conclusion: 计算证据支持：如同经典模型式情形，通过提高水平，模 p Bianchi 模型式总可能提升到特征零。

Abstract: Mod $p$ Hecke eigenforms for congruence subgroups of $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ which do not lift to characteristic $0$ have received much attention, and were named ethereal modular forms by G. Schaeffer. In this paper, we study liftability for mod $p$ Bianchi modular forms: computations suggest that a lift to characteristic zero may always be possible by increasing the level, as in the case of classical modular forms.
  We develop efficient algorithms for computing degeneracy maps in cohomology, which allow us to rigorously verify eigenvalue congruences. For small primes and certain fields, we also prove that lifts always exist as a consequence of the recent modularity theorem due to Caraiani-Newton. A Magma implementation, complemented by LMFDB data due to Cremona, shows that in favourable parameter ranges, most mod $p$ Bianchi modular forms lift.

</details>


### [103] [Gaps theorems for linear forms with cubic coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07471)
*Alan Haynes*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究三次数域中由基生成的二维线性型的小数部分间隙数 $G(\mathbf{\alpha},\mathbf{\tau})$，证明其随 $\mathbf{\tau}$ 变化无界，并在存在复嵌入情形下给出显式下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨数域背景下二维序列小数部分间隙数量的增长行为，特别是其是否随参数 $\mathbf{\tau}$ 无界，从而联系到轨道密度与格空间上的对角线群作用等数论与动力系统问题。

Method: 设 $1,\alpha_1,\alpha_2$ 为三次数域 $K\subset\mathbb{R}$ 的一组基，考察 $m_1\alpha_1+m_2\alpha_2$ 的小数部分的不同间隙数 $G(\mathbf{\alpha},\mathbf{\tau})$，其中 $0\le m_i<\tau_i$。当 $K$ 全实，借助 Shapira 关于格空间上对角群轨道的一个密度定理推导无界性；当 $K$ 有复嵌入时，采用显式构造方法。

Result: 证明 $G(\mathbf{\alpha},\mathbf{\tau})$ 随 $\mathbf{\tau}$ 变化是无界的。在 $K$ 全实情形由 Shapira 的密度定理推出该结论；在 $K$ 有复嵌入情形，通过显式构造不仅得到无界性，还证明存在常数 $c,\kappa>0$，使得对无穷多个 $\mathbf{\tau}\in\mathbb{N}^2$ 有 $G(\mathbf{\alpha},\mathbf{\tau})\ge c(\log\max\{\tau_1,\tau_2\})^\kappa$。

Conclusion: 三次数域基生成的二维小数部分间隙数具有无界增长性，且复嵌入情形下可给出对数幂次型下界，为相关数论分布问题提供了定量结果。

Abstract: Let $1,α_1,α_2$ be a basis for a cubic number field $K\subset\mathbb{R}$, and for $τ_1,τ_2\ge 1$ let $G(\mathbfα,\mathbfτ)$ denote the number of distinct gaps between the fractional parts of the numbers $m_1α_1+m_2α_2$, with $m_1,m_2\in\mathbb{Z}$, $0\le m_1<τ_1$ and $0\le m_2<τ_2$. We show that $G(\mathbfα,\mathbfτ)$ is unbounded as $\mathbfτ$ varies. When $K$ is totally real this is deduced from a density theorem of Shapira for orbits of the diagonal group on the space of lattices. When $K$ has a complex embedding we give an explicit construction, which also shows that there are constants $c,κ>0$ for which $G(\mathbfα,\mathbfτ)\ge c(\log\max\{τ_1,τ_2\})^κ$ for infinitely many $\mathbfτ\in\mathbb{N}^2$.

</details>


### [104] [Further results on the paramodular Hecke algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07520)
*Jennifer Johnson-Leung,Joshua Parker,Brooks Roberts*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究分级副模Hecke代数 $\mathcal{H}$ 的结构，通过固定某个自同构 $\alpha$ 定义交换子环 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$，并证明由 $T(q^k)$ 生成的生成函数是关于 $t$ 的有理函数，同时给出 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$ 的结构及其与 $\mathcal{H}$ 的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在进一步刻画分级副模Hecke代数的结构，通过引入自同构不变子环来研究其交换子结构及生成函数的有理性，从而揭示 $\mathcal{H}$ 的内部结构与对称性。

Method: 定义自同构 $\alpha$ 固定的交换子环 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$；证明元素 $T(q^k)$（次数为 $k$ 的双陪集之和）属于 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$；利用生成函数方法研究以 $T(q^k)$ 为系数的形式幂级数，证明其为以 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$ 为系数的显式有理函数；并进一步确定 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$ 的结构及其与 $\mathcal{H}$ 的关系。

Result: 证明了 $T(q^k) \in \mathcal{H}^\alpha$；证明了由 $T(q^k)$ 生成的形式幂级数 $\sum_k T(q^k) t^k$ 是关于 $t$ 的显式有理函数，其系数取自 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$；同时确定了 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$ 的结构及其与 $\mathcal{H}$ 的关系。

Conclusion: 分级副模Hecke代数中存在由自同构 $\alpha$ 固定的交换子环 $\mathcal{H}^\alpha$，其包含所有次数双陪集之和 $T(q^k)$，且对应生成函数为显式有理函数；$\mathcal{H}^\alpha$ 的结构及其与 $\mathcal{H}$ 的关系得到了明确刻画。

Abstract: In this paper we further examine the structure of the graded paramodular Hecke algebra $\mathcal H$. We define a commutative subring $\mathcal{H}^α$ of $\mathcal{H}$ as the elements fixed by a certain automorphism $α$, and we show that the elements $T(q^k)$, defined as the sum of the double cosets of degree $k$, are contained in $\mathcal{H}^α$. We prove that the formal power series in the indeterminate $t$ with coefficients $T(q^k)$ is an explicit rational function in $t$ with coefficients from $\mathcal{H}^α$. We also determine the structure of $\mathcal{H}^α$ and its relation to $\mathcal{H}$.

</details>


### [105] [Primitive normal progressions in finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07538)
*Gustav Kjærbye Bagger,Matthew Fernando*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究有限域 $\mathbb{F}_{q^n}$ 中长度 $m$ 的等差数列，是否可使其各项均为本原元且至少一项为正规元；结合筛法估计、分歧界与计算论证，显著改进 $m=4$ 的已有结果，给出 $5\le m\le 10$ 的部分结果，并在 $m\ge 53$ 时给出超越前人的一般界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限域中同时具有本原性与正规性的元素（本原正规元）在编码理论、密码学及有限域构造中有重要应用。将该问题推广到等差数列情形，可刻画本原正规元在算术结构中的分布规律。已有文献对短长度等差数列的覆盖范围有限，尤其在 $m=4$ 及中等长度区间结果较弱，故有必要改进相关存在性判定。

Method: 方法上综合三类工具：(1) 筛法估计，用于对本原元与正规元条件同时计数；(2) 分歧界（ramified bounds），处理特征和与素理想分歧带来的误差项；(3) 计算论证，用于验证小参数或边界情形。通过上述组合降低对 $q^n$ 的规模要求，从而扩展可处理的长短区间。

Result: 对长度 $m=4$，所得存在性条件显著优于既有文献；对 $5\leq m\leq 10$，得到部分情形下的结论；当 $m\geq 53$ 时，给出的一般界优于此前所有已知结果。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了有限域中等差数列内本原正规元存在性问题的研究：短长度（$m=4$）条件大幅改进，中等长度（$5\le m\le 10$）取得部分进展，长长度（$m\ge 53$）给出更优一般界，但 $m$ 在 11 至 52 之间的区间仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Consider an arithmetic progression inside the finite field $\mathbb{F}_{q^n}$. We study the existence of progressions whose terms are all primitive and at least one of them is normal. Using a combination of sieve estimates, ramified bounds, and computational arguments, we improve significantly upon previous literature for progressions of length $m=4$ and obtain partial results whenever $5\leq m\leq 10$. Finally, we provide a general bound which outperforms previous results in the range $m\geq 53$.

</details>


### [106] [A new bound for the Furstenberg--Sárközy theorem using the van der Corput property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07765)
*Steve Fan,Andrew Lott*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了无平方差的集合大小的上界改进：|A| ≪ N exp(-c√(log N log log N))，并给出匹配下界说明该界在常数意义下最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Green 和 Sawhney 近期给出了无平方差集合的上界结果，本文旨在改进这一上界，并从定量 van der Corput 角度揭示其本质与最优性。

Method: 利用带符号系数的 van der Corput 性质的定量版本，并基于 Slijepčević、Slijepčević–Ninčević 以及 Fan–Lott 的构造。上界证明是初等且自包含的；同时对平方数的 van der Corput 见证的常系数给出匹配下界。

Result: 得到改进上界 |A| ≪ N exp(-c√(log N log log N))；并证明对平方数任何 van der Corput 见证的常系数存在匹配下界，表明所得定量 van der Corput 界在常数 c 意义下已是最优。

Conclusion: 本文在无平方差集合这一问题上推广并加强了 Green–Sawhney 的结果，同时说明了所依赖的定量 van der Corput 方法的紧性。

Abstract: We show that if $A\subseteq \mathbb{N}\cap[1,N]$ has no nonzero square difference, then \[
  |A|\ll N\exp(-c\sqrt{\log N\log\log N}), \] improving upon a recent result of Green and Sawhney. The proof exploits a quantitative version of the van der Corput property with signed coefficients and builds on previous constructions of Slijepčević, Slijepčević--Ninčević, and Fan-Lott. The proof of the upper bound is elementary and self-contained. We also prove a matching lower bound for the constant coefficient of any van der Corput witness for squares, showing that our quantitative van der Corput bound is sharp up to the constant $c$.

</details>


### [107] [Carlet's cyclic-additive conjecture for the Kasami monomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07905)
*Gábor P. Nagy,Douglas S. McNeil,Attila Vajda*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文证明了Kasami函数的导数像集合满足Carlet引入的循环-加性差集条件，对任意允许的$(n,k)$均成立，并在Lean 4中完成机器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kasami函数是密码学中重要的幂函数，其非线性性质与差集结构密切相关。Carlet提出的循环-加性差集条件可刻画其Fourier谱性质，NSUCRYPTO 2019将其作为开放问题提出，此前对Kasami函数尚无证明。

Method: 以导数像$\Delta_k$的已知半尺寸性质为起点，将Fourier修正项表示为扭化根计数，通过对Fermat三次曲面的关联（incidence）论证证明其非负性，并利用关于斜率的精确平均导出逐点等式。

Result: 对所有互异非零$v_1,v_2\in K$，有$|\{(x,y,z)\in\Delta_k^3: v_1x+v_2y+(v_1+v_2)z=0\}|=2^{2n-3}$，且该结论覆盖所有允许的$(n,k)$对。

Conclusion: Kasami函数满足Carlet的循环-加性差集条件，解决了NSUCRYPTO 2019的开放问题；证明已在Lean 4与Mathlib中形式化并通过机器检验。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a finite field of characteristic two with $|K| = 2^{n}$, let $\gcd(k,n) = 1$, let $d_{k} = 4^{k} - 2^{k} + 1$ be the Kasami exponent, and let $Δ_{k} = \{(b+1)^{d_{k}} + b^{d_{k}} + 1 : b \in K\}$ be the image of the normalised derivative of the Kasami monomial in the direction $1$. We show that, for all distinct nonzero $v_{1},v_{2} \in K$, \[
  \bigl|\{(x,y,z) \in Δ_{k}^{3} :
  v_{1}x + v_{2}y + (v_{1}+v_{2})z = 0\}\bigr| = 2^{2n-3}. \] This establishes the cyclic-additive difference-set condition introduced by Carlet and later posed for the Kasami functions at NSUCRYPTO~2019. Starting from the known half-size property of the derivative image, we express the Fourier correction as twisted root counts and prove their required nonnegativity by an incidence argument on the Fermat cubic. An exact average over the slopes then forces equality pointwise. The argument covers every admissible pair $(n,k)$ and has been formalised and machine-checked in Lean~4 with Mathlib.

</details>


### [108] [A concise proof of the 8 case of Sylvester's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08015)
*Hongbo Yin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出Sylvester猜想中 $p\equiv 8 \mod 9$ 情形的更简洁直接的证明：通过深度挖掘作者此前工作并结合其对情形8的旧有思路，使用普通迹与模函数的Kronecker同余，避免了Gross-Zagier公式、$\sqrt{-3}$-division边界与Fermat同源等复杂工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sylvester猜想（150年前）断言每个满足 $p\equiv 4,7,8\mod 9$ 的素数可表示为两个有理立方数之和。情形4、7已由作者证明，情形8由Burungale-Tian证明，但其证明复杂难懂，且虽本质上使用作者结果却未充分利用，因此需要更简单直接的证明。

Method: 深度挖掘作者在情形4、7中的结果，并结合其此前关于情形8的工作（Yin8）中的思想；具体使用普通迹（ordinary trace）与模函数的Kronecker同余。

Result: 给出了情形 $p\equiv 8\mod 9$ 的更简洁、直接的证明，且不使用Gross-Zagier公式、$\sqrt{-3}$-division边界和Fermat同源。

Conclusion: 该简洁证明为这一古老而简洁的猜想画上圆满句号，完成了Sylvester猜想全部三种情形的证明。

Abstract: The 150 years old Sylvester's conjecture asserts that every prime $p\equiv 4,7,8\mod 9$ is the sum of two rational cubes. Recently, a proof of this old problem is obtained by the work of mine for the case 4,7 \cite{Yin} and the work of Burungale-Tian for the case 8 \cite{BT}. Burungale-Tian's paper essentially uses the results of paper \cite{Yin}, but does not make full use of the power. Their proof for the case $8$ is complicated and hard to follow. In this paper we give a much simpler and direct proof by deep mining my result in \cite{Yin} combining with the ideas of my previous work on case 8 in \cite{Yin8}. In particular, we use the ordinary trace and the Kronecker congruence of modular functions, but do not use the Gross-Zagier formula, $\sqrt{-3}$-division boundary and Fermat isogeny as in \cite{BT}.This concise proof provides a perfect end to this old concise conjecture.

</details>


### [109] [The Artin-Davenport conjecture on cubic forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08226)
*Dante Bonolis,Tim Browning,Jakob Glas,Victor Y. Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明任意十个及以上变量的整系数三次型都存在非平凡整数零点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究整系数三次型的整数零点存在性（即三次型的Waring问题/Hasse原则失效情形），旨在将已知结果的变量数下界进一步降低，确定"任意多个变量必有非平凡整数零点"的临界变量数。

Method: 沿用"几何方法处理部分情形、圆法处理其余情形"的框架。几何侧引入奇异轨迹维数与关联簇（incidence variety）的界；圆法侧引入分层几何筛（stratified geometric sieve），配合有限域上点计数的分层结果以及立方根Hensel提升分析。

Result: 证明任意十个或更多变量的整系数三次型必有非平凡整数零点。

Conclusion: 将三次型非平凡整数零点存在性的变量数门槛降至10，改进了既有结果，并给出了几何方法与解析数论方法两类新工具。

Abstract: We show that any integral cubic form in ten or more variables has a nontrivial integer zero. Like earlier authors on this topic, we use geometric methods to produce a zero in some cases, and the circle method in all other cases. New ingredients are required on both fronts: in the first case, bounds on the dimension of singular loci and incidence varieties, and in the second case, a stratified geometric sieve, together with stratification results for point counts over finite fields and a cube-root Hensel lifting analysis.

</details>


### [110] [The average number of representations of an integer as a sum of three or more primes, over multiples of a fixed integer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08285)
*Alessandra Migliaccio,Alessandro Zaccagnini*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将 Ikeda 与 Suriajaya（2025）的结果推广到将整数表示为任意 j≥3 个素数之和的平均表示数，证明了相应渐近公式在一般情形下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原结果仅针对特定（少数）素数个数情形，作者希望将其统一推广到任意 j≥3 的一般情形，以刻画广义 Goldbach 问题中表示数的平均渐近行为。

Method: 在证明中引入新的工具，如广义 Goldbach 问题奇异级数的平均值估计。

Result: 证明了任意 j≥3 个素数之和的平均表示数满足相应的渐近公式，即一般情形下的渐近公式成立。

Conclusion: 将 Ikeda–Suriajaya 的结果成功推广到一般情形，表明广义 Goldbach 问题中平均表示数的渐近公式具有普遍性。

Abstract: We extend a result by Ikeda and Suriajaya (2025) to the average number of representations of an integer as a sum of an arbitrary number $j\ge 3$ of primes. We show that the corresponding asymptotic formula holds in the general case. In the proof we use new ingredients like averages for the singular series of the generalized Goldbach problem.

</details>


### [111] [On Sárközy's Local Extrema Conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08424)
*Alexander P. Mangerel*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究正取值乘性函数序列中局部极值模式出现的频率，给出使其对数密度为0的所有乘性函数的精确分类，并据此强形式解决了Sárközy 2001年的两个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画乘性函数序列$(f(n))_n$中形如$f(n)>\omega\max\{f(n-1),f(n+1)\}$或$f(n)<\frac1\omega\min\{f(n-1),f(n+1)\}$的模式出现频率，并确定哪些乘性函数使这些模式的对数密度为0，从而统一解释局部极大/极小值有限的现象。

Method: 基于加性函数的“pretentiousness”二分法；使用作者先前建立的加性函数版本的Matomäki-Radziwiłł定理，以及关于加性函数间隔$g(n+1)-g(n)$集中性的新上界。

Result: 对所有正取值乘性函数进行了sharp分类：明确哪些函数使至少一种局部极值模式仅以对数密度0出现；并由此以强形式解决Sárközy 2001年关于$f:\mathbb{N}\to\mathbb{N}$具有有限个局部极大值或有限个局部极小值的两个猜想。

Conclusion: 乘性函数局部极值模式的稀有性可由加性函数的pretentiousness dichotomy完整刻画，所建立的新间隔集中性估计具有独立价值。

Abstract: Let $f: \mathbb{N} \rightarrow (0,\infty)$ be a multiplicative function, and let $ω> 0$. We consider the frequency with which patterns of the form $$ f(n) > ω\max\{f(n-1),f(n+1)\}, \quad f(n) < \frac{1}ω\min\{f(n-1),f(n+1)\} $$ occur in the sequence $(f(n))_n$, and give a sharp classification of all positive-valued multiplicative functions for which at least one of these patterns only occurs with logarithmic density $0$.
  As a particular application, we resolve, in a strong form, two conjectures of A. Sárközy from 2001, classifying all multiplicative functions $f: \mathbb{N} \rightarrow \mathbb{N}$ that have either finitely many local maxima, or finitely many local minima.
  The proof is based on a natural ``pretentiousness'' dichotomy for additive functions. Key tools in the analysis include an analogue of the Matomäki-Radziwiłłtheorem for additive functions, due to the author, as well as a new bound for the concentration of the gaps $g(n+1)-g(n)$ of an additive function $g$, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [112] [The least prime with a given primitive root and a variant of the larger sieve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08569)
*Daniel R. Johnston,Sunil Naik*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在广义黎曼假设下，几乎所有的整数 $g$（非 $-1$、非平方数）满足其最小原根模素数 $p_g \leq (\log|g|)^{3.44}$，例外仅 $O((\log N)^{2.44})$ 个；核心技术为 Gallagher 大筛法的新变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fan 与 Pollack 近期仅证明了一致上界 $p_g \leq (\log|g|)^{19}$，该界远非最优。作者希望在 GRH 假设下大幅改进指数，并刻画例外集规模，从而给出比统一上界更强、几乎对所有 $g$ 成立的精细估计。

Method: 证明 Gallagher 大筛法（larger sieve）的一个新变体，并将其应用于对满足条件的整数 $g$ 的计数；同时在 GRH 假设下对区间 $|g|\in[N,2N]$ 内整数 $g$ 进行例外个数的估计。另讨论若干可无条件成立的其他“almost all”型界。

Result: 在 GRH 假设下，对所有 $|g|\in[N,2N]$ 中非 $-1$、非平方的整数 $g$，除至多 $O((\log N)^{2.44})$ 个例外外，均有 $p_g \leq (\log|g|)^{3.44}$。该指数 3.44 显著优于 Fan–Pollack 的一致界指数 19。

Conclusion: 在 GRH 下，最小原根的上界可对几乎所有的 $g$ 改进至 $(\log|g|)^{3.44}$，例外数目为对数多项式量级；所用的 Gallagher 大筛法新变体具有独立价值，此外尚可获得若干无条件“almost all”结果。

Abstract: For an integer $g$ not equal to $-1$ or a square, we study the least prime $p_g$ such that $g$ is a primitive root modulo $p_g$. We show that, assuming the Generalised Riemann Hypothesis, all such $g$ with $|g|\in[N,2N]$ have $p_g\leq(\log |g|)^{3.44}$ with at most $O((\log N)^{2.44})$ exceptions. This can be directly compared to the uniform bound $p_g\leq(\log |g|)^{19}$ proven recently by Fan and Pollack. To obtain our result, we prove a new variant of Gallagher's larger sieve, which may be of independent interest. In addition to our conditional result, we discuss other ``almost all" bounds for $p_g$ that hold unconditionally.

</details>


### [113] [Local newforms, Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker functions, and explicit test vectors for $\mathrm{GL}(n)\times \mathrm{GL}(n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08633)
*Alexandros Groutides*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker函数与Whittaker新形式之间的逆平均公式，实现了两者之间的双向显式转换，并给出GL(n)×GL(n)尖点对的新显式检验向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Girsch-Kurinczuk近期获得了p进GL(n)在零深度与极小极大整深度尖点表示中新形式的公式，其核心结果是平均公式，用Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker函数给出Whittaker新形式的积分表示。本文旨在补充这一结果的反向方向，并应用于Rankin-Selberg L因子的显式检验向量构造。

Method: 首先通过建立Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker函数以Whittaker新形式表示的积分表示，证明一个逆平均公式；随后结合本文平均公式与Kurinczuk-Matringe的先前工作，构造GL(n)×GL(n)尖点对在Rankin-Selberg L因子不恒等于1时的显式检验向量。

Result: 证明了逆平均公式，与原有平均公式共同给出Whittaker新形式与Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker函数之间显式的双向转换；并得到以Whittaker新形式表示的GL(n)×GL(n)尖点对的新显式检验向量。

Conclusion: 本文完善了Girsch-Kurinczuk关于p进GL(n)新形式的理论，建立了两个重要向量的双向对应关系，并拓展了Rankin-Selberg L因子的显式检验向量结果。

Abstract: Recently, Girsch-Kurinczuk have obtained new formulas for local newforms in depth-zero and minimax integral-depth cuspidal representations of $p$-adic $\mathrm{GL}(n)$. One of their main results is an averaging formula which provides an integral representation of the Whittaker newform in terms of the Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker function. In the present paper, we first prove a reverse averaging formula by establishing an integral representation of the Paškūnas-Stevens Whittaker function in terms of the Whittaker newform. Together, these formulas give an explicit two-way passage between these two distinguished vectors. Finally, we use our averaging formula and earlier work of Kurinczuk-Matringe to give new explicit test vectors in terms of Whittaker newforms for such cuspidal $\mathrm{GL}(n)\times\mathrm{GL}(n)$ pairs, whenever their Rankin-Selberg $L$-factor is not identically equal to $1$.

</details>


### [114] [New integer sequence OEIS A392714 counts Wronskians: fast evaluation via late-growing permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08636)
*Kian C. Shah,Arthemy V. Kiselev*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究\\(N=2p\\)个加权微分算子\\(w_j(x)\\partial_x^p\\)的交替复合，其系数为常数\\(c(p)\\)乘Wronskian；通过将权取单项式\\(w_j=x^{j-1}\\)并把求和限制在晚增长置换集\\(\Phi_p\\)上，用带符号的下降阶乘乘积算法算出\\(c(2)=2\\)至\\(c(18)\\approx4.881\\times10^{462}\\)，序列注册为OEIS A392714，并给出\\(\\log c(p)\\simeq2p^2\\log p-bp^2+\\bar o(p^2)\\)、\\(b\\ge2.6744\\)的渐近增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定紧阶\\(p\\)的加权微分算子\\(w_j(x)\\partial_x^p\\)交替复合后的系数常数\\(c(p)\\)。\\(p=1\\)时由向量场的Lie括号给出\\(c(1)=1\\)，需找出\\(p\\ge2\\)的值（如\\(c(2)=2\\)、\\(c(3)=90\\)）。直接对\\((2p)!\\)个置换作符号展开在\\(p\\ge4\\)时不可行，故需要更高效的算法来获得较大的\\(c(p)\\)及其增长规律。

Method: 将权取单项式\\(w_j=x^{j-1}\\)，把原求和缩减到\\(S_{2p-1}\\)中较小的晚增长置换子集\\(\\Phi_p\\)；把\\(c(p)\\)表示为下降阶乘之积的带符号和，据此实现并加速算法。

Result: 算出\\(c(2)=2\\)、\\(c(3)=90\\)，并得到直到\\(c(18)=4.881\\ldots\\times10^{462}\\)的全部整数值；所得序列为新序列，注册为OEIS A392714；\\(p\\gg1\\)时\log c(p)\\simeq2p^2\\log p-bp^2+\\bar o(p^2)，且\\(b\\ge2.6744\\)。

Conclusion: 该工作给出算子交替复合系数\\(c(p)\\)的高效计算方法与大范围数值，指出其对应新整数序列A392714，并刻画了\\(c(p)\\)的（次）主阶渐近增长率及下界\\(b\\ge2.6744\\)。

Abstract: The alternating composition of $N = 2p$ weighted differential operators $w_j(x)\cdot\partial_x^{\,p}$ of strict order $p$ on the line $\mathbb{R} \ni x$ is again an operator of order $p$; its coefficient is the universal constant $c(p)$ times the Wronskian of the weights $w_1,\ldots,w_N$. Lie brackets of vector fields fix $c(p=1)=1$; we want to find $c(p \geqslant 2)$: e.g., $c(2) = 2$ or $c(3) = 90$. Direct symbolic expansion (over $|S_{2p}| =(2p)!$ permutations) fails for $p \geqslant 4$. Taking the monomials $w_j = x^{j-1}$ reduces the summation to the much smaller set $Φ_p \subseteq S_{2p-1} \subsetneq S_{2p}$ of late-growing permutations. Expressing $c(p)$ as a signed sum of products of falling factorials, we implement and speed up the algorithm that gains all the integer values up to $c(18) = 4.881\ldots \cdot 10^{462}$. The resulting sequence is new, now registered as OEIS A392714; its (sub)leading-order growth rate is $\log c(p) \simeq 2p^2\log p -b p^2 + \overline{o}(p^2)$ for $p\gg 1$, with $b\geqslant 2.6744$.

</details>


### [115] [New algebraic points on covers of elliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08753)
*Diana Mocanu,George C. Turcas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文研究与椭圆曲线$n$次覆盖$C\to E$相关的"新点"集合$C(L)_{\text{new}}$的稀疏性，证明在适当假设下，满足$C(L)_{\text{new}}\neq\varnothing$的原始$n$次域$L$所对应的绝对判别式计数为$O(X^{1/2})$或$O(X/(\log X)^\alpha)$，并在$n=2,3,4,5$时给出密度为$0$的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Khawaja与Siksek研究了$C$为$\mathbb{P}^1$的$n$次覆盖时$C(L)_{\text{new}}$的空性猜想，并给出证据。本文旨在补充其工作，将类似结果推广到椭圆曲线$E$的$n$次覆盖$C\to E$的情形。

Method: 在适当假设下，利用Bhargava–Shankar–Wang与McGown–Thorne–Tucker关于带局部约束的域计数结果，对满足$C(L)_{\text{new}}\neq\varnothing$的原始$n$次域$L$的绝对判别式进行计数估计。

Result: 主结果表明，在适当假设下，绝对判别式不超过$X$且满足$C(L)_{\text{new}}\neq\varnothing$的原始$n$次域$L$的个数为$O(X^{1/2})$或$O(X/(\log X)^\alpha)$（$\alpha>0$）。在$n=2,3,4,5$时这些域在同次域中密度为$0$（$n=4$时在原始域中亦然）。此外，给出$8$条双椭圆模曲线$X_0(N)$的具体例子，使得$X_0(N)(L)_{\text{new}}=\emptyset$对$100\%$的二次域$L$成立，并指出LMFDB中次数为$3$和$5$的模覆盖亦具类似结论。

Conclusion: 该工作将Khawaja–Siksek关于$\mathbb{P}^1$覆盖的稀疏性结果推广到椭圆曲线覆盖，证明了小次数情形下"新点"域密度为零，并给出双椭圆模曲线及模覆盖的具体实例。

Abstract: For a smooth projective curve $C/\mathbb{Q}$ of genus $\geq 2$ and $L/\mathbb{Q}$ an extension, we write $C(L)_{\text{new}}=\{P\in C(L):\mathbb{Q}(P)=L\}$. Recent work of Khawaja and Siksek conjectures that this set is empty for $100\%$ of degree $n$ number fields $L$, when ordered by absolute discriminant. Moreover, they bring evidence towards this conjecture when $C$ is a degree $n$ cover of $\mathbb{P}^1$. We complement their work by proving analogous results for degree $n$ covers $ψ:C\to E$ of elliptic curves $E$.
  Our main result shows that, under suitable hypotheses, the number of distinct absolute discriminants at most $X$ of primitive degree $n$ fields $L$ with $C(L)_{\text{new}}\neq\varnothing$ is $O(X^{1/2})$ or $O(X/(\log X)^α)$, for some $α>0$. In degrees $2,3,4$ and $5$ we show that these fields have density $0$ among all fields of the same degree (in degree $4$, also among the primitive ones). The novelty is for degrees $4$ and $5$, where we use work of Bhargava--Shankar--Wang and McGown--Thorne--Tucker to count fields with specified local constraints.
  Moreover, we give concrete examples of $8$ bielliptic modular curves $X_0(N)$, for which $X_0(N)(L)_{\text{new}}=\emptyset$ for $100 \%$ of quadratic fields $L$. Lastly, we point out modular covers of degrees $3$ and $5$ in the LMFDB for which similar conclusions hold.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [The effect of edge deletion on noncommutative distances on graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07718)
*Eric Swartz*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 删除图的一条边可能"减小"非交换距离，加权4-环是最小例子；其上确界2/√3对所有图成立；n-环出现此现象当且仅当4|n；被删边/顶点可距两顶点任意远。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Dirac算子D定义的有限加权图上的非交换(Connes)距离d^D，考察删边这一局部扰动如何影响两顶点间的非交换距离——这是一个反直觉的问题，因为通常期望删边使距离不减。

Method: 对有限加权图上的Dirac算子D及关联非交换距离d^D，分析删除一条边后得到的D'与对应距离d^{D'}；通过加权4-环的完全刻画确定sup d^D/d^{D'}，并结合图论(边数)距离与加权(测地)距离讨论被删边/顶点与端点的位置关系。

Result: 删边确实能减小两顶点间的非交换距离；最小例子来自加权4-环，对4-环完全确定sup d^D/d^{D'}=2/√3，且该上界对所有图成立；对n-环，删边减小两顶点间非交换距离当且仅当n被4整除；被删边不必与x,y相邻，且可在图论距离与加权测地距离上任意远；删除距x,y任意远(两种距离意义下)的单个顶点可使d^D(x,y)改变任意大的因子。

Conclusion: 删边/删点等局部扰动对非交换距离的影响可以是非单调且高度非局部的：删边可减小距离且上确比有统一界2/√3，n-环情形由4|n完全判定；被删元素可在两种距离度量下任意远离端点，删单点还能任意放大距离。

Abstract: For a Dirac operator $D$ on a finite weighted graph, let $d^D$ denote the associated noncommutative (Connes) distance. We show that deleting an edge can actually decrease the noncommutative distance between two vertices in the graph. The smallest example of this phenomenon comes from a weighted 4-cycle, and the case of deleting an edge from a weighted 4-cycle is determined completely: if $d^{D'}$ denotes the noncommutative distance in the graph after edge deletion, we prove that $\sup d^D/d^{D'} = 2/\sqrt{3}$ for the $4$-cycle, and further that this upper bound holds for all graphs. On the other hand, we show that deleting an edge can decrease the noncommutative distance between two vertices of an $n$-cycle precisely when $n$ is divisible by $4$.
  In cases when a deleted edge decreases the noncommutative distance between two vertices $x$ and $y$, we show that the deleted edge need not be incident to either of the vertices $x$ or $y$, and in fact the deleted edge can be arbitrarily far from both in the graph-theoretic (number of edges) distance and in the weighted (geodesic) distance. Moreover, deletion of a single vertex arbitrarily far from both $x$ and $y$ in either the graph-theoretic or weighted distance can change $d^D(x,y)$ by an arbitrarily large factor.

</details>


### [117] [Molnár's Theorem for split $C^*$-by-nilpotent algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08010)
*Mario Galici,Gianmarco La Rosa,Manuel Mancini,Gábor P. Nagy*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究算子代数正元集合上Kubo-Ando平均的代数结构，利用解析局部展开方法，将L. Molnár关于C*-代数的结果推广到split C*-by-nilpotent代数，并证明在任意C*-代数中恒等式[x,[y,z]]=0蕴含交换性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubo-Ando平均是算子代数正元集合上一类重要的二元运算，但其代数结构尚待深入理解。近期L. Molnár针对C*-代数给出了相关结果，本文希望将这一结果推广到更一般的代数类，并探究由其性质导出的交换性条件。

Method: 采用二元运算的解析局部展开方法（method of analytic local expansion of binary operations），将L. Molnár的C*-代数结果推广至split C*-by-nilpotent代数（即可由C*-代数与幂零代数分裂扩张得到的代数）。

Result: 成功将L. Molnár的结果从C*-代数推广到split C*-by-nilpotent代数；并进一步证明：在任意C*-代数A中，恒等式[x,[y,z]]=0蕴含该代数是交换的。

Conclusion: 通过解析局部展开技术刻画了Kubo-Ando平均的代数结构，并将其结论推广到更广的代数类，最终给出了C*-代数交换性的一个新判据。

Abstract: Kubo-Ando means are a rich class of important binary operations on the set of positive elements of an operator algebra. The aim of this article is to understand the algebraic structure of these operations. We generalize a recent result of L. Molnár for $C^*$-algebras to the class of split $C^*$-by-nilpotent algebras by using the method of analytic local expansion of binary operations. Eventually, we prove that in any $C^*$-algebra $\mathcal{A}$, the identity $[x,[y,z]]=0$ implies commutativity.

</details>


### [118] [A noncommutative transfer principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08032)
*Louis E Labuschagne,Claud Steyn*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文将广义Calderón转移原理推广到σ-紧局部紧Hausdorff群在半有限von Neumann代数上的保迹群作用情形，并发展2-变量递减重排与极大算子工具，据此将交换情形下的遍历收敛结果提升到非交换Orlicz空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Calderón转移原理及其传递算子工具在交换情形已有成果（\cite{dBL}），但其在半有限von Neumann代数上的非交换推广尚缺，尤其是缺乏适用于群作用的遍历收敛框架。作者希望建立一套协议，把群语境下的正则算子转移到代数语境，从而获得非交换Orlicz空间上的遍历收敛定理。

Method: 在保迹群作用下，先在非交换语境构造并证明"转移算子"的存在性；随后为此建立两件工具：(1) 代数L^\infty \overline{\otimes} \M（(Γ,ν)为Radon测度空间）上的所谓2-变量递减重排理论；(2) 适用于该语境的极大算子概念。利用这些工具，将\cite{dBL}中的遍历收敛结果提升到半有限von Neumann代数的非交换设定。

Result: 证明非交换语境下转移算子的存在性；建立2-变量递减重排理论与相应极大算子理论；成功将交换情形的遍历收敛结果提升为非交换Orlicz空间上的遍历收敛结果；并给出展示这些工具应用的实例。

Conclusion: 扩展后的Calderón转移原理为半有限von Neumann代数上保迹群作用的非交换遍历分析提供了一套系统协议，配合2-变量递减重排和极大算子，可将交换结果系统迁移至非交换Orlicz空间。

Abstract: We extend the generalised Calderon Transfer Principle as presented in \cite{dBL} to the setting of trace preserving group actions of $σ$-compact locally compact Hausdorff groups on semifinite von Neumann algebras $\M$. At its essence the transfer principle consists of showing that a large class of regular operators in the group context may be translated to operators in the algebra context by means of this group action. The end result is a protocol for proving ergodic convergence results for group actions on noncommutative Orlicz space of semifinite von Neumann algebras. We start with proving the existence of the transferred operator in the noncommutative context. Two tools are developed for the purpose of actually proving convergence results: (1) a theory of what may be called 2-variable decreasing rearrangements for the algebra $L^\infty \overline{\otimes} \M$ (where ($Γ,ν$) is a Radon measure space) and (2) a concepts of maximal operators suited to the present context. These tools are then used to lift the ergodic convergence results presented in \cite{dBL} to the noncommutative setting. In closing we present examples illustrating the application of the tools

</details>


### [119] [Representations of the infinite Cuntz algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08094)
*David. E. Evans,Arnaud Brothier*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文构造了从Cuntz代数到具有有限生成元的Pythagorean代数的内蕴单完全正映射，用于提升状态与循环表示，并通过归纳极限得到构造无穷Cuntz代数上有效状态的方法，进而获得大量两两不等价的不可约表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种有效机制来构造无穷Cuntz代数上的状态及不可约表示，以刻画其表示论结构。

Method: 定义从Cuntz代数到具有有限生成元的Pythagorean代数的内蕴单完全正（ucp）映射，用于提升状态和循环表示；该映射限制到Cuntz-Toeplitz代数与收缩代数；通过归纳极限构造无穷Cuntz代数上的状态，并结合已有技巧生成不可约表示族。

Result: 得到构造 O_∞ 上状态的有效机制，并获得大量两两不等价的不可约表示，其中一个族与Hilbert空间的单位球面存在明显双射。

Conclusion: 该ucp映射与归纳极限方法为研究无穷Cuntz代数的状态空间与不可约表示提供了统一有效框架，并揭示了表示族与单位球面的对应关系。

Abstract: We define an intrinsic unital completely positive (ucp) map from the Cuntz algebra to the Pythagorean with finitely many generators. This permits to lift states and cyclic representations. This process restricts to the Cuntz-Toeplitz algebras and the contraction algebras. Taking inductive limits we obtain an effective machinery for constructing states on the infinite Cuntz algebra $\mathcal O_\infty$. Combining this approach with previous techniques of the first author we obtain large families of pairwise inequivalent irreducible representations of $\mathcal O_\infty$: one family being in an obvious bijection with the unit sphere of Hilbert's space.

</details>


### [120] [Finite-dimensional approximations for C*-algebras of product systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08193)
*Evgenios T. A. Kakariadis,Ioannis Apollon Paraskevas*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 研究由可数群单位子半群上乘积系统表示生成的C*-代数：精确性等价于核在可构造理想上的精确性，核性等价于核在不动点代数中的核可嵌入性；在紧对齐、饱和条件下，核的性质可由系数C*-代数继承。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在推广右LCM半群上紧对齐饱和乘积系统的已知结论，刻画更一般（可数群的单位子半群上）乘积系统所生成C*-代数的精确性与核性，并判断核的性质能否从系数C*-代数继承。

Method: 通过研究乘积系统的表示，利用核（cores）、可构造理想、不动点代数等工具，分析生成C*-代数的精确性与核性；进一步研究单射等变Fock协变表示，并在半群有限对齐、强有限对齐及乘积系统饱和、紧对齐的假设下比较核与系数C*-代数的性质。

Result: 第一部分：生成C*-代数的精确性等价于核在可构造理想上的精确性；其核性等价于核在不动点代数中的核可嵌入性。第二部分：若半群有限对齐且乘积系统饱和、紧对齐，则核的精确性等价于系数C*-代数的精确性；若半群还强有限对齐，则核的核可嵌入性等价于系数C*-代数的核可嵌入性。去掉紧对齐假设后，即使对右LCM半群这些结论也不成立。

Conclusion: 乘积系统生成C*-代数的精确性与核性可由核在可构造理想上、不动点代数中的相应性质刻画；在紧对齐、饱和（及强有限对齐）条件下，核的性质可从系数C*-代数继承，而紧对齐假设是必要的。

Abstract: We study C*-algebras generated by representations of product systems over unital subsemigroups of amenable discrete groups. In the first part, we show that exactness of the generated C*-algebra is equivalent to exactness of the cores over the constructible ideals. Nuclearity of the generated C*-algebra is equivalent to nuclear embeddability of the cores in the fixed point algebra. In the second part, we further study injective equivariant Fock covariant representations, and whether the properties of the cores are inherited from properties of the coefficient C*-algebra, as in the case of compactly aligned saturated product systems over right LCM semigroups. If the semigroup is finitely aligned and the product system is saturated and compactly aligned, then exactness of the cores is equivalent to exactness of the coefficient C*-algebra. If in addition the semigroup is strongly finitely aligned, then nuclear embeddability of the cores is equivalent to nuclear embeddability of the coefficient C*-algebra. These results do not hold without the compact alignment hypothesis, even for right LCM semigroups.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Spectral gaps of double covers of hyperbolic surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07193)
*Lawford Hatcher,Bram Petri*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 研究双覆盖上小特征值出现与避免的条件：多数有限面积可定向双曲曲面模空间中，存在所有二次覆盖均出现新小特征值的曲面；而四穿孔球面及任意亏格闭曲面可存在无新小特征值的二次覆盖，后者借助双覆盖版Cheeger不等式证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨双曲曲面在二次覆盖（双覆盖）下是否必然出现新的小特征值，即双覆盖与小特征值（谱几何）之间的关系，并厘清哪些曲面能避免这种谱现象。

Method: 构造或分析模空间中曲面的二次覆盖；对四穿孔球面与闭曲面，采用适用于双覆盖的Cheeger不等式变体进行论证。

Result: 在多数有限面积可定向双曲曲面模空间中，存在所有二次覆盖均具有新小特征值的曲面；四穿孔球面必有二次覆盖无新小特征值；任意亏格的闭曲面也存在具有该性质的曲面。

Conclusion: 二次覆盖是否必然产生新小特征值依赖于曲面类型：一般模空间中的曲面可被构造为“必然出现”，而四穿孔球面与某些闭曲面则可“避免”该现象，其证明可借助双覆盖版的Cheeger不等式。

Abstract: We prove that, in most moduli spaces of orientable hyperbolic surfaces of finite area, there exist surfaces all of whose covers of degree two have a new small eigenvalue. On the other hand, we show that four punctured spheres always admit a double cover without new small eigenvalues and that there exist closed surfaces of any genus with the same property. We prove the last two of these results using a version of Cheeger's inequality adapted to double covers.

</details>


### [122] [Kuznecov formulas for domains with fractal boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08487)
*Xucheng Hu,Yakun Xi*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究流形上区域内的谱投影积分，证明当边界为s-Ahlfors正则且区域满足双侧螺钻条件时，N_Ω(λ)=vol(Ω)+O(λ^{s-d})且余项最优；进一步指出二阶渐近由边界的s维穿越体积决定，并讨论自相似边界的非格与格情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子遍历/谱投影的局部化问题，即在紧黎曼流形上，对区域Ω内Laplace特征函数的积分平方和进行渐近分析，将谱渐近与边界的几何/测度性质联系起来。

Method: 在∂Ω为s-Ahlfors正则（s∈[d-1,d)）且Ω满足双侧螺钻条件的假设下，通过分析谱投影核与边界的相互作用给出N_Ω(λ)的渐近展开；引入边界的s维穿越体积V^s(∂Ω)，并利用其存在性刻画精确的二阶渐近项。

Result: 证得N_Ω(λ)=vol(Ω)+O(λ^{s-d})且余项最优；当边界存在自然的s维穿越体积时，得到两项渐近N_Ω(λ)=vol(Ω)-C_{d,s}V^s(∂Ω)λ^{s-d}+o(λ^{s-d})。对可容许自相似边界，非格情形穿越体积存在，两项渐近成立；格情形二阶行为由对数周期剖面主导。

Conclusion: 该结果刻画了谱投影积分的二阶渐近与边界s维测度之间的关系，穿越体积为决定性的几何量；自相似边界的格与非格情形出现不同行为，显示了边界几何对谱渐近的精细影响。

Abstract: Let $(M,g)$ be a compact $d$-dimensional Riemannian manifold without boundary, and let $\{e_j\}_{j=0}^\infty$ be an orthonormal basis of Laplace eigenfunctions with frequencies $\{λ_j\}_{j=0}^\infty$. For a domain $Ω\subset M$, consider $$ N_Ω(λ) = \sum_{λ_j\leqλ} \left|\int_Ωe_j\,dV_g\right|^2. $$ Suppose that $\partialΩ$ is $s$-Ahlfors regular, with $s\in{[d-1,d)}$, and that $Ω$ satisfies a two-sided corkscrew condition. We prove that $$ N_Ω(λ) = \operatorname{vol}(Ω)+O\bigl(λ^{s-d}\bigr), $$ and that this remainder is sharp. We further identify the geometric quantity governing the second term: an exact asymptotic of order $λ^{s-d}$ holds precisely when the boundary admits a natural $s$-dimensional crossing volume $\mathcal V^s(\partialΩ)$, in which case $$ N_Ω(λ) = \operatorname{vol}(Ω) - C_{d,s}\, \mathcal V^s(\partialΩ)\, λ^{s-d} + o\bigl(λ^{s-d}\bigr). $$ For admissible self-similar boundaries, the crossing volume exists in the non-lattice case, and hence the above two-term asymptotic holds. In the lattice case, the second-order behavior is instead governed by a log-periodic profile.

</details>


### [123] [On the Dirac Faber-Krahn Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08665)
*Habib Ammari,Jiayu Qiu*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文证明了非负质量狄拉克算子在无限质量边界条件下的严格Faber-Krahn不等式，即第一正特征值在给定面积下由圆盘取得最小值，且等号成立当且仅当区域为圆盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Faber-Krahn不等式是谱几何中的经典结果，但针对狄拉克算子的相应版本，尤其在非负质量及无限质量边界条件下，尚缺乏严格的等号刻画与证明。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立该算子第一正特征值的严格 Faber-Krahn 型不等式。

Method: 核心方法是证明与第一正特征值对应的狄拉克旋量上分量在区域上全局非零，该性质通过分析其狄拉克流及伴随的流函数得到。基于此全局非零性，构造一个度为1的球面值旋量映射，并利用平面等周不等式与径向圆盘剖面进行精确比较。

Result: 证明了 λ₁⁺(Ω;m) ≥ λ₁⁺(B_{|Ω|};m)，其中 B_{|Ω|} 是与 Ω 等面积的圆盘；且等号成立当且仅当 Ω = B_{|Ω|}，即为严格不等式。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典 Faber-Krahn 不等式推广到非负质量狄拉克算子并给出严格等号条件，关键突破在于旋量上分量的全局非零性及其诱导的度一映射构造。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the strict Faber-Krahn inequality for non-negative-mass Dirac operators on simply connected domains with infinite-mass boundary condition. Denoting the first positive eigenvalue of the corresponding Dirac operator with mass $m\geq 0$ as $λ_1^{+}(Ω;m)$, our main result shows that \begin{equation*} λ_1^{+}(Ω;m)\geq λ_1^{+}(B_{|Ω|};m), \end{equation*} where $B_{|Ω|}$ is the disk with the same area as the domain $Ω$, and the equality is strictly valid if and only if $Ω=B_{|Ω|}$. The key observation leading to the proof is that the upper component of the Dirac spinor associated with $λ_1^{+}(Ω;m)$ is non-vanishing over $Ω$, which is proved by exploiting its Dirac current and the associated stream function. Based on this global non-vanishing property, we construct a sphere-valued spinor map of degree one, which allows a sharp comparison with the radial disk profile using the planar isoperimetry.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Homotopy Poisson and BV$_\infty$ structures on derived moduli spaces of flat connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07195)
*Sergei A. Merkulov*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文将等变（quasi-Poisson / quasi-BV）结构重新表述为 $M\times\Pi g$ 上的同伦（homotopy Poisson / $BV_\infty$）结构，并将其应用于带边曲面上平坦 $G$-联络的模空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有 Lie（超）群 $G$ 作用的（超）流形上，quasi-Poisson 与 quasi-BV 结构通常以"偏离严格性"的方式出现，缺少统一的同伦代数框架。作者希望用更高阶的同伦结构（而非仅在原流形上）来统一刻画这类结构，并给出可计算的构造，从而为模空间等几何对象提供自然解释。

Method: 核心构造基于如下事实：任意带不变（奇或偶）标量积的 Lie（超）代数 $g$ 存在本质上唯一的一族高阶 Jacobiator。利用该族，将 $M$ 上 $G$-等变的 quasi-Poisson / quasi-BV 结构与微分（超）流形 $\mathcal{M}=M\times\Pi g$ 上特殊类别的 homotopy Poisson / $BV_\infty$ 结构建立一一对应。随后结合 Fock–Rosly 构造，将上述对应应用于带边曲面上平坦 $G$-联络的模空间。

Result: 证明了 quasi-Poisson 与 quasi-BV 结构可等同于 $M\times\Pi g$ 上的一类 homotopy Poisson 与 $BV_\infty$ 结构；并通过 Fock–Rosly 构造，将该等同性落实到带边曲面上平坦 $G$-联络模空间的等变结构分析中。

Conclusion: quasi-Poisson/quasi-BV 并非孤立现象，而是 $M\times\Pi g$ 上更高阶同伦结构的投影/约化表现；高阶 Jacobiator 的存在性与唯一性是这一对应的关键，且该框架对 Fock–Rosly 型模空间具有直接应用价值。

Abstract: We show that quasi-Poisson and quasi-BV structures on (super)manifolds $M$ admitting an action of a Lie (super)group $G$ can be identified with a special class of homotopy Poisson and respectively $BV_\infty$ structures on the associated differential (super)manifolds of the form ${\mathcal M}:=M\times Πg$, $g$ being the Lie (super)algebra of $G$. The construction is based on the proven existence of an essentially unique family of higher Jacobiators for any Lie (super)algebra $g$ with an invariant (odd or even) scalar product.
  We apply these arguments to the moduli spaces $M$ of flat $G$-connections on a surface with boundaries using Fock-Rosly construction.

</details>


### [125] [Contact Monopoles in Dimension Five: Rigidity and Homogeneous Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07246)
*Luis Portilla*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文引入接触5-流形上的接触单极子（contact monopole），研究其基本几何性质，构造来自Boothby–Wang纤维化的自然例子，并证明紧致刚性定理；随后在单位切球丛与齐性Sasakian流形上求解并分类不变解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触几何框架下引入单极子这类对象，将接触瞬子（contact instanton）与Higgs场理论联系，为接触5-流形提供新的模空间与不变量研究角度，并探索其与反自对偶接触瞬子的关系及形变复形的椭圆性。

Method: 在接触度量5-流形上定义接触单极子；从Kähler曲面的Boothby–Wang纤维化构造例子；证明反自对偶接触瞬子配平行Higgs场给出接触单极子；证明紧致接触度量5-流形上光滑接触单极子的刚性（平行Higgs场且满足约化曲率方程F_A∧ω=0）；在Sasakian情形下结合通常横向型条件联系反自对偶接触瞬子；说明形变复形非椭圆；在单位切球丛上用典范适配标架导出局部方程并证纯拉回构型刚性；在T_1S^3≅SO(4)/SO(2)上用Wang定理将不变接触单极子方程约化为有限维代数系统并分类。

Result: 紧致接触度量5-流形上任一光滑接触单极子具有平行Higgs场并满足F_A∧ω=0；Sasakian情形下对应反自对偶接触瞬子；相关形变复形非椭圆；单位切球丛上纯拉回构型刚性；在T_1S^3上分类不变解：U(1)情形得到非平凡拓扑荷的非平坦齐性U(1)接触单极子；SO(3)情形除m=1外各权约化到极大环面，m=1给出真正非阿贝尔的齐性SO(3)接触单极子分支。

Conclusion: 接触单极子是接触度量5-流形上有意义的几何对象，与反自对偶接触瞬子及Higgs场结构密切相关；紧致情形下具有强刚性，且单位切球丛与齐性Sasakian流形上可显式构造与分类不变解，为后续模空间与不变量研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We introduce contact monopoles on contact metric $5$-manifolds and study their basic geometric properties. We construct natural examples from Boothby--Wang fibrations over Kähler surfaces and relate the equation to contact instantons on Sasakian $5$-manifolds. In particular, we show that anti-self-dual contact instantons with a parallel Higgs field provide contact monopoles. We also prove a compact rigidity result: on compact contact metric $5$-manifolds, every smooth contact monopole has parallel Higgs field and satisfies the reduced curvature equation $F_A\wedgeω=0$. In the Sasakian case, this relates compact contact monopoles to anti-self-dual contact instantons under the usual transverse type condition. We also explain why the associated deformation complex is not elliptic in the usual sense.
  We then focus on unit tangent sphere bundles of oriented Riemannian $3$-manifolds, endowed with their canonical contact metric structures. In this setting, we derive a local form of the contact monopole equation with respect to the canonical adapted frame and prove that pure pullback configurations from the base are rigid. Finally, for the homogeneous Sasakian manifold $T_1S^3\cong SO(4)/SO(2)$, we use Wang's theorem to reduce the invariant contact monopole equation to finite-dimensional algebraic systems and classify the invariant solutions for homogeneous $U(1)$- and $SO(3)$-bundles. In the abelian case, the classification yields non-flat homogeneous $U(1)$ contact monopoles for nonzero topological charge. In the non-abelian case, all weights except $m=1$ reduce to the maximal torus, while the weight $m=1$ gives a genuinely non-abelian branch of homogeneous $SO(3)$ contact monopoles.

</details>


### [126] [Discreteness of real $\mathrm{SL}_n$-opers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07453)
*Panagiotis Dimakis,Richard Wentworth*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了实 SL_n-opers 构成不可约平坦联络模空间中的一个离散子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实 SL_n-opers 与不可约平坦联络模空间之间的几何关系此前缺乏明确的离散性刻画，本文旨在刻画其作为子集的结构性质。

Method: 通过分析实 SL_n-opers 在不可约平坦联络模空间中的嵌入，利用其参数化与刚性性质证明离散性。

Result: 实 SL_n-opers 的集合在不可约平坦联络模空间中为离散子集。

Conclusion: 实 SL_n-opers 不能连续变形，其在模空间中呈孤立分布，反映了此类 oper 的刚性特征。

Abstract: We prove that the set of real $\mathrm{SL}_n$-opers forms a discrete subset of the moduli space of irreducible flat connections.

</details>


### [127] [Curvature-Based Bounds for $p$-Capacity and Capacity--Area Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07564)
*Xiaoshang Jin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文建立凸区域 \(p\)-容量的曲率型上下界与容量–面积不等式：在欧氏空间中，若边界 Gauss–Kronecker 曲率满足 \(0<K_-\le K\le K_+\)，则对 \(1<p\le \frac{n+1}{2}\) 有以球为极值的双边容量比较，且上界比较的指数临界为 \(p=\frac{n+1}{2}\)；在夹紧条件下给出显式尺度不变容量–面积下界，并推出特定参数下的 Pólya–Szegő 不等式；在 \(\operatorname{Sec}\le -k^2<0\) 的 Cartan–Hadamard 流形上给出保留主曲率信息的容量下界及等号刚性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究凸区域 \(p\)-容量能否由边界曲率定量控制，并寻找容量–面积不等式及曲率比较中最优的指数范围，同时将结果推广到负曲率流形。

Method: 以边界 Gauss–Kronecker 曲率的双边夹紧 \(0<K_-\le K\le K_+\) 为条件，通过与半径由曲率界决定的球做容量比较；对夹紧比 \(\rho=K_-/K_+\) 推出尺度不变容量–面积下界；在 Cartan–Hadamard 流形上用截面曲率上界 \(\operatorname{Sec}\le -k^2<0\) 结合边界主曲率进行估计。

Result: 欧氏空间中 \(1<p\le \frac{n+1}{2}\) 时成立双边比较 \(\operatorname{Cap}_p(B(K_+^{1/(1-n)}))\le \operatorname{Cap}_p(\Omega)\le \operatorname{Cap}_p(B(K_-^{1/(1-n)}))\)，下界对全范围 \(1<p<n\) 成立；当 \(n\ge4,\ \frac{n+1}{2}<p<n\) 时上界比较可能失效，故 \(p=\frac{n+1}{2}\) 为临界指数。夹紧条件下得到显式尺度不变容量–面积下界；\(n=3,p=2\) 时 \(\operatorname{Cap}_2(\Omega)/\sqrt{|\partial\Omega|}\ge 4\sqrt{\pi}\rho^{1/4}/(1+\sqrt{\rho})\)，从而在 \(\rho\ge \rho_*\simeq0.15484\) 时蕴含 Pólya–Szegő 不等式。流形情形给出保留主曲率的容量下界、Gauss–Kronecker 曲率估计及等号刚性。

Conclusion: 边界曲率可有效控制凸区域 \(p\)-容量，容量比较存在最优临界指数 \(p=\frac{n+1}{2}\)；夹紧曲率给出显式容量–面积下界并可推出 Pólya–Szegő 型结论；负曲率 Cartan–Hadamard 流形上等号情形对应翘曲乘积型外部几何。

Abstract: We establish curvature-based bounds for the $p$-capacity of convex domains and related capacity--area inequalities. Let $K$ denote the Gauss--Kronecker curvature of $\partialΩ$. In Euclidean space, if $
0<K_-\leq K\leq K_+, $
then, for every $1<p\leq \frac{n+1}{2}$, $$ \operatorname{Cap}_p\left(B(K_+^{\frac{1}{1-n}})\right) \leq \operatorname{Cap}_p(Ω) \leq \operatorname{Cap}_p\left(B(K_-^{\frac{1}{1-n}})\right). $$ The lower comparison remains valid for the full range $1<p<n$. Moreover, the exponent range in the upper comparison is optimal in general: when $n\geq 4$ and $\frac{n+1}{2}<p<n$, the upper comparison may fail. Thus $p=\frac{n+1}{2}$ is the critical exponent for the curvature-radius upper comparison.
  Under the pinching condition $
\frac{K_-}{K_+}=ρ\in (0, 1], $
we further obtain an explicit scale-invariant capacity--area lower bound for every $1<p<n$. In the special case $n=3$ and $p=2$, $$ \frac{\operatorname{Cap}_2(Ω)} {\sqrt{|\partialΩ|}} \geq \frac{4\sqrtπ\,ρ^{1/4}} {1+\sqrtρ}, $$ which implies the Pólya--Szegö inequality whenever $$ ρ\geq ρ_*:= \left( \frac{π^2-4-π\sqrt{π^2-8}}{4} \right)^2 \simeq 0.15484. $$
  On a Cartan--Hadamard manifold with $\operatorname{Sec}\leq -k^2<0$, we obtain a $p$-capacity lower bound retaining all principal curvatures of the boundary, together with corresponding Gauss--Kronecker curvature estimates and a rigidity characterization of the equality case by a warped-product exterior geometry.

</details>


### [128] [Uniformly Positive Scalar Curvature on Manifold Interiors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07608)
*Shuli Chen*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 利用Seiberg–Witten理论给出某些4-流形内部存在一致正数量曲率完备度量的拓扑障碍，并研究5、6、7维紧定向流形内部正数量曲率完备度量与带极小边界（及边界乘积结构）的正数量曲率度量之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一致正数量曲率完备度量在非紧流形（流形内部）上的存在性及其拓扑限制，并厘清其与紧流形带边界正数量曲率度量之间的对应关系。

Method: 对4-流形内部，使用Seiberg–Witten理论构造一致正数量曲率完备度量的拓扑障碍；对5、6、7维紧定向流形，将内部一致正数量曲率完备度量与带极小边界（6、7维及5维附加假设下可进一步取边界乘积形式）的正数量曲率度量相联系。

Result: 同伦K3流形的任何去心爆破（punctured blowups）均不允许一致正数量曲率的完备度量。若5、6、7维紧定向光滑流形X的内部存在一致正数量曲率完备度量，则X存在极小边界的正数量曲率度量；6、7维可选取边界附近为乘积形式；5维在附加假设并可能改变光滑结构后亦可取边界乘积形式。

Conclusion: 一致正数量曲率完备度量的存在性受到拓扑障碍约束（如去心爆破的同伦K3流形），且其与紧流形带极小边界（及乘积边界）的正数量曲率度量存在密切联系，尤其在高维情形下可推出边界乘积结构。

Abstract: We obtain topological obstructions to the existence of a complete Riemannian metric with uniformly positive scalar curvature on the interiors of certain 4-manifolds using Seiberg--Witten theory. In particular, no punctured blowups of a homotopy K3 manifold admit complete metrics of uniformly positive scalar curvature.
  Further, let $X$ be a compact oriented smooth manifold of dimension $5$, $6$, or $7$. We show that if $\operatorname{Int}X$ admits a complete metric of uniformly positive scalar curvature, then $X$ admits a positive scalar curvature metric with minimal boundary. In dimensions $6$ and $7$, the metric may moreover be chosen to be a product near the boundary. In dimension 5, under additional hypotheses and after possibly changing the smooth structure on $X$, one can likewise obtain a positive scalar curvature metric that is a product near the boundary.

</details>


### [129] [The Sharp Isoperimetric Inequality in the First Heisenberg Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07632)
*Wei-Chun Chen,Wei-Ting Kao,Chun-Kai Lien*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在第一海森堡群中，对任意具有有限水平周长与正有限体积的集合，证明了尖锐水平等周不等式，且等号成立当且仅当集合为Pansu球的左平移与伸缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海森堡群中水平周长的等周问题长期只有非尖锐版本或部分情形结果，缺少对所有有限水平周长集合统一成立的尖锐常数与等号刻画。

Method: 在次黎曼几何框架下，以水平周长与水平体积为基本量，对有限水平周长集合建立尖锐水平等周不等式，并分析等号情形。

Result: 对所有正有限体积、有限水平周长的集合证明了尖锐水平等周不等式；等号仅在集合为Pansu球的左平移与伸缩时取到。

Conclusion: 该结果完整刻画了第一海森堡群中水平等周问题的最优常数与极小集，确立Pansu球作为唯一（模平移与伸缩）等周极小集。

Abstract: We prove the sharp horizontal isoperimetric inequality in the first Heisenberg group for all sets of finite horizontal perimeter and positive finite volume, with equality precisely for left translates and dilates of the Pansu ball.

</details>


### [130] [New Curvature Operator of the Second Kind Conditions for Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07687)
*Xiaolong Li,Peter Petersen*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 利用第二类曲率算子(COSK)建立Tachibana型刚性定理，给出常数$C_n$（$\frac{C_n}{n}\to\frac{5}{4}$）下的刚性结果，并研究shifted COSK与局部对称Einstein流形的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Einstein流形上，通过第二类曲率算子(COSK)的曲率条件来刻画常截面曲率等刚性性质，并将经典Tachibana型刚性推广到COSK框架，同时探索shifted曲率算子的类似结果。

Method: 结合Weyl张量上的对称作用与交换子作用给出sharp估计，得到常数$C_n$；引入shifted第二类曲率算子；为调和形式建立Ricci-corrected Bochner恒等式；计算对称空间COSK特征值并进行分类。

Result: 得到sharp估计使$\frac{C_n}{n}\to\frac{5}{4}$；证明$n\geq5$的完备Einstein流形若具$C_n$-非负COSK则有常非负截面曲率；给出shifted锥中完备单连通局部对称Einstein流形的分类，并用显式空间验证估计的sharp性，列出对称空间COSK特征值以识别borderline情形。

Conclusion: COSK框架为Tachibana型刚性提供了统一且sharp的工具，Ricci-corrected Bochner恒等式与特征值列表为进一步分类及识别极端情形奠定基础。

Abstract: We establish Tachibana-type rigidity theorems with the aid of the curvature operator of the second kind (COSK). A sharp estimate that combines the symmetric and commutator actions on Weyl tensors leads to a constant $C_{n}$, with $\frac{C_{n}}{n}\to\frac{5}{4}$, such that a complete Einstein manifold of dimension $n\geq5$ with $C_{n}$-nonnegative COSK has constant nonnegative sectional curvature. We also explore shifted curvature operators of the second kind leading to similar rigidity results, offer an elegant Ricci-corrected Bochner identity for harmonic forms, and classify the complete simply connected locally symmetric Einstein manifolds in the resulting shifted cone. Explicit spaces establish the sharpness of the tensor estimates, and complete lists of the eigenvalues of the COSKs for symmetric spaces identify which of them are borderline cases.

</details>


### [131] [Codimension Reduction of Minimal Submanifolds with Flat Normal Bundle in Spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07789)
*Juan Li,Zhiyuan Xu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究平坦法丛的完备极小浸入子流形，证明当第二基本形式模长平方 S<2n 时，子流形落入全测地大球面 S^{n+1}，且上界 2n 最优；并在 S+λ₂ ≤ 2n+2n/(3n-2) 条件下给出余维数约化定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极小浸入子流形的余维数约化（codimension reduction）是子流形几何的核心问题之一：在何种曲率或第二基本形式条件下，高余维子流形实际上落在低维全测地子流形中。对于平坦法丛、余维 q≥2 的完备极小浸入，已有结果多要求更强条件或依赖 pinching 常数，本文旨在给出关于 S 的尖锐定量条件，并刻画其最优性。

Method: 设 x:M^n→S^{n+q}（n≥3, q≥2）为完备连通流形的极小浸入且法丛平坦。利用平坦法丛可对角化形状算子并可同时选取规范正交标架，引入形状算子 Gram 矩阵的特征值，特别是第二特征值 λ₂。结合 Simons 型不等式与 pinching 技术，对 S 及 S+λ₂ 作定量估计，并通过结构方程与曲率估计推导余维数约化。

Result: 证明若 S<2n，则 M^n 位于 S^{n+1} 中；三个球面的标准极小乘积表明上界 2n 是尖锐、不可改进的。进一步在 S+λ₂ ≤ 2n+2n/(3n-2) 条件下建立余维数约化定理，同样得出 M^n⊂S^{n+1}。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了平坦法丛完备极小浸入余维数约化的尖锐 S-型判据：S<2n 时余维降至 1，且常数 2n 最优；借助第二特征值 λ₂ 的修正条件放宽了单纯 S-pinching 的限制，扩展了经典余维数约化定理的适用范围。

Abstract: Let $x:M^n\to\mathbb{S}^{n+q}$ be a minimal immersion of a complete connected manifold with flat normal bundle, where $n\ge3$ and $q\ge2$. We prove that if the squared norm of the second fundamental form $S$ satisfies $S<2n$, then $M^n$ lies in $\mathbb{S}^{n+1}$. The standard minimal products of three spheres show that the upper bound $2n$ is optimal. We also prove a codimension reduction theorem under the condition $S+λ_2\le 2n+\frac{2n}{3n-2}$, where $λ_2$ is the second largest eigenvalue of the Gram matrix of the shape operators, implying that $M^n$ lies in $\mathbb{S}^{n+1}$.

</details>


### [132] [Harmonic maps from $\mathbb{S}^{2n+1}$ to $\mathbb{C}P^n$ with low Morse index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07815)
*Luiz Lara*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究复Hopf纤维化的Jacobi谱与低指标刚性：用表示论刻画齐次Riemann纤维化的Jacobi算子，算得Berger度量下指标与零化度，并将Rivière刚性推广到高维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画复Hopf纤维化（Berger度量）的Jacobi谱，并给出低指标调和映射的刚性结论，把Rivière关于S^3→S^2的刚性论证推广到高维S^{2n+1}→CP^n。

Method: 1) 给出齐次Riemann纤维化Jacobi算子的表示论描述；2) 应用于π:(S^{2n+1},g_ε)→(CP^n,h)，其中g_ε为Berger度量、h为Fubini-Study度量，对n≥3确定全部负、零特征值并求Morse指标与零化度的显式公式；3) 将Rivière的刚性论证由S^3→S^2推广到高维。

Result: 对n≥3及任意ε>0，给出π的Morse指标与零化度显式公式；特别地单位圆球度量下Ind(π)=2n+2、Null(π)=n(3n+5)。若φ:S^{2n+1}→CP^n调和且Ind(φ)=2n+2，则每点处dφ_x∘dφ_x^*与CP^n的复结构交换；从而在域等距意义下，φ经由Hopf纤维化分解为φ=P∘π∘f，P:CP^n→CP^n全纯。

Conclusion: 建立了齐次Riemann纤维化Jacobi谱的表示论框架，获得复Hopf纤维化在Berger度量下的完整指标/零化度信息，并将低指标刚性从S^3→S^2推广至S^{2n+1}→CP^n，证明指标达2n+2的调和映射必（在域等距下）透过Hopf纤维化分解且末端为全纯映射。

Abstract: We study the Jacobi spectrum and low-index rigidity of complex Hopf fibrations. We first give a representation-theoretic description of the Jacobi operator of a homogeneous Riemannian fibration and apply it to \[
  π:(\mathbb{S}^{2n+1},g_ε)\longrightarrow(\mathbb{C}P^n,h), \] where $g_ε$ is a Berger metric and $h$ is the Fubini--Study metric. For $n\geq3$, we determine all negative and zero eigenvalues and obtain explicit formulas for the Morse index and nullity for every $ε>0$. In particular, for the unit round metric, $\mathrm{Ind}(π)=2n+2$ and $\mathrm{Null}(π)=n(3n+5)$. We then extend Rivière's rigidity argument from $\mathbb{S}^3\to\mathbb{S}^2$ to higher dimensions. If $φ:\mathbb{S}^{2n+1}\to\mathbb{C}P^n$ is harmonic and $\mathrm{Ind}(φ)=2n+2$, then $dφ_x\circ dφ_x^\ast$ commutes with the complex structure of $\mathbb{C}P^n$ at every point. Consequently, up to an isometry of the domain, $φ$ factors through the Hopf fibration as $φ=P\circπ\circ f$, where $P:\mathbb{C}P^n\to\mathbb{C}P^n$ is holomorphic.

</details>


### [133] [Higher localised $\widehat{A}$-genera: a note on disconnected fixed-point sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07833)
*Hao Guo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文放宽了高局部化 $\widehat{A}$-亏格作为正标量曲率障碍的两个假设：去掉不动点集连通性与群元正则性假设，证明该障碍结论仍成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前由作者与 Mathai 引入的高局部化 $\widehat{A}$-亏格 $\widehat{A}_g(M,\omega)$ 可阻碍 $\Gamma$-不变正标量曲率度量，但该结论依赖两个限制性假设：不动点集 $M^g$ 连通，且 $g$ 关于某实群 $2$-上循环正则。本文旨在消除这些限制，使障碍结论更具普遍性。

Method: 针对有限生成离散群 $\Gamma$ 在自旋流形 $M$ 上的真余紧作用，研究由 $H^*(\underline{B}\Gamma,\mathbb{R})$ 中度数不超过 $2$ 的元素生成的子环所对应的 $\omega$，通过推广原有的高局部化 $\widehat{A}$-亏格构造与指标论证，在不假设 $M^g$ 连通及 $g$ 正则的情形下建立相应的障碍理论。

Result: 证明原结论中关于不动点集 $M^g$ 连通以及 $g$ 关于实群 $2$-上循环正则的两个假设均可去掉，即高局部化 $\widehat{A}$-亏格在更一般情形下仍是 $\Gamma$-不变正标量曲率度量的障碍。

Conclusion: 高局部化 $\widehat{A}$-亏格对 $\Gamma$-不变正标量曲率度量的障碍性在更弱的假设下依然成立，从而扩展了该障碍理论的适用范围。

Abstract: For a proper cocompact action of a finitely generated discrete group $Γ$ on a spin manifold $M$, the higher localised $\widehat{A}$-genera $\widehat{A}_g(M,ω)$ introduced earlier by the author and Mathai were shown to be an obstruction to $Γ$-invariant metrics of positive scalar curvature, for $ω$ arising from the subring of $H^*(\underline{B}Γ,\mathbb{R})$ generated by elements of degree at most $2$, under the assumptions that the fixed-point set $M^g$ is connected and that $g$ is regular with respect to a certain real group $2$-cocycle. We show that both assumptions can be dropped.

</details>


### [134] [The interpolation method for the complex Hessian equation on compact Kähler manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07852)
*Jiaogen Zhang,Xi Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在紧Kähler流形上研究复Hessian方程，利用双截曲率与解的振幅约束，将Laplacian估计线性地由梯度估计控制，从而用标准插值论证（无需blow-up技巧）得到梯度估计；当方程右端在L^1范数意义下逼近1时亦可获得所需梯度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复Hessian方程的先验估计通常依赖blow-up（爆破）技术，该技术较为复杂且技术要求高。作者希望在附加曲率与解的振幅约束下，寻找一条更直接的估计路径，简化梯度估计的推导，并推广到右端项仅在L^1意义下接近1的情形。

Method: 在紧Kähler流形上对复Hessian方程施加双截曲率（bisectional curvature）约束与解的振幅（oscillation）约束，证明Laplacian估计可被梯度估计线性控制；随后采用标准插值论证（interpolation argument）替代blow-up技术，导出解的梯度估计，并处理右端在L^1范数下趋于1的情形。

Result: 在双截曲率与解的振幅约束下，Laplacian估计可由梯度估计线性控制，进而通过标准插值论证得到梯度估计；此外，当复Hessian方程右端在L^1范数下逼近1时，也能获得所期望的梯度估计。

Conclusion: 该工作为复Hessian方程的先验估计提供了一种不依赖blow-up技巧的替代途径，把Laplacian估计归约为梯度估计的线性控制，并给出右端近似为1情形下的梯度估计作为应用。

Abstract: In current work, we delve into the study of complex Hessian equations on compact Kähler manifolds. By imposing a constraint on both the bisectional curvature and the oscillation of the solution, we demonstrate that the Laplacian estimate can be linearly controlled by its gradient estimate. Consequently, we are able to derive gradient estimate for the solution using a standard interpolation argument, without the need to employ the blow-up technique. As an application, we demonstrate that when the right-hand side of the complex Hessian equations approximate 1 in the $L^1$ norm, the desired gradient estimate is obtained.

</details>


### [135] [HKT geometry on locally conformally hyperkähler manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07897)
*Giovanni Gentili,Luigi Vezzoni*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究紧致局部共形超凯勒流形上的HKT几何：证明四元数Monge-Ampère方程可解，验证HKT-Ricci流最大存在时间猜想，并在四元数Hopf曲面上构造非平凡HKT孤立子及对称初值的归一化流收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Alesker与Dinew-Sroka关于四元数Monge-Ampère方程可解性的结果，并验证HKT-Ricci流最大存在时间的猜想，同时探究四元数Hopf曲面上HKT几何的孤立子结构与流的收敛行为。

Method: 在紧致局部共形超凯勒流形上分析四元数Monge-Ampere方程；研究HKT-Ricci流的最大存在时间；在四元数Hopf曲面上构造非平凡HKT孤立子，并对对称初值分析归一化流的收敛。

Result: 证明了紧致局部共形超凯勒流形上四元数Monge-Ampere方程总是可解的；确认了HKT-Ricci流最大存在时间的猜想；在四元数Hopf曲面上构造了非平凡HKT孤立子；证明了对称初值下归一化流的收敛性。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了局部共形超凯勒流形上HKT几何的理论，将四元数Monge-Ampere方程可解性结果推广至更一般情形，验证了HKT-Ricci流最大存在时间猜想，并在具体模型空间（四元数Hopf曲面）上给出孤立子与收敛性结论。

Abstract: We study the HKT geometry of compact locally conformally hyperkähler manifolds. We prove that on these manifolds the quaternionic Monge-Ampère equation is always solvable, extending results of Alesker and of Dinew-Sroka, and we confirm the conjecture on the maximal existence time of the HKT-Ricci flow. On quaternionic Hopf surfaces we construct non-trivial HKT solitons and prove convergence of the normalized flow for symmetric initial data.

</details>


### [136] [Besicovitch Covering on Alexandrov Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07912)
*Maxime Marot*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明有限维测地局部紧的曲率有下界Alexandrov空间（含完备截面曲率有下界黎曼流形）满足Besicovitch覆盖定理，方法是证明其按Federer意义是"方向有限"的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Besicovitch覆盖定理是欧氏空间及众多度量空间中的核心覆盖工具，广泛用于测度论、调和分析与几何测度论。然而对于曲率有下界（CBB）的Alexandrov空间这类仅具合成曲率下界的奇异度量空间，是否满足该定理此前并不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用几何测度论的路径：引入Federer的"方向有限"（directionally limited）概念，证明有限维测地局部紧CBB Alexandrov空间具有该性质，从而由Federer的经典结论导出Besicovitch覆盖定理。

Result: 所有有限维测地局部紧的CBB Alexandrov空间均满足Besicovitch覆盖定理；作为特例，完备且截面曲率有下界的黎曼流形亦满足该定理。

Conclusion: CBB Alexandrov空间落入满足Besicovitch覆盖定理的度量空间类别，从而可将经典覆盖论证推广到这类含奇点的空间，并统一涵盖低曲率下界的黎曼流形情形。

Abstract: We prove that finite-dimensional geodesic locally compact Alexandrov spaces with curvature bounded below (CBB), and in particular complete Riemannian manifolds with lower bounded sectional curvature, satisfy a Besicovitch covering theorem by showing that they are directionally limited in the sense of Federer.

</details>


### [137] [Noncompactness for the constant $Q_{2N}$-curvature problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07992)
*Liuwei Gong,Seunghyeok Kim,Juncheng Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文在球面上构造固定光滑度量，使常Q_{2N}-曲率方程存在L^∞无界的正解序列，从而在每一阶建立非紧性，并给出维度下界，预期最优。核心技术是推导闭爱因斯坦度量处TT方向上总Q_{2N}-曲率固定体积Hessian的显式公式，并将其化为Lichnerowicz Laplacian的N次多项式，通过有限矩阵的符号分析得到非定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常Q-曲率方程解集在高阶情形是否非紧此前仅对低阶（N=1,2,3）已知，需要在高阶N上建立非紧性并确定维度门槛，同时理解Hessian结构与阶数N、维数n的关系。

Method: 在S^n上构造非局部共形平坦的固定光滑度量；基于Juhl公式推导固定体积下总Q_{2N}-曲率在TT方向、闭爱因斯坦度量（Ric=(n-1)g）处的Hessian，识别为Lichnerowicz Laplacian的N次多项式；对由代数Weyl张量生成的度量扰动，将固定气泡中心的二次约化能量表示为该Hessian在四维TT空间上的正倍数；对气泡尺度求导得到有限矩阵，进行精确符号分析并延拓到实n。

Result: 对每个N≥4及相应维数范围（N=4,5时n≥2N+20；6≤N≤8时n≥2N+19；9≤N≤17时n≥2N+18；N≥18时n≥2N+17）构造度量，使常Q_{2N}-曲率方程有L^∞无界正解序列，且度量可在C^L范数下任意接近圆度量。结合N=1,2,3的结果，所有阶均非紧。矩阵符号分析给出阈值n_N=2N+a_*+c_*N^{-1}+O(N^{-2})，a_*≈16.201871，c_*≈13.512880，解释了n≥2N+17的界。

Conclusion: 常Q_{2N}-曲率方程在所有阶N上均非紧，维度下界随N变化并预期最优；非定性源于特定TT扰动下Hessian的符号变化，阈值渐近由n_N=2N+a_*+c_*N^{-1}+…刻画。

Abstract: For every integer $N\ge4$, we construct a fixed smooth, non-locally-conformally-flat metric on the $n$-dimensional unit sphere $\mathbb{S}^n$ for which the constant $Q_{2N}$-curvature equation admits an $L^\infty$-unbounded sequence of positive solutions. The construction applies for $n\ge2N+20$ when $N=4,5$, $n\ge2N+19$ when $6\le N\le8$, $n\ge2N+18$ when $9\le N\le17$, and $n\ge2N+17$ when $N\ge18$. For each $L \in \mathbb{N} \cup \{0\}$, the metric can be chosen arbitrarily close to the round metric in the $C^L$ norm. Together with the known cases $N=1,2,3$, this establishes noncompactness at every order, with dimension bounds that we expect to be optimal. We derive an explicit formula for the fixed-volume Hessian of total $Q_{2N}$-curvature in transverse-traceless (TT) directions at closed Einstein metrics satisfying $\operatorname{Ric}=(n-1)g$. Building on Juhl's formulas, we identify this Hessian, for every $N\in\mathbb{N}$ and $n>2N$, as a degree-$N$ polynomial in the Lichnerowicz Laplacian. For the metric perturbations generated by algebraic Weyl tensors in the construction, the quadratic reduced energy with fixed bubble center is a positive multiple of this Hessian restricted to a four-dimensional TT space. Differentiation with respect to the bubble scale then yields a finite matrix whose exact sign analysis establishes the required indefiniteness in the stated dimension ranges. After continuation to real $n$, this matrix changes from negative definite to indefinite at $n_N=2N+a_*+c_*N^{-1}+O(N^{-2})$ as $N\to\infty$, with $a_*\approx16.201871$ and $c_*\approx13.512880$, explaining the eventual bound $n\ge2N+17$.

</details>


### [138] [A solution to a problem of Ghomi and Raffaelli on closed asymptotic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08050)
*Weiran Ding*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 构造了欧氏三维空间中一个光滑嵌入开环带，具有严格负高斯曲率与单射高斯映射，并包含一条零法自环绕数的简单闭渐近曲线，正面回答了Ghomi–Raffaelli的问题1.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Ghomi与Raffaelli提出的问题1.1，即是否存在满足特定几何条件的负曲率曲面及其上的简单闭渐近曲线。

Method: 从两条简单平面曲线出发，要求其切向量互为正倍数；通过用含一个自由长度参数的多边形对进行圆角化处理，使二者的混合积分（mixed integral）归零，进而完成曲面的构造。

Result: 成功构造出所需的光滑嵌入开环带：高斯曲率严格为负、高斯映射单射，且其上存在一条具有零法自环绕数的简单闭渐近曲线。

Conclusion: 该构造为Ghomi–Raffaelli问题1.1提供了肯定性答案，并给出了一种基于平面曲线混合积分归零的显式构造思路。

Abstract: We construct a smooth embedded open annulus in Euclidean three-space with strictly negative Gaussian curvature and injective Gauss map that contains a simple closed asymptotic curve of zero normal self-linking. This gives an affirmative answer to Problem 1.1 of Ghomi and Raffaelli. The construction starts with two simple planar curves whose tangent vectors are positive multiples of one another. Their mixed integral is made zero by rounding a pair of polygons with one free length parameter.

</details>


### [139] [Positive Einstein metrics on infinitely many four-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08052)
*Tristan Ozuch*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在 $\#_k(\mathbb{S}^2\times\mathbb{S}^2)$ 与 $k\mathbb{CP}^2\#(2k+1)\overline{\mathbb{CP}}^2$（$k\geq 2$）上构造了正数量曲率的光滑 Einstein 度量，从而给出无穷多个闭 4-流形同胚型上的正 Einstein 度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正 Einstein 4-流形的存在性受拓扑与数量曲率约束，已知例子稀缺，尤其缺乏无穷多同胚型的构造；本文旨在系统构造此类度量并刻画其退化与渐近行为。

Method: 从 $\mathbb{S}^2\times\mathbb{S}^2$ 乘积度量与 $\mathbb{CP}^2\#3\overline{\mathbb{CP}}^2$ 的对称 Kähler-Einstein 度量出发，沿环面引入锥角 $2\pi\beta$ 的预设奇性并作变形；在 $\beta=\frac{2}{k+1}$ 时调整环绕环面的周期以消除锥奇性，同时改变拓扑。并通过 Wick 旋转得到 Lorentz 静态轴对称 Einstein 度量。

Result: 构造出两族光滑正数量曲率 Einstein 度量，覆盖无穷多同胚型；第一族表明任何带正数量曲率度量的单连通 spin 4-流形都同胚于某 Einstein 4-流形；Wick 旋转给出正宇宙学常数、任意多视界且表面重力相同的 Lorentz 静态轴对称解。当 $k\to+\infty$，度量沿环面轨道塌缩到边界 Hausdorff 维数为 $\frac{4}{3}$ 的二维圆盘，并在曲率尺度上收敛到具 Kasner 渐近的完备周期 Ricci 平坦度量：第一族为 Euclidean Myers-Korotkin-Nicolai 度量，第二族为新度量。

Conclusion: 该工作将锥奇性变形、拓扑变换与塌缩分析结合，统一构造了多族正 Einstein 4-度量，并揭示其极限几何与 Ricci 平坦 Kasner 渐近之间的深刻联系，拓展了正 Einstein 度量的存在性范围。

Abstract: We construct smooth Einstein metrics with positive scalar curvature on $\#_k({\mathbb S^2}\times {\mathbb S^2})$ and on $k\mathbb{CP}^2\#(2k+1)\overline{\mathbb{CP}}{}^{2}$, for $k\geqslant2$. In particular, we show that positive Einstein metrics exist on infinitely many homeomorphism types of closed $4$-manifolds.
  The first family shows that any simply connected spin $4$-manifold carrying metrics with positive scalar curvature is homeomorphic to an Einstein $4$-manifold. Wick rotation gives associated Lorentzian static axisymmetric Einstein metrics with positive cosmological constant and arbitrarily many horizons with the same surface gravity.
  The metrics are obtained by varying the cone angle $2πβ$ of a prescribed singularity along a torus, starting from the product metric on $\mathbb S^2\times\mathbb S^2$ and from the symmetric Kähler-Einstein metric on $\mathbb{CP}^2\#3\overline{\mathbb{CP}}{}^2$. At $β=\frac{2}{k+1}$, changing the period of the angle around the torus removes the cone singularity and changes the topology.
  For both families, as $k\to+\infty$, the metrics collapse along the torus orbits to an explicit $2$-dimensional disk, whose boundary has Hausdorff dimension $\frac{4}{3}$. At the scale of curvature, they converge to complete periodic Ricci-flat metrics with Kasner asymptotics: the Euclidean Myers-Korotkin-Nicolai metric for the first family, and a new one for the second.

</details>


### [140] [Biconservative and Biharmonic Hypersurfaces in Product Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08104)
*V. Branding,S. Montaldo,C. Oniciuc,A. Ratto*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究乘积空间 $L^m(\varepsilon)\times\mathbb{R}$ 中的双守恒与双调和超曲面，给出等参及常角超曲面的完全分类，并在平均曲率梯度为主方向的情形下得到分类结果与新例子，最后证明 ${\mathbb H}^m\times{\mathbb R}$ 中一类真双调和超曲面的整体不存在定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双守恒（biconservative）与双调和（biharmonic）超曲面是子流形几何中的重要研究对象，乘积空间 $L^m(\varepsilon)\times\mathbb{R}$ 提供了兼具曲率与乘积结构的自然背景。已有结果多集中于常曲率空间或特定乘积空间，缺少对等参超曲面、常角超曲面及平均曲率梯度为主方向等一般情形的系统刻画，也缺乏 ${\mathbb H}^m\times{\mathbb R}$ 中整体性结果。

Method: 在乘积空间 $L^m(\varepsilon)\times\mathbb{R}$（$L^m(\varepsilon)$ 为常截面曲率 $\varepsilon=\pm1$ 的空间形式）中研究超曲面。对等参超曲面与常角超曲面给出完全描述；对平均曲率函数的梯度为主方向的超曲面，通过结构方程与分类论证得到分类结果；构造新的完备、非柱面、非全脐双守恒超曲面例子并证明相应刚性定理；最后结合曲率有下界与平均曲率平方有界的条件证明整体不存在定理。

Result: 得到等参超曲面与常角超曲面情形的完全描述；在平均曲率梯度为主方向时获得若干分类结果，并构造出 ${\mathbb H}^m\times{\mathbb R}$ 中新的完备、非柱面、非全脐双守恒超曲面例子，同时给出与之相关的刚性定理；证明 ${\mathbb H}^m\times{\mathbb R}$ 中满足平均曲率平方有界且 Ricci 曲率有下界的完备真双调和超曲面的整体不存在定理。

Conclusion: 论文系统刻画了乘积空间 $L^m(\varepsilon)\times\mathbb{R}$ 中双守恒与双调和超曲面的结构，揭示了等参、常角及平均曲率梯度为主方向等条件下的分类规律，并给出 ${\mathbb H}^m\times{\mathbb R}$ 中新型双守恒例子、刚性结果及真双调和超曲面的整体不存在性。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate biconservative and biharmonic hypersurfaces in product spaces $L^m(\varepsilon)\times {\mathbb R}$, where $L^m(\varepsilon)$ denotes the space form ${\mathbb S}^m$ or ${\mathbb H}^m$ of constant sectional curvature $\varepsilon=1$ or $\varepsilon=-1$ respectively. We obtain their complete description in the case of isoparametric hypersurfaces and hypersurfaces of constant angle. Moreover, we prove some classification results for biconservative and biharmonic hypersurfaces for which the gradient of the mean curvature function is a principal direction. In this context we obtain new examples of complete, non-cylindrical, non-totally umbilical biconservative hypersurfaces in ${\mathbb H}^m\times {\mathbb R}$. Then we prove a rigidity theorem related to these examples. Finally, we prove a global non-existence theorem for complete proper biharmonic hypersurfaces in ${\mathbb H}^m \times {\mathbb R}$ such that the square of the mean curvature function is bounded and the Ricci curvature is bounded from below.

</details>


### [141] [Positivity of the canonical bundle and Hermitian metrics with quasi-negative holomorphic sectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08111)
*Xueyuan Wan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了紧Kähler流形上若存在关于Chern联络具拟负全纯截曲率的Hermitian度量（无需为Kähler度量），则其典范丛丰沛，从而在Kähler情形下证明了Yang–Zheng拟负猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yang与Zheng提出的拟负猜想关注拟负全纯截曲率（即曲率非正且在某点严格负）对流形典范丛正性的影响；此前相关结论多限于Kähler度量设定，而此处允许实现曲率的Hermitian度量非Kähler，需处理更一般的复几何情形。

Method: 在紧Kähler流形上考虑关于Chern联络的Hermitian度量，利用其拟负全纯截曲率条件，结合复几何中典范丛正性（丰沛性）的判定工具进行证明，不要求该Hermitian度量为Kähler。

Result: 若紧Kähler流形具有关于Chern联络拟负全纯截曲率的Hermitian度量，则该流形的典范丛是丰沛的。

Conclusion: 本文在Kähler情形下证明了Yang–Zheng的拟负猜想，并表明实现该曲率条件的度量可以不是Kähler度量。

Abstract: We prove that if a compact Kähler manifold admits a Hermitian metric with quasi-negative holomorphic sectional curvature for the Chern connection, then its canonical bundle is ample. The Hermitian metric realizing this curvature need not be Kähler. This proves the Kähler case of the quasi-negative conjecture of Yang and Zheng.

</details>


### [142] [Complete non-orientable minimal surfaces of finite total curvature: Approximation and interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08163)
*Antonio Alarcon,Francisco J. Lopez*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了 $\mathbb{R}^n$ ($n\ge 3$) 中有限总曲率完备不可定向极小曲面的带插值一致逼近定理，并由此构造出具有任意给定有限拓扑抛物型开共形不可定向曲面复结构的这类极小曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逼近与插值理论多针对可定向极小曲面（如经典的 Runge 型/Weierstrass 表示逼近结果），不可定向情形因缺少整体可定向复结构而难以直接套用。本文旨在填补这一空白，将一致逼近与插值结果推广到高维欧氏空间中有限总曲率的完备不可定向极小曲面。

Method: 利用有限总曲率极小曲面的 Weierstrass 型表示与共形结构理论，借助不可定向曲面到其可定向双覆盖的性质、以及抛物型有限拓扑开曲面上的逼近（Runge 型）与插值技巧，构造在给定紧集上一致逼近并满足插值条件的完备不可定向极小浸入。核心思路可概括为：将不可定向问题化归至双覆盖上的可定向逼近问题，再通过取商实现不可定向性，同时控制总曲率有限性与完备性。

Result: 在 $\mathbb{R}^n$ ($n\ge 3$) 中建立了有限总曲率完备不可定向极小曲面的一致逼近与插值定理；作为推论，证明了对任意有限拓扑、抛物型的开共形不可定向曲面，均存在以该曲面为复结构的此类完备极小曲面。

Conclusion: 该工作将极小曲面的逼近/插值理论从可定向情形系统推广至不可定向情形，并给出复结构实现的存在性结果，表明有限总曲率完备不可定向极小曲面在共形结构选取上具有高度灵活性，为后续研究不可定向极小曲面的构造与分类提供了工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove a uniform approximation theorem with interpolation for complete non-orientable minimal surfaces of finite total curvature in the Euclidean space $\mathbb{R}^n$ $(n\ge 3)$. In particular, we establish the existence of such surfaces with the complex structure of any open conformal non-orientable surface of finite topology and parabolic type.

</details>


### [143] [Increase in topological complexity along the mean curvature flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08357)
*Natasha Diederen*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了 $\mathbb{S}^{p+q-1}$ 在 $\mathbb{R}^{p+q}$ 中的光滑嵌入，其在平均曲率流下于一点形成孤立奇点，随后光滑地演化为 $\mathbb{S}^{p-1}\times\mathbb{S}^q$；并给出 $SO(p)\times SO(q)$-不变平均曲率流的一般性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究平均曲率流中"奇点后继续演化"的具体例子稀缺：尽管已知平均曲率流可越过奇点（如柱面收缩为颈状奇点后继续），但严格构造出经历孤立奇点后拓扑发生改变的光滑嵌入例子，并系统刻画 $SO(p)\times SO(q)$-对称情形，具有基础性意义。

Method: 考虑由图形轮廓曲线旋转生成的 $SO(p)\times SO(q)$-不变初始数据，建立该类平均曲率流的一般理论；分析"气泡片"（bubblesheet）奇点；核心技术是"瞬时光滑性"（instant smoothness）结果，其中结合精细的逼近论证与伪局部性（pseudolocality）估计。

Result: 构造出 $\mathbb{S}^{p+q-1}\subset\mathbb{R}^{p+q}$ 的一个光滑嵌入，其在平均曲率流下演化时于一点形成孤立奇点，奇点之后该流成为 $\mathbb{S}^{p-1}\times\mathbb{S}^q$ 的光滑嵌入；同时获得 $SO(p)\times SO(q)$-不变平均曲率流的若干一般性结论及气泡片奇点的分析。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了平均曲率流穿越孤立奇点并实现拓扑从 $\mathbb{S}^{p+q-1}$ 到 $\mathbb{S}^{p-1}\times\mathbb{S}^q$ 变化的显式范例，说明平均曲率流在奇点后可具有良好光滑性；其"瞬时光滑性"方法与对称性分析可推广至更一般的等变平均曲率流问题。
