Abstract: We construct an example of a smooth embedding of $\mathbb{S}^{p+q-1}$ in $\mathbb{R}^{p+q}$ whose evolution under the mean curvature flow forms an isolated singularity at one point, after which it is a smoothly embedded copy of $\mathbb{S}^{p-1}\times \mathbb{S}^q$. In doing so, we prove some general results about $SO(p)\times SO(q)$-invariant mean curvature flow whose initial data is generated by the rotation of a graphical profile curve, including an analysis of bubblesheet singularities. The most involved part of our analysis is an instant smoothness result, which requires a delicate approximation and pseudolocality.

</details>


### [144] [Coordinate isoperimetric mass and a positive mass theorem for distributional scalar curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08615)
*Melanie Graf,Eduardo Hafemann*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文将 Huisken 提出的坐标等周质量推广到连续渐近平坦度量，建立新的渐近表达式，并证明其有限性判据、坐标不变性以及与 Lee–LeFloch 广义 ADM 质量的等价性，进而对 $W^{1,p}_{-\tau}$ 渐近平坦度量（$p>n$，$\tau>(n-2)/2$，非负分布标量曲率）给出正质量定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典 Riemannian 正质量定理推广到低正则度量是自 2000 年代初以来的持续工作，近期重新受到关注。已有方法多假设无穷远处光滑或引入新的质量定义，本文希望避免这两点，回到 Huisken 最初提出的坐标等周质量。

Method: 不假设无穷远光滑，也不引入新质量定义，而是采用 Huisken 的坐标等周质量；对连续渐近平坦度量建立新的渐近表达式，用于证明有限性判据、坐标不变性及与 Lee–LeFloch 广义 ADM 质量的等价性。证明依赖一个密度定理（类似 Lee–Lesourd–Unger 的版本，但正则性与衰减假设更弱）。

Result: 对 $W^{1,p}_{-\tau}$ 渐近平坦度量（$p>n$，$\tau>(n-2)/2$，非负分布标量曲率），坐标等周质量确实坐标不变；并在除渐近平坦外无任何拓扑假设的条件下建立了该正则性下的正质量定理。所依赖的密度定理在更弱正则性与衰减假设下成立。

Conclusion: 通过坐标等周质量的新渐近表达式，可在低正则渐近平坦设定下证明正质量定理，且无需额外拓扑假设；所用密度定理本身可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: Extending the well-known Riemannian positive mass theorem to low-regularity metrics is an ongoing effort, starting from the early 2000s, that has recently seen renewed interest. Contrary to many other approaches we do not wish to assume smoothness at infinity nor to introduce a new definition of mass tailored to our approach, but rather go back to the coordinate isoperimetric mass originally proposed by Huisken. We establish new asymptotic expressions for the coordinate isoperimetric mass of continuous asymptotically flat metrics, which constitute the main ingredient in proving a finiteness criterion, coordinate invariance and equality to the generalized ADM mass of Lee and LeFloch under reasonable assumptions. In particular, for $W^{1,p}_{-τ}$ asymptotically flat metrics, with $p>n$, $τ>(n-2)/2$, and nonnegative distributional scalar curvature, the coordinate isoperimetric mass is indeed coordinate invariant, and we establish a positive mass theorem in this regularity without any topological assumptions aside from asymptotic flatness. Our proof relies on a density theorem similar to that of Lee, Lesourd, and Unger, but under a weaker regularity and decay assumption, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [145] [On the Bach tensor and quadratic curvature functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08628)
*Letizia Branca,Davide Dameno*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究四维流形上依赖Bach张量梯度的二次泛函的临界点，证明弱Bach-平行度量的存在性与极小化子存在唯一性，并对全局极小值进行分类及构造新例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该泛函的临界点推广了Bach-平坦条件，旨在深化对Bach张量相关变分问题及其与共形几何、已知曲率泛函联系的理解，并给出度量的分类与构造。

Method: 通过研究二次泛函关于共形变分的临界点（弱Bach-平行度量），利用共形梯度孤子理论、de Rham分裂定理（完全四维流形版本）以及对四维柱面与三维曲率泛函的等价性分析，进行极小化子与全局极小值的刻画和分类。

Result: 证明任意闭四维流形上存在弱Bach-平行度量，且有无穷多共形类包含该泛函的唯一极小化子（相差常数正缩放）；对曲面乘积给出分类结果；构造出既非局部共形平坦也非共形Einstein的Bach-平坦度量新显例；刻画完全曲面上的HCMU度量；证明4D柱面上Bach-平行条件与三维某二次曲率泛函临界度量存在性的等价，并在曲率与有限能量假设下刻画平坦三维流形。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Bach张量梯度泛函的变分框架，统一了若干共形与曲率泛函问题，提供了存在性、唯一性、分类及显式构造，并揭示四维Bach-平行与三维曲率泛函之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We study the critical points of a quadratic functional depending on the gradient of the Bach tensor on Riemannian four-manifolds, which generalize the Bach-flat condition. We show that, on every closed four-manifold, there exists a weak Bach-parallel metric, i.e. a critical point for this functional with respect to conformal variations: in particular, we prove that there exist infinitely many conformal classes which contain a unique minimizer for the functional, up to constant positive rescaling. Next, we analyze the global minima of the functional, i.e. metrics with parallel Bach tensor, relating these metrics to well-known variational problems. Using a version of de Rham's splitting theorem on complete four-manifolds, we provide a classification result for products of surfaces, exploiting the theory of conformal gradient solitons; we also construct a new explicit example of a Bach-flat metric which is neither locally conformally flat nor conformally Einstein and we characterize HCMU metrics on complete surfaces. Finally, we prove an equivalence between the Bach-parallel condition on 4D cylinders and the existence of critical metrics for a well-known quadratic curvature functional in dimension three: in this direction, we also prove a characterization of flat three-manifolds, under some curvature and finite energy assumptions.

</details>


### [146] [Topology of tame contractible $n$-manifolds, $n=6,7$, and BiRicci Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08686)
*Paul Sweeney*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明满足特定拓扑条件下的6、7维流形内域若具有正BiRicci曲率及二次衰减的完备度量，则微分同胚于欧氏空间，并构造了基于Higman群的Newman流形反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正曲率（正BiRicci曲率）与流形拓扑结构之间的关系，探讨在何种衰减条件下可推出流形为欧氏空间，同时寻找拓扑条件满足但度量性质不同的反例以界定结果的尖锐性。

Method: 利用几何分析中的曲率比较、度量衰减估计与拓扑手术等方法，结合相对同伦群条件π_i(X,∂X)=0，分析完备正BiRicci曲率度量的几何约束；并通过Higman群的表示构造紧致可缩Newman流形。

Result: 当n=6,7时，若内域支持具有C-二次衰减（C>n²/4）的完备正BiRicci曲率度量且满足π_i(X,∂X)=0（3≤i≤n-3），则M微分同胚于R^n；此外构造出n≥6的紧致可缩Newman流形N^n，满足同伦群条件且其内部具一致正标量曲率度量。

Conclusion: 在适当的曲率衰减与拓扑条件下，高维流形内域可被完全刻画为欧氏空间；同时构造的Newman流形表明仅凭标量曲率正性不足以强制欧氏拓扑，凸显BiRicci曲率条件的重要性。

Abstract: We show that if $M^n$, $n=6,7$, is the interior of a compact, contractible $n$-manifold with boundary $X$, such that $π_i(X,\partial X)=0$, $3\leq i \leq n-3$, and supports complete metrics with positive BiRicci curvature with $C$-quadratic decay at infinity for some $C>\frac{n^2}{4}$, then $M$ is diffeomorphic to $\mathbb{R}^n$. Furthermore, we construct a compact, contractible Newman $n$-manifold $N^n$, $n\geq6$, based on a presentation of the Higman group such that $π_i(N,\partial N)=0$, $3\leq i \leq n-3$, and $\mathrm{int}(N)$ admits a metric with uniform positive scalar curvature.

</details>


### [147] [Compactness and Noncompactness for the Positive Yamabe--Escobar Problem with Minimal Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08759)
*Santiago Cordero-Misteli*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究Yamabe--Escobar方程（正数量曲率、极小边界）正解集的紧性猜想：证明了在维数3≤n≤14时该猜想成立，且阈值n*=14是尖锐的；对局部共形平坦与脐点边界两类特殊情形分别给出n*=18与n*=20。同时证明了相应维数范围内Weyl--脐性消没猜想，并通过扰动非紧性例子构造高维反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Yamabe--Escobar方程（指定正数量曲率与极小边界）正解集的紧性问题：紧性猜想断言唯一的障碍是共形等价于圆半球，但该猜想的成立维数范围及其尖锐阈值此前未知。

Method: 通过分析解序列的blow-up（爆破）行为来判定紧性；对紧性断言给出维数区间内的证明，并通过在半球上构造非脐点、非局部共形平坦的度量反例来证明阈值的尖锐性；对局部共形平坦度量与脐点边界度量两个特殊类分别进行平行分析；此外通过扰动非紧性例子构造Weyl--脐性消没猜想的反例。

Result: 紧性猜想在3≤n≤14成立，且阈值n*=14为尖锐：在n≥15存在非脐点且非局部共形平坦的半球度量反例。特殊情形下，局部共形平坦度量的阈值n*^{LCF}=18，脐点边界度量的阈值n*^{umb}=20。在三种情形下均证明Weyl--脐性消没猜想在4≤n≤n*成立，并在n>n*通过扰动非紧性例子构造反例。

Conclusion: 所得结果表明紧性障碍与维数阈值密切相关，且三个阈值（14、18、20）各自尖锐；其中局部共形平坦的非紧性构造是首次在圆半球共形类之外、针对正数量曲率-极小边界Yamabe--Escobar方程给出的。

Abstract: We study the Yamabe--Escobar equation prescribing positive constant scalar curvature and minimal boundary. The compactness conjecture asks whether being conformally equivalent to the round hemisphere is the only obstruction for its set of positive solutions to be compact. In this paper we show that this is true for dimensions $3\leq n\leq 14$. We show that this dimensional threshold $n_*=14$ is sharp by constructing in $n\geq 15$ nonumbilic and non-locally-conformally-flat examples of metrics in the hemisphere for which a noncompact sequence of solutions exists. We do the same for the special classes of locally conformally flat metrics, $n_*^{LCF}=18$, and the metrics with umbilic boundary, $n_*^{umb} =20$. In all three settings we also prove that the Weyl--umbilicity vanishing conjecture holds for $4\leq n\leq n_*$ and construct counterexamples in $n>n_*$ by perturbing the noncompactness examples. To the best of the author's knowledge, these are the first locally conformally flat noncompactness constructions for the positive-scalar-curvature, minimal-boundary case of the Yamabe--Escobar equation, outside the conformal class of the round hemisphere.

</details>
